```scss
        grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);
    }

    @include abs.screen(sm) {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }
}

.clock-zodiac-card {
    position: relative;
    overflow: hidden;
    @extend %flexColumn;
    justify-content: space-between;
    min-height: 13.2rem;
    padding: 1.8rem;
    background-color: var(--bg-surface);
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 2rem;
    text-align: left;
    cursor: pointer;
    @extend %transInteractive;

    &:hover,
    &:focus-visible {
        border-color: var(--primary-color);
        transform: translateY(-3px);
        box-shadow: 0 0.8rem 2rem var(--shadow-color);

        .clock-zodiac-card__img {
            opacity: 0.45;
            transform: scale(1.08) rotate(0deg);
        }
    }

    &.is-current {
        background-color: var(--text-color);
        border-color: var(--text-color);
        color: var(--body-bg);
        box-shadow: 0 1.2rem 2.8rem var(--shadow-color);

        .clock-zodiac-card__name,
        .clock-zodiac-card__canchi,
        .clock-zodiac-card__fortune {
            color: var(--body-bg);
        }

        .clock-zodiac-card__utc {
            color: var(--body-bg);
            opacity: 0.78;
        }

        .clock-zodiac-card__img {
            opacity: 0.28;
            filter: brightness(0) invert(1);
        }
    }

    &.is-selected:not(.is-current) {
        border: 2px solid var(--primary-color);
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
    }

    &.is-dimmed {
        opacity: 0.22;
        transform: scale(0.97);
    }

    &__top,
    &__bottom {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        @extend %flexBetween;
        width: 100%;
    }

    &__bottom {
        align-items: flex-end;
        margin-top: auto;
    }

    &__name {
        font-size: 1.5rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--text-color);
    }

    &__utc {
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        font-weight: 600;
        color: var(--text-muted);
        font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
    }

    &__canchi {
        font-size: 2.6rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        line-height: 1;
        color: var(--text-color);
        font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
    }

    &__fortune {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        gap: 0.4rem;
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--text-muted);
    }

    &__img {
        position: absolute;
        right: 0.8rem;
        bottom: 0.6rem;
        width: 5.4rem;
        height: 5.4rem;
        object-fit: contain;
        opacity: 0.16;
        filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
        transform: scale(0.88) rotate(-8deg);
        pointer-events: none;
        z-index: 1;
        transition:
            transform 0.35s ease,
            opacity 0.35s ease;
    }

    &__mid {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 0.35rem;
        margin: 0.4rem 0;
    }

    &__element-tag {
        display: inline-block;
        width: fit-content;
        padding: 0.2rem 0.8rem;
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        font-size: 1.15rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--primary-color);
    }

    &__lucdieu {
        font-size: 1.2rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-color);
    }

    &.is-current {
        .clock-zodiac-card__element-tag {
            background-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.16);
            border-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.28);
            color: var(--body-bg);
        }

        .clock-zodiac-card__lucdieu {
            color: var(--body-bg);
        }
    }
}
```

### week-calendar.scss

```scss
// scss/components/_week-calendar.scss
@use "../abstracts" as abs;

.week-cal {
    @extend %flexColumn;
    gap: 2rem;
    // Yêu cầu 4: Tăng padding-bottom (thêm 2.4rem / 24px) cho container chính để Legend không bị sát mép
    padding: 2.6rem 2.8rem 3.2rem;
    background-color: var(--body-bg);
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 2.8rem;
    box-shadow: 0 0.8rem 2.4rem var(--shadow-color);
    // Yêu cầu 1: Đặt line-height: 1.5 (số thập phân) cho toàn bộ văn bản thường trong layout lịch tuần
    line-height: 1.5;
    @extend %transTheme;

    @include abs.screen(md) {
        padding: 1.8rem 1.8rem 2.4rem;
        border-radius: 2rem;
        gap: 1.6rem;
    }

    // ==========================================================================
    // 1. HEADER & THANH ĐIỀU HƯỚNG TUẦN (Yêu cầu 4: Giảm padding/margin quanh thanh điều hướng)
    // ==========================================================================
    &__header {
        @extend %flexBetween;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        align-items: flex-end;
        gap: 1.2rem;
        padding-bottom: 0.4rem;
    }

    &__intro {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 0.4rem;
    }

    &__title-row {
        @extend %flexWrapGap;
    }

    &__week-badge,
    &__lunar-badge {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        padding: 0.35rem 1.1rem;
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        line-height: 1.5;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        color: var(--primary-color);
    }

    &__lunar-badge {
        color: var(--text-muted);
    }

    &__heading {
        font-size: clamp(2.2rem, 2.8vw, 3rem);
        font-weight: 800;
        line-height: 1.25;
        color: var(--text-color);
        letter-spacing: -0.02em;
    }

    // Thanh điều hướng gọn gàng, giảm padding xung quanh
    &__toolbar {
        @extend %flexItemCenter;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        gap: 0.8rem;
        padding: 0.6rem 0.8rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 99.9rem;

        @include abs.screen(md) {
            width: 100%;
            justify-content: space-between;
            border-radius: 1.4rem;
        }
    }

    &__nav-btn {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        gap: 0.6rem;
        height: 3.6rem;
        padding: 0 1.4rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        font-size: 1.3rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        line-height: 1.5;
        color: var(--text-color);
        @extend %transInteractive;

        &:hover {
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: var(--primary-color);
            transform: translateY(-1px);
        }
    }

    &__toolbar-center {
        @extend %flexItemCenter;
        gap: 0.8rem;
        padding: 0 0.4rem;
    }

    &__range-label {
        font-size: 1.4rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        line-height: 1.5;
        color: var(--text-color);
        font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            display: none;
        }
    }

    &__today-btn {
        height: 3.4rem;
        padding: 0 1.4rem;
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        background-color: var(--primary-color);
        color: #ffffff;
        font-size: 1.3rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        line-height: 1.5;

        &:hover {
            background-color: var(--primary-hover);
        }
    }

    // ==========================================================================
    // 2. KHỐI BENTO CHÍNH: CỘT TRÁI (THẺ DETAIL FLAT UI) + CỘT PHẢI (LƯỚI 7 NGÀY)
    // ==========================================================================
    &__body {
        display: grid;
        grid-template-columns: 38rem 1fr;
        align-items: stretch;
        gap: 2rem;

        @include abs.screen(xl) {
            grid-template-columns: 34rem 1fr;
        }

        @include abs.screen(lg) {
            grid-template-columns: 1fr;
        }
    }

    // YÊU CẦU 5: Cải thiện thẻ Detail (Thẻ to bên trái) theo Flat UI + Flexbox space-between
    &__hero {
        position: relative;
        overflow: hidden;
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        justify-content: space-between;
        gap: 1.6rem;
        padding: 2.6rem;
        background-color: var(--primary-color);
        border: 1px solid var(--primary-hover);
        border-radius: 2.4rem;
        color: #ffffff;
        line-height: 1.5;
        box-shadow: 0 1rem 2.8rem var(--shadow-color);
        @extend %transTheme;

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            padding: 2rem;
            border-radius: 2rem;
        }

        &-top {
            position: relative;
            z-index: 2;
            @extend %flexColumn;
            gap: 0.8rem;
            padding-bottom: 1.6rem;
            border-bottom: 1px solid var(--hero-border-color);
        }

        &-badges {
            @extend %flexBetween;
            flex-wrap: wrap;
            gap: 0.8rem;
        }

        &-pill,
        &-fortune {
            @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
            gap: 0.6rem;
            padding: 0.35rem 1.2rem;
            border-radius: 99.9rem;
            font-size: 1.25rem;
            font-weight: 800;
            line-height: 1.5;
        }

        &-pill {
            background-color: #ffffff;
            color: var(--primary-active);
        }

        &-fortune {
            background-color: var(--hero-box-bg);
            border: 1px solid var(--hero-border-color);
            color: #ffffff;
        }

        &-date {
            @extend %flexItemCenter;
            align-items: baseline;
            flex-wrap: wrap;
            gap: 1rem;
            margin-top: 0.2rem;
        }

        &-solar {
            font-size: clamp(5rem, 5.8vw, 6.4rem);
            font-weight: 800;
            line-height: 1;
            letter-spacing: -0.03em;
            font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
        }

        &-weekday {
            font-size: 2.2rem;
            font-weight: 700;
            line-height: 1.3;
            color: var(--hero-text-soft);
        }

        &-lunar {
            font-size: 1.4rem;
            line-height: 1.5;
            color: var(--hero-text-soft);
        }

        // 2 Tag ngang Tiết khí & Trực đổi màu theo Theme
        &-meta-pills {
            @extend %flexWrapGap;
            gap: 0.8rem;
            margin-top: 0.4rem;
        }

        &-meta-badge {
            @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
            gap: 0.6rem;
            padding: 0.35rem 1.1rem;
            background-color: var(--hero-box-bg);
            border: 1px solid var(--hero-border-color);
            border-radius: 99.9rem;
            font-size: 1.25rem;
            font-weight: 600;
            line-height: 1.5;
            color: #ffffff;

            strong {
                font-weight: 800;
            }
        }

        &-hours {
            position: relative;
            z-index: 2;
        }

        &-label {
            display: block;
            margin-bottom: 0.6rem;
            font-size: 1.25rem;
            font-weight: 800;
            text-transform: uppercase;
            letter-spacing: 0.04em;
            line-height: 1.5;
            color: var(--hero-text-soft);
        }

        &-hours-val {
            font-size: 1.5rem;
            font-weight: 700;
            line-height: 1.5;
        }

        // Yêu cầu 5: Tái sử dụng cụm .fengshui-actions của index.html nhưng theo Flat UI (Xóa nền)
        &-actions.fengshui-actions {
            position: relative;
            z-index: 2;
            grid-template-columns: 1fr;
            gap: 1.2rem;
            padding: 0;
            border: none;
        }

        &-footer {
            position: relative;
            z-index: 2;
            @extend %flexBetween;
            padding-top: 0.4rem;
        }

        // Nút Subtle Button lấy màu --hero-chip-bg từ _palette.scss
        &-link {
            @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
            gap: 0.8rem;
            height: 4rem;
            padding: 0 1.8rem;
            background-color: var(--hero-chip-bg);
            color: #ffffff;
            border: none;
            border-radius: 99.9rem;
            font-size: 1.35rem;
            font-weight: 700;
            line-height: 1.5;
            @extend %transInteractive;

            &:hover,
            &:focus-visible {
                background-color: var(--primary-active);
                color: #ffffff;
                transform: translateY(-2px);
            }
        }

        &-zodiac {
            position: absolute;
            right: -0.8rem;
            bottom: -0.8rem;
            width: 14rem;
            height: 14rem;
            object-fit: contain;
            opacity: 0.14;
            filter: var(--zodiac-filter-active);
            pointer-events: none;
            z-index: 1;
        }
    }

    // Tuỳ biến Flat UI cho 2 khối Việc nên làm / Việc nên tránh bên trong thẻ Hero
    &__flat-action.fengshui-actions__box {
        gap: 0.8rem;
        padding: 1.4rem;
        background-color: var(--hero-box-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--hero-border-color);
        border-radius: 1.6rem;
        @extend %transTheme;

        .fengshui-actions__header {
            justify-content: flex-start;
            gap: 0.8rem;
            margin-bottom: 0.2rem;
        }

        .fengshui-actions__badge {
            font-size: 1.25rem;
            font-weight: 800;
            line-height: 1.5;
            color: #ffffff;
        }

        .fengshui-actions__icon {
            width: 2.2rem;
            height: 2.2rem;
            font-size: 1.15rem;
            background-color: var(--hero-chip-bg);
            border: 1px solid var(--hero-border-color);
            color: #ffffff;
        }

        .fengshui-actions__list {
            gap: 0.6rem;
        }

        .fengshui-actions__chip {
            padding: 0.4rem 1.1rem;
            font-size: 1.25rem;
            line-height: 1.5;
            background-color: var(--hero-chip-bg);
            border: 1px solid var(--hero-border-color);
            color: #ffffff;
            box-shadow: none;
        }
    }

    // ==========================================================================
    // 3. CỘT PHẢI: LƯỚI 2 CỘT 7 NGÀY & THẺ CHỦ NHẬT .day-card--full-width
    // ==========================================================================
    &__grid {
        @extend %gridCols2;
        gap: 1.4rem;

        @include abs.screen(md) {
            grid-template-columns: 1fr;
        }
    }

    &__card {
        position: relative;
        overflow: hidden;
        @extend %flexColumn;
        justify-content: space-between;
        gap: 1rem;
        padding: 1.8rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 2rem;
        text-align: left;
        line-height: 1.5;
        cursor: pointer;
        @extend %transInteractive;

        &:hover,
        &:focus-visible {
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            background-color: var(--body-bg);
            transform: translateY(-2px);
            box-shadow: 0 0.8rem 2rem var(--shadow-color);

            .week-cal__card-zodiac {
                opacity: 0.4;
                transform: scale(1.08) rotate(0deg);
            }
        }

        // YÊU CẦU 3: Trạng thái Active có nền màu chính của theme làm mờ 8-10% (--primary-soft-bg) + viền màu theme
        &--active,
        &[aria-current="date"] {
            background-color: var(--primary-soft-bg, color-mix(in srgb, var(--primary-color) 9%, var(--body-bg)));
            border: 2px solid var(--primary-color);
            box-shadow: 0 0.8rem 2.2rem var(--shadow-color);

            .week-cal__card-day {
                color: var(--primary-color);
            }

            &:hover,
            &:focus-visible {
                background-color: var(--primary-soft-bg, color-mix(in srgb, var(--primary-color) 12%, var(--body-bg)));
            }
        }

        // YÊU CẦU 2: Modifier .day-card--full-width cho thẻ Chủ Nhật (Chia 2 cột: Trái Ngày/Can Chi/Giờ tốt - Phải Nên làm/Nên tránh)
        &.day-card--full-width {
            grid-column: span 2;
            display: grid;
            grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
            align-items: start;
            gap: 2.4rem;

            @include abs.screen(md) {
                grid-column: span 1;
                grid-template-columns: 1fr;
                gap: 1.2rem;
            }

            .day-card__col-left {
                position: relative;
                z-index: 2;
                @extend %flexColumn;
                justify-content: space-between;
                gap: 1.2rem;
                height: 100%;
                padding-right: 2rem;
                border-right: 1px dashed var(--border-color);

                @include abs.screen(md) {
                    padding-right: 0;
                    border-right: none;
                }
            }

            .day-card__col-right {
                position: relative;
                z-index: 2;
                @extend %flexColumn;
                justify-content: center;
                height: 100%;

                .week-cal__card-meta {
                    padding-top: 0;
                    border-top: none;
                    gap: 1rem;
                }
            }
        }

        &.is-highlighted {
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            background-color: var(--primary-soft-bg, var(--body-bg));
            transform: translateY(-2px);
        }

        &.is-dimmed {
            opacity: 0.22;
            transform: scale(0.98);
        }

        &-head {
            position: relative;
            z-index: 2;
            @extend %flexBetween;
            align-items: flex-start;
            gap: 0.8rem;
            width: 100%;
        }

        &-title-group {
            @extend %flexColumn;
            gap: 0.2rem;
        }

        &-day {
            font-size: 1.7rem;
            font-weight: 800;
            line-height: 1.35;
            color: var(--text-color);

            &--sun {
                color: var(--color-danger);
            }
        }

        &-sub {
            font-size: 1.3rem;
            font-weight: 600;
            line-height: 1.5;
            color: var(--text-muted);
        }

        &-section {
            position: relative;
            z-index: 2;
            @extend %flexColumn;
        }

        // Yêu cầu 1: Tiêu đề nhỏ ('Giờ tốt', 'Nên làm', 'Nên tránh') có margin-bottom 0.5rem (5px) tách biệt nội dung
        &-label,
        &-meta-title {
            display: block;
            margin-bottom: 0.5rem;
            font-size: 1.25rem;
            font-weight: 700;
            line-height: 1.5;
            color: var(--text-color);
        }

        &-hours {
            font-size: 1.3rem;
            line-height: 1.5;
            color: var(--text-muted);
            @extend %textClamp2;
        }

        &-meta {
            position: relative;
            z-index: 2;
            @extend %flexColumn;
            gap: 0.8rem;
            padding-top: 1rem;
            border-top: 1px dashed var(--border-color);
            width: 100%;
        }

        &-meta-group {
            @extend %flexColumn;
        }

        &-do,
        &-dont {
            font-size: 1.3rem;
            font-weight: 600;
            line-height: 1.5;
        }

        &-do {
            color: var(--color-success);
        }

        &-dont {
            color: var(--color-danger);
        }

        &-zodiac {
            position: absolute;
            right: 0.8rem;
            bottom: 0.8rem;
            width: 4.8rem;
            height: 4.8rem;
            object-fit: contain;
            opacity: 0.14;
            filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
            transform: scale(0.88) rotate(-8deg);
            pointer-events: none;
            z-index: 1;
            transition:
                transform 0.35s ease,
                opacity 0.35s ease;
        }
    }

    // ==========================================================================
    // 4. ĐỒNG BỘ BADGE TRÊN THẺ VÀ CHÚ THÍCH LEGEND CUỐI TRANG (Yêu cầu 6)
    // ==========================================================================
    &__badge,
    &__legend-badge {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        gap: 0.6rem;
        padding: 0.35rem 1.1rem;
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        font-size: 1.2rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        line-height: 1.5;
        border: 1px solid transparent;
        @extend %textNoWrap;

        &[hidden] {
            display: none !important;
        }

        &--good {
            background-color: rgba(234, 179, 8, 0.15);
            border-color: rgba(234, 179, 8, 0.35);
            color: #b45309;
        }

        &--bad {
            background-color: rgba(107, 114, 128, 0.14);
            border-color: rgba(107, 114, 128, 0.3);
            color: var(--text-color);
        }

        &--today {
            background-color: var(--primary-soft-bg, rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.14));
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: var(--primary-color);
        }
    }

    &__legend-badge {
        height: 3.6rem;
        padding: 0 1.4rem;
        font-size: 1.3rem;
    }

    &__badge-dot {
        display: inline-block;
        width: 0.8rem;
        height: 0.8rem;
        border-radius: 50%;
        @extend %flexItemFixed;

        &--good {
            background-color: #eab308;
        }

        &--bad {
            background-color: #374151;
        }

        &--today {
            background-color: var(--primary-color);
        }

        &--light {
            background-color: #ffffff;
        }
    }

    &__footer {
        @extend %flexBetween;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        gap: 1.4rem;
        padding-top: 1.2rem;
        border-top: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    }

    &__legend {
        padding-top: 0;
    }

    &__footnote {
        padding: 0.6rem 1.4rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        line-height: 1.5;
        color: var(--text-muted);
    }
}
```

### month-calendar.scss

```scss
// scss/components/_month-calendar.scss
@use "../abstracts" as abs;

.month-cal {
    @extend %flexColumn;
    gap: 1.8rem;
    padding: 2.4rem;
    background-color: var(--body-bg);
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 2.4rem;
    box-shadow: 0 0.4rem 1.6rem var(--shadow-color);
    @extend %transTheme;

    @include abs.screen(sm) {
        padding: 1.4rem;
        border-radius: 1.8rem;
        gap: 1.2rem;
    }

    // 1. Thanh điều khiển Tháng / Năm
    &__toolbar {
        @extend %flexBetween;
        gap: 1.2rem;
    }

    &__nav-btn {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        width: 4.2rem;
        height: 4.2rem;
        border-radius: 1.2rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        font-size: 1.4rem;
        color: var(--text-color);
        @extend %transInteractive;

        &:hover {
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: var(--primary-color);
            transform: translateY(-1px);
        }
    }

    &__controls {
        @extend %flexItemCenter;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        justify-content: center;
        gap: 1rem;
    }

    &__today-btn {
        height: 4.2rem;
        padding: 0 1.8rem;
        background-color: var(--primary-color);
        color: #ffffff;
        border-radius: 1.2rem;
        font-size: 1.4rem;
        box-shadow: 0 0.4rem 1.2rem var(--shadow-color);

        &:hover {
            background-color: var(--primary-hover);
            transform: translateY(-1px);
        }
    }

    // Custom Dropdown <details>
    &__dropdown {
        position: relative;
    }

    &__summary {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        gap: 0.8rem;
        height: 4.2rem;
        padding: 0 1.6rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 1.2rem;
        font-size: 1.4rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--text-color);
        cursor: pointer;
        user-select: none;
        @extend %transTheme;

        &:hover {
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
        }
    }

    &__arrow {
        width: 1.6rem;
        height: 1.6rem;
        @extend %transBurgerBar;
    }

    &__dropdown[open] &__summary {
        border-color: var(--primary-color);
        box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.2);
    }

    &__dropdown[open] &__arrow {
        transform: rotate(180deg);
    }

    &__dropdown-menu {
        position: absolute;
        top: calc(100% + 0.8rem);
        left: 50%;
        transform: translateX(-50%);
        z-index: 60;
        padding: 0.8rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 1.6rem;
        box-shadow: 0 1.2rem 3rem var(--shadow-color);
        @extend %animDropdownFadeIn;

        &--grid {
            display: grid;
            grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
            gap: 0.6rem;
            min-width: 28rem;
        }

        &--year {
            @extend %flexColumn;
            gap: 0.4rem;
            min-width: 13rem;
        }
    }

    &__option {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        width: 100%;
        padding: 0.8rem 1rem;
        border-radius: 1rem;
        font-size: 1.35rem;
        font-weight: 600;
        color: var(--text-color);
        @extend %textNoWrap;
        @extend %transTheme;

        &:hover {
            background-color: var(--bg-surface);
            color: var(--primary-color);
        }

        &.is-selected {
            background-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: #ffffff;
        }
    }

    // 2. Hàng Thứ trong tuần (Bento Header Cells)
    &__weekdays {
        display: grid;
        grid-template-columns: repeat(7, 1fr);
        gap: 0.8rem;

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            gap: 0.4rem;
        }
    }

    &__weekday {
        @extend %flexCenter;
        height: 3.8rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 1.2rem;
        font-size: 1.35rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--text-color);

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            height: 3.2rem;
            font-size: 1.15rem;
            border-radius: 0.8rem;
        }

        &--sat {
            color: var(--primary-color);
        }

        &--sun {
            color: var(--color-danger);
            background-color: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.08);
            border-color: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.25);
        }
    }

    // 3. Lưới 42 ô ngày theo PHƯƠNG ÁN 1 (Bento Góc Đối Xứng)
    &__grid {
        display: grid;
        grid-template-columns: repeat(7, 1fr);
        gap: 0.8rem;

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            gap: 0.4rem;
        }
    }

    &__cell {
        position: relative;
        overflow: hidden;
        @extend %flexTopLeft;
        flex-direction: column;
        justify-content: space-between;
        min-height: 9.6rem;
        padding: 1rem 1rem 0.8rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 1.6rem;
        cursor: pointer;
        z-index: 1;
        @extend %transInteractive;

        @include abs.screen(xl) {
            min-height: 8.6rem;
            padding: 0.8rem;
        }

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            min-height: 6.8rem;
            border-radius: 1.2rem;
            padding: 0.6rem 0.5rem;
        }

        // Ngày của tháng trước / tháng sau
        &--muted {
            opacity: 0.45;
            background-color: transparent;

            .month-cal__zodiac-img {
                opacity: 0.12;
            }
        }

        // Ngày Hôm nay (Khi chưa phải là ô Active)
        &--today:not(&--active) {
            border: 2px solid var(--primary-color);
            background-color: var(--body-bg);

            .month-cal__solar {
                color: var(--primary-color);
            }
        }

        // Hover vào ô thường (Tách biệt khỏi ô --active để không bao giờ bị trắng xoá)
        &:not(&--active):hover,
        &:not(&--active):focus-visible {
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            background-color: var(--body-bg);
            transform: translateY(-2px);
            box-shadow: 0 0.8rem 1.8rem var(--shadow-color);

            .month-cal__zodiac-img {
                opacity: 0.9;
                filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
                transform: scale(1.12) rotate(0deg) translate(-0.2rem, -0.2rem);
            }
        }

        // Trạng thái Đang Chọn (--active): Nền primary-500, Chữ trắng, Con giáp màu trầm primary-900 dương bản
        &--active {
            background-color: var(--primary-color);
            border: 2px solid var(--primary-color);
            color: #ffffff;
            box-shadow: 0 0.8rem 2rem var(--shadow-color);

            .month-cal__solar,
            .month-cal__lunar {
                color: #ffffff;
            }

            .month-cal__badge {
                background-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.24);
                color: #ffffff;
                border-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.45);
            }

            .month-cal__zodiac-img {
                opacity: 0.58;
                filter: var(--zodiac-filter-active);
                transform: scale(1.06) rotate(0deg);
            }

            // Khi Hover vào chính ô Active: Giữ nguyên nền đậm, làm nét thêm con giáp
            &:hover,
            &:focus-visible {
                background-color: var(--primary-color);
                border-color: var(--primary-hover);
                transform: translateY(-2px);
                box-shadow: 0 1rem 2.4rem var(--shadow-color);

                .month-cal__zodiac-img {
                    opacity: 0.78;
                    filter: var(--zodiac-filter-active);
                    transform: scale(1.14) rotate(0deg) translate(-0.2rem, -0.2rem);
                }
            }
        }

        // Khi di chuột vào nút chú thích ở chân lịch (Highlight Filter)
        &.is-highlighted:not(&--active) {
            opacity: 1 !important;
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            background-color: var(--body-bg);
            transform: translateY(-3px) scale(1.03);
            box-shadow: 0 0.8rem 2rem var(--shadow-color);
            z-index: 2;

            .month-cal__zodiac-img {
                opacity: 0.9;
                filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
                transform: scale(1.1) rotate(0deg);
            }
        }

        &.is-dimmed {
            opacity: 0.18;
            transform: scale(0.96);
        }
    }

    // Cụm Số Ngày Dương & Ngày Âm xếp dọc trên mọi màn hình (Phương án 1 - Chống đè nhãn Mùng 1 / Rằm)
    &__date-head {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        align-items: flex-start;
        gap: 0.25rem;
        max-width: 55%;
        line-height: 1.05;
    }

    &__solar {
        font-size: 2rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-color);
        font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;

        @include abs.screen(xl) {
            font-size: 1.8rem;
        }

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            font-size: 1.45rem;
        }
    }

    &__lunar {
        font-size: 1.2rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--text-muted);
        font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            font-size: 1rem;
        }
    }

    // Nhãn Mùng 1 / Rằm / Ngày Lễ ở Góc Trên Phải
    &__badge {
        position: absolute;
        top: 0.6rem;
        right: 0.6rem;
        z-index: 3;
        padding: 0.2rem 0.6rem;
        background-color: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.14);
        border: 1px solid rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.3);
        color: var(--color-danger);
        font-size: 1rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        border-radius: 0.6rem;
        line-height: 1.2;
        @extend %textNoWrap;

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            top: 0.35rem;
            right: 0.35rem;
            padding: 0.1rem 0.35rem;
            font-size: 0.85rem;
        }
    }

    // Cụm chấm tròn trạng thái (Hoàng Đạo / Hắc Đạo / Ngày Lễ) ở Góc Dưới Trái
    &__dots {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        @extend %flexItemCenter;
        gap: 0.5rem;
        margin-top: auto;
    }

    &__dot {
        display: inline-block;
        width: 0.8rem;
        height: 0.8rem;
        border-radius: 50%;

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            width: 0.6rem;
            height: 0.6rem;
        }

        &--good {
            background-color: #eab308;
            box-shadow: 0 0 0 2px rgba(234, 179, 8, 0.25);
        }

        &--bad {
            background-color: #374151;
            box-shadow: 0 0 0 2px rgba(55, 65, 81, 0.2);
        }

        &--holiday {
            background-color: #ef4444;
            box-shadow: 0 0 0 2px rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.25);
        }
    }

    // Thẻ <img> 12 Con Giáp (128x128px, fill="#000") ở Góc Dưới Phải
    &__zodiac-img {
        position: absolute;
        right: 0.5rem;
        bottom: 0.4rem;
        width: 4.6rem;
        height: 4.6rem;
        object-fit: contain;
        opacity: 0.2;
        filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
        transform: scale(0.78) rotate(-8deg);
        transform-origin: bottom right;
        pointer-events: none;
        z-index: 1;
        transition:
            transform 0.4s cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1),
            opacity 0.35s ease,
            filter 0.3s ease;

        @include abs.screen(xl) {
            width: 4rem;
            height: 4rem;
        }

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            width: 2.8rem;
            height: 2.8rem;
            right: 0.2rem;
            bottom: 0.2rem;
        }
    }

    // 4. Chú thích cuối bảng (Bo góc Bento 1.2rem)
    &__legend {
        @extend %flexCenter;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        gap: 1rem;
        padding-top: 0.6rem;
    }

    &__legend-pill {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        gap: 0.8rem;
        height: 3.8rem;
        padding: 0 1.4rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 1.2rem;
        font-size: 1.3rem;
        font-weight: 600;
        color: var(--text-color);
        cursor: pointer;
        @extend %transInteractive;

        &:hover,
        &:focus-visible,
        &.is-active {
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            background-color: var(--body-bg);
            transform: translateY(-2px);
            box-shadow: 0 0.6rem 1.4rem var(--shadow-color);
        }
    }

    &__legend-dot {
        display: inline-block;
        width: 1rem;
        height: 1rem;
        border-radius: 50%;

        &--good {
            background-color: #eab308;
        }
        &--bad {
            background-color: #374151;
        }
        &--holiday {
            background-color: #ef4444;
        }
        &--today {
            border: 2px solid var(--primary-color);
        }
    }
}
```

### converter-modal.scss

```scss
// scss/components/_converter-modal.scss
@use "../abstracts" as abs;

.converter-modal {
    @extend %fixedCover;
    z-index: 90;
    @extend %flexCenter;
    padding: 2.4rem;

    &[hidden] {
        display: none !important;
    }

    @include abs.screen(sm) {
        padding: 1rem;
    }

    &__backdrop {
        @extend %absCover;
        background-color: var(--overlay-bg);
        backdrop-filter: blur(6px);
    }

    // FIX ẢNH 5: Mở rộng khung Bento Modal lên 112rem (1120px) để các thành phần bên trong rộng thoáng, không bị nhồi nhét
    &__dialog {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        width: 100%;
        max-width: 112rem;
        max-height: calc(100vh - 4rem);
        overflow-y: auto;
        padding: 3.2rem 3.6rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1.5px solid var(--primary-color);
        border-radius: 3rem;
        box-shadow: 0 2.4rem 6.4rem var(--shadow-color);
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 2.2rem;
        @extend %animDropdownFadeIn;
        @extend %transTheme;

        @include abs.screen(xl) {
            max-width: 96rem;
            padding: 2.6rem 2.8rem;
        }

        @include abs.screen(lg) {
            max-width: 76rem;
            padding: 2.2rem;
        }

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            padding: 1.6rem;
            border-radius: 2rem;
            gap: 1.6rem;
            max-height: calc(100vh - 2rem);
        }
    }

    // FIX ẢNH 2: Tăng khoảng cách (margin/gap/padding) trên và dưới của cụm Tiêu đề Modal
    &__header {
        @extend %flexBetween;
        align-items: center;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        gap: 2rem;
        padding-top: 0.4rem;
        padding-bottom: 2.2rem;
        border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    }

    &__title-wrap {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 1rem; // Tăng khoảng cách giữa badge "TIỆN ÍCH VẠN NIÊN" và tiêu đề "Đổi Ngày Âm ⇄ Dương"
    }

    &__badge {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        gap: 0.6rem;
        width: fit-content;
        padding: 0.45rem 1.2rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        font-size: 1.2rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--primary-color);
        text-transform: uppercase;
        letter-spacing: 0.05em;

        &-icon {
            width: 1.5rem;
            height: 1.5rem;
        }
    }

    &__title {
        font-size: 2.6rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        line-height: 1.2;
        color: var(--text-color);

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            font-size: 2rem;
        }
    }

    // FIX ẢNH 3: Cố định kích thước đều nhau cho 2 nút Tab chuyển đổi chiều Âm/Dương
    &__tabs {
        display: grid;
        grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);
        gap: 0.6rem;
        padding: 0.5rem;
        min-width: 42rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 1.6rem;

        @include abs.screen(md) {
            order: 3;
            width: 100%;
            min-width: 0;
        }
    }

    &__tab {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        height: 4.2rem;
        padding: 0 1.8rem;
        border-radius: 1.2rem;
        font-size: 1.4rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--text-muted);
        @extend %textNoWrap;
        @extend %transTheme;

        &:hover {
            color: var(--text-color);
        }

        &.is-active {
            background-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: #ffffff;
            box-shadow: 0 0.4rem 1.2rem var(--shadow-color);
        }
    }

    &__close-btn {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        width: 4.4rem;
        height: 4.4rem;
        border-radius: 1.4rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        font-size: 1.7rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--text-muted);
        @extend %transInteractive;

        &:hover {
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: var(--primary-color);
            transform: rotate(90deg);
        }
    }

    // FIX ẢNH 3 & ẢNH 5: Bộ 4 Cột Đồng Phẳng (Ngày | Tháng | Năm | Chế độ Tháng Nhuận) -> Chiều cao cố định 100%
    &__control-bar {
        padding: 1.8rem 2rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 2.2rem;
    }

    &__pickers {
        @extend %gridCols4;
        gap: 1.6rem;

        @include abs.screen(lg) {
            grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);
            gap: 1.2rem;
        }

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            grid-template-columns: 1fr;
        }
    }

    &__field {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 0.6rem;
        position: relative;
    }

    &__label {
        font-size: 1.2rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--text-muted);
        text-transform: uppercase;
        letter-spacing: 0.04em;
        padding-left: 0.4rem;
    }

    &__dropdown {
        position: relative;
    }

    &__summary {
        @extend %flexBetween;
        height: 4.8rem;
        padding: 0 1.6rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 1.4rem;
        font-size: 1.5rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--text-color);
        cursor: pointer;
        user-select: none;
        @extend %transTheme;

        &:hover {
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
        }

        &-main {
            @extend %flexItemCenter;
            gap: 0.9rem;
        }

        &-icon {
            width: 1.9rem;
            height: 1.9rem;
            opacity: 0.8;
        }
    }

    &__arrow {
        width: 1.6rem;
        height: 1.6rem;
        @extend %transBurgerBar;
    }

    &__dropdown[open] &__summary {
        border-color: var(--primary-color);
        box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.18);
    }

    &__dropdown[open] &__arrow {
        transform: rotate(180deg);
    }

    // Ô Cột 4: Nút gạt Tháng Nhuận có cùng chiều cao 4.8rem với 3 ô Ngày/Tháng/Năm
    &__leap-toggle {
        @extend %flexYCenterLeft;
        gap: 1rem;
        width: 100%;
        height: 4.8rem;
        padding: 0 1.6rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 1.4rem;
        font-size: 1.4rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--text-color);
        cursor: pointer;
        @extend %transTheme;

        &:hover:not(.is-disabled) {
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
        }

        &.is-active {
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            background-color: rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.1);
            color: var(--primary-color);
        }

        &.is-disabled {
            opacity: 0.7;
            cursor: default;
            background-color: var(--body-bg);
            border-style: dashed;
        }
    }

    &__checkbox {
        width: 1.8rem;
        height: 1.8rem;
        accent-color: var(--primary-color);
        pointer-events: none;
        @extend %flexItemFixed;
    }

    &__leap-text {
        @extend %textEllipsis;
    }

    // Popover Lưới Bento cho Ngày / Tháng / Năm
    &__popover {
        position: absolute;
        top: calc(100% + 0.8rem);
        left: 0;
        z-index: 80;
        width: 100%;
        min-width: 28rem;
        padding: 1.4rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1.5px solid var(--primary-color);
        border-radius: 1.8rem;
        box-shadow: 0 1.6rem 3.6rem var(--shadow-color);
        @extend %animDropdownFadeIn;

        &--day {
            min-width: 32rem;

            @include abs.screen(sm) {
                min-width: 100%;
            }
        }

        &--year {
            right: 0;
            left: auto;

            @include abs.screen(md) {
                left: 0;
                right: auto;
            }
        }
    }

    &__popover-head {
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--text-muted);
        text-align: center;
        margin-bottom: 1rem;
        padding-bottom: 0.6rem;
        border-bottom: 1px dashed var(--border-color);
    }

    &__year-nav {
        @extend %flexBetween;
        margin-bottom: 1rem;
        padding-bottom: 0.6rem;
        border-bottom: 1px dashed var(--border-color);
    }

    &__year-step {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        width: 3.4rem;
        height: 3.4rem;
        border-radius: 0.8rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--text-color);
        @extend %transInteractive;

        &:hover {
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: var(--primary-color);
        }
    }

    &__year-range-label {
        font-size: 1.35rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--primary-color);
    }

    &__grid-options {
        display: grid;
        gap: 0.6rem;

        &--days {
            grid-template-columns: repeat(7, 1fr);
        }

        &--months {
            grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
        }

        &--years {
            grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr);
        }
    }

    &__option-btn {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        width: 100%;
        height: 3.8rem;
        padding: 0 0.6rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 1rem;
        font-size: 1.35rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--text-color);
        font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
        @extend %transInteractive;

        &:hover {
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: var(--primary-color);
            transform: translateY(-1px);
        }

        &.is-selected {
            background-color: var(--primary-color);
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: #ffffff;
            box-shadow: 0 0.4rem 1rem var(--shadow-color);
        }
    }

    // 3. Khối Bento Dashboard Kết quả
    &__bento-body {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 1.8rem;
    }

    // Tầng A: 2 Đảo Lớn Dương Lịch ⇄ Âm Lịch
    &__hero-compare {
        display: grid;
        grid-template-columns: 1fr auto 1fr;
        align-items: center;
        gap: 1.8rem;

        @include abs.screen(md) {
            grid-template-columns: 1fr;
            gap: 1.2rem;
        }
    }

    &__date-card {
        position: relative;
        overflow: hidden;
        @extend %flexColumn;
        justify-content: space-between;
        min-height: 14.2rem;
        gap: 0.8rem;
        padding: 2.2rem 2.4rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 2.2rem;
        @extend %transTheme;

        &--lunar {
            background-color: var(--body-bg);
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            box-shadow: 0 0.8rem 2.4rem var(--shadow-color);
        }
    }

    &__date-top {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        @extend %flexBetween;
        gap: 1rem;
    }

    &__date-tag {
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-muted);
        text-transform: uppercase;
        letter-spacing: 0.04em;
    }

    &__date-badge {
        padding: 0.4rem 1.2rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        font-size: 1.2rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-color);

        &--primary {
            background-color: var(--primary-color);
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: #ffffff;
        }
    }

    &__date-num {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        font-size: 3.8rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        line-height: 1.05;
        color: var(--primary-color);
        font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            font-size: 2.8rem;
        }
    }

    &__date-sub {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        font-size: 1.4rem;
        font-weight: 600;
        color: var(--text-color);
    }

    &__date-watermark {
        position: absolute;
        right: 1.6rem;
        bottom: 1rem;
        width: 6.4rem;
        height: 6.4rem;
        opacity: 0.07;
        pointer-events: none;
    }

    &__date-zodiac {
        position: absolute;
        right: 1.4rem;
        bottom: 0.8rem;
        width: 7rem;
        height: 7rem;
        object-fit: contain;
        opacity: 0.22;
        filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
        transform: rotate(-8deg);
        pointer-events: none;
    }

    &__swap-btn {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        width: 4.8rem;
        height: 4.8rem;
        border-radius: 50%;
        background-color: var(--primary-color);
        color: #ffffff;
        font-size: 2rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        box-shadow: 0 0.8rem 2rem var(--shadow-color);
        margin: 0 auto;
        @extend %transInteractive;

        &:hover {
            background-color: var(--primary-hover);
            transform: rotate(180deg) scale(1.08);
        }
    }

    // FIX ẢNH 4 (PHẦN TRÊN): Tăng chiều cao 4 Card Can Chi / Mệnh / Tiết khí (min-height: 11.6rem) để không còn cảm giác nhồi nhét
    &__stats-grid {
        @extend %gridCols4;
        gap: 1.6rem;

        @include abs.screen(lg) {
            grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);
        }

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            grid-template-columns: 1fr;
        }
    }

    &__stat-card {
        position: relative;
        overflow: hidden;
        @extend %flexItemCenter;
        min-height: 11.6rem;
        gap: 1.5rem;
        padding: 1.8rem 2rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 2rem;
        @extend %transInteractive;

        &:hover {
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            background-color: var(--body-bg);
            transform: translateY(-2px);
            box-shadow: 0 0.8rem 2rem var(--shadow-color);

            .converter-modal__stat-bg-zodiac {
                opacity: 0.22;
                transform: scale(1.08) rotate(0deg);
            }
        }
    }

    &__stat-icon-box {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        @extend %flexItemFixed;
        width: 5.2rem;
        height: 5.2rem;
        border-radius: 1.5rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        box-shadow: 0 0.4rem 1.2rem var(--shadow-color);
    }

    &__stat-zodiac {
        width: 3.4rem;
        height: 3.4rem;
        object-fit: contain;
        filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
    }

    &__stat-element-icon {
        width: 2.8rem;
        height: 2.8rem;
        object-fit: contain;
    }

    &__stat-content {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        @extend %flexColumn;
        justify-content: center;
        gap: 0.45rem;
        min-width: 0;
    }

    &__stat-label {
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--text-muted);
        @extend %textNoWrap;
    }

    &__stat-value {
        font-size: 1.8rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        line-height: 1.2;
        color: var(--text-color);
    }

    &__stat-sub {
        font-size: 1.3rem;
        font-weight: 600;
        color: var(--primary-color);
    }

    &__stat-bg-zodiac {
        position: absolute;
        right: 0.6rem;
        bottom: 0.4rem;
        width: 5.6rem;
        height: 5.6rem;
        object-fit: contain;
        opacity: 0.1;
        filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
        transform: rotate(-10deg);
        pointer-events: none;
        transition:
            transform 0.35s ease,
            opacity 0.35s ease;
    }

    // FIX ẢNH 4 (PHẦN DƯỚI): Tăng chiều cao 6 Card Giờ Hoàng Đạo (min-height: 10.4rem) & Tăng cỡ chữ Title Giờ ("Giờ Giáp Tý")
    &__hours-section {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 1.6rem;
        padding: 2rem 2.2rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 2.2rem;
    }

    &__hours-head {
        @extend %flexBetween;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        gap: 1.2rem;
    }

    &__hours-title-group {
        @extend %flexItemCenter;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        gap: 1.2rem;
    }

    &__fortune-pill {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        gap: 0.7rem;
        padding: 0.5rem 1.4rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        font-size: 1.3rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-color);

        &[data-fortune="good"] {
            border-color: rgba(234, 179, 8, 0.5);
            background-color: rgba(234, 179, 8, 0.12);

            .converter-modal__fortune-dot {
                background-color: #eab308;
                box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgba(234, 179, 8, 0.25);
            }
        }

        &[data-fortune="bad"] {
            border-color: rgba(107, 114, 128, 0.4);

            .converter-modal__fortune-dot {
                background-color: #6b7280;
            }
        }
    }

    &__fortune-dot {
        width: 0.9rem;
        height: 0.9rem;
        border-radius: 50%;
        background-color: var(--primary-color);
    }

    &__hours-heading {
        font-size: 1.65rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-color);
    }

    &__hours-note {
        font-size: 1.3rem;
        font-weight: 600;
        color: var(--text-muted);
    }

    &__hours-grid {
        display: grid;
        grid-template-columns: repeat(6, 1fr);
        gap: 1.4rem;

        @include abs.screen(lg) {
            grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
        }

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);
            gap: 1rem;
        }
    }

    &__hour-card {
        position: relative;
        overflow: hidden;
        @extend %flexColumn;
        justify-content: space-between;
        min-height: 10.4rem; // Cao hơn hẳn so với 7.4rem cũ để thoáng và tôn hình con giáp
        padding: 1.5rem 1.6rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 1.6rem;
        @extend %transInteractive;

        &:hover {
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            transform: translateY(-3px);
            box-shadow: 0 0.8rem 1.8rem var(--shadow-color);

            .converter-modal__hour-zodiac {
                opacity: 0.45;
                transform: scale(1.1) rotate(0deg);
            }
        }
    }

    &__hour-top {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        @extend %flexItemCenter;
        gap: 0.7rem;
    }

    &__hour-dot {
        width: 0.8rem;
        height: 0.8rem;
        border-radius: 50%;
        background-color: #eab308;
        box-shadow: 0 0 0 2px rgba(234, 179, 8, 0.22);
        @extend %flexItemFixed;
    }

    // Tăng cỡ chữ Title giờ ("Giờ Giáp Tý") lên 1.65rem to rõ, sang trọng
    &__hour-name {
        font-size: 1.65rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        line-height: 1.2;
        color: var(--text-color);
    }

    &__hour-time {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        margin-top: auto;
        padding-top: 0.8rem;
        font-size: 1.35rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--primary-color);
        font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
    }

    &__hour-zodiac {
        position: absolute;
        right: 0.6rem;
        bottom: 0.5rem;
        width: 4.6rem;
        height: 4.6rem;
        object-fit: contain;
        opacity: 0.2;
        filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
        transform: rotate(-8deg);
        pointer-events: none;
        transition:
            transform 0.3s ease,
            opacity 0.3s ease;
    }

    // 4. Footer Modal
    &__footer {
        @extend %flexBetween;
        gap: 1.4rem;
        padding-top: 0.4rem;

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            flex-direction: column;

            .btn {
                width: 100%;
            }
        }
    }

    &__today-btn {
        height: 4.8rem;
        padding: 0 2.2rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        font-size: 1.45rem;
        color: var(--text-color);

        &:hover {
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: var(--primary-color);
        }
    }

    &__jump-btn {
        @extend %flexItemFluid;
        height: 4.8rem;
        padding: 0 2.6rem;
        background-color: var(--primary-color);
        color: #ffffff;
        font-size: 1.55rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        box-shadow: 0 0.8rem 2rem var(--shadow-color);

        &:hover {
            background-color: var(--primary-hover);
            transform: translateY(-1px);
        }
    }
}
```

### festival-calendar.scss

```scss
// scss/components/_festival-calendar.scss
@use "../abstracts" as abs;

.festival-cal {
    @extend %flexColumn;
    gap: 2rem;
    line-height: 1.5;

    &__dot {
        display: inline-block;
        width: 0.75rem;
        height: 0.75rem;
        border-radius: 50%;
        @extend %flexItemFixed;

        &--lunar {
            background-color: #ef4444;
        }

        &--solar {
            background-color: #2563eb;
        }
    }

    // ==========================================================================
    // ROW 2: WORKSPACE 2 CỘT (TRÁI: STICKY HERO | PHẢI: CONTROLS + LIST EVENT)
    // ==========================================================================
    &__workspace {
        display: grid;
        grid-template-columns: 38rem minmax(0, 1fr);
        align-items: start;
        gap: 2rem;

        @include abs.screen(xl) {
            grid-template-columns: 34rem minmax(0, 1fr);
        }

        @include abs.screen(lg) {
            grid-template-columns: 1fr;
        }
    }

    // FIX GAP CHUẨN 2REM: 1.6rem (top của Countdown) + chiều cao Countdown + 2rem (gap chuẩn)
    &__sticky-left {
        position: sticky;
        top: calc(1.6rem + var(--countdown-exact-h, 15.2rem) + 2rem);
        z-index: 20;

        @include abs.screen(lg) {
            position: static;
        }
    }

    &__main-right {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 2rem;
        min-width: 0;
    }
}

// ==========================================================================
// ROW 1 (STICKY): THANH ĐỒNG HỒ ĐẾM NGƯỢC LỚN + CUSTOM DROPDOWN KIỂU A
// ==========================================================================
// ==========================================================================
// ROW 1 (STICKY): THANH ĐỒNG HỒ ĐẾM NGƯỢC LỚN + CUSTOM DROPDOWN KIỂU A
// ==========================================================================
.festival-countdown-bar {
    position: sticky;
    top: 1.6rem;
    z-index: 45;
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1.15fr) minmax(0, 1fr);
    align-items: center;
    gap: 2.4rem;
    padding: 2.2rem 2.8rem;
    background-color: var(--body-bg);
    border: 1.5px solid var(--primary-color);
    border-radius: 2.6rem;
    box-shadow: 0 1.2rem 3.2rem var(--shadow-color);
    @extend %transTheme;

    @include abs.screen(xl) {
        position: relative;
        top: 0;
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
        gap: 1.8rem;
    }

    @include abs.screen(sm) {
        padding: 1.6rem;
        border-radius: 2rem;
        gap: 1.4rem;
    }

    &__info {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        justify-content: space-between;
        gap: 0.85rem;
        min-width: 0;
    }

    &__top-row {
        display: grid;
        grid-template-columns: auto minmax(0, 1fr);
        align-items: center;
        gap: 1.2rem;

        // FIX MOBILE: Xuống dưới 768px (md) xếp dọc Badge và thanh Chọn sự kiện để không bao giờ tràn viền phải
        @include abs.screen(md) {
            grid-template-columns: 1fr;
            gap: 1rem;
        }
    }

    &__badge {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        @extend %flexItemFixed;
        gap: 0.65rem;
        width: fit-content;
        height: 3.6rem;
        padding: 0 1.3rem;
        background-color: var(--primary-soft-bg, var(--bg-surface));
        border: 1px solid var(--primary-color);
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        font-size: 1.2rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--primary-color);
        @extend %textNoWrap;
    }

    &__pulse-dot {
        width: 0.8rem;
        height: 0.8rem;
        border-radius: 50%;
        background-color: var(--primary-color);
        animation: pulse 2s ease-in-out infinite;
    }

    &__selector {
        @extend %flexYCenterRight;
        gap: 0.8rem;
        width: 100%;
        min-width: 0;

        @include abs.screen(md) {
            justify-content: flex-start;
        }
    }

    &__select-label {
        @extend %flexItemFixed;
        font-size: 1.3rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-muted);
        @extend %textNoWrap;
    }

    &__heading {
        font-size: clamp(1.9rem, 2.3vw, 2.5rem);
        font-weight: 800;
        line-height: 1.3;
        min-height: 3.25rem;
        color: var(--text-color);
        @extend %textEllipsis;

        strong {
            color: var(--primary-color);
        }

        // FIX MOBILE: Cho phép tiêu đề tự xuống dòng trên màn hình nhỏ để không bị cắt mất tên ngày lễ
        @include abs.screen(md) {
            white-space: normal;
            overflow: visible;
            text-overflow: clip;
            min-height: 0;
        }
    }

    &__subtitles {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 0.3rem;
        min-width: 0;
    }

    &__sub-line {
        font-size: 1.35rem;
        font-weight: 600;
        line-height: 1.45;
        min-height: 1.95rem;
        color: var(--text-color);
        @extend %textEllipsis;

        &--lunar {
            color: var(--text-muted);
        }

        @include abs.screen(md) {
            font-size: 1.25rem;
            white-space: normal;
            overflow: visible;
            text-overflow: clip;
            min-height: 0;
        }
    }

    &__clock-wrap {
        @extend %flexYCenterRight;
        width: 100%;
    }

    &__timer {
        display: grid;
        grid-template-columns: 1fr auto 1fr auto 1fr auto 1fr;
        align-items: center;
        gap: 0.8rem;
        width: 100%;
        padding: 1.4rem 1.8rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 2.2rem;
        font-family: abs.$body-font-family;

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            padding: 1rem;
            gap: 0.4rem;
            border-radius: 1.6rem;
        }
    }

    &__unit {
        @extend %flexColumnCenterAll;
        gap: 0.35rem;
        height: 9.6rem;
        padding: 1rem 0.6rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 1.6rem;
        box-shadow: 0 0.4rem 1.2rem var(--shadow-color);
        @extend %transTheme;

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            height: 7.2rem;
            padding: 0.6rem 0.2rem;
            border-radius: 1.2rem;
        }

        &--sec {
            background-color: var(--primary-soft-bg, var(--body-bg));
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
        }
    }

    &__num {
        font-family: abs.$body-font-family;
        font-size: clamp(2.6rem, 3.8vw, 4.4rem);
        font-weight: 800;
        line-height: 1;
        letter-spacing: -0.03em;
        color: var(--primary-color);
        font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
        @extend %transTheme;

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            font-size: 2.2rem;
        }

        &--sec {
            color: var(--primary-active);
        }
    }

    &__unit-label {
        font-size: 1.15rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        text-transform: uppercase;
        letter-spacing: 0.04em;
        color: var(--text-muted);

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            font-size: 1rem;
        }
    }

    &__sep {
        font-size: 2.6rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--primary-color);
        animation: pulse 2s ease-in-out infinite;

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            font-size: 1.8rem;
        }
    }
}

// ==========================================================================
// CUSTOM DROPDOWN CHỌN SỰ KIỆN KIỂU A (KHÔNG TRÀN VIỀN TRÊN MOBILE)
// ==========================================================================
.festival-picker {
    position: relative;
    width: 31rem;
    max-width: 100%;
    min-width: 0;

    // FIX MOBILE: Cho phép co giãn linh hoạt (flex: 1 1 0%) để nằm gọn 100% trong khung Countdown
    @include abs.screen(md) {
        flex: 1 1 0%;
        width: auto;
    }

    &__sr-only {
        @extend %srOnly;
    }

    &__summary {
        @extend %flexBetween;
        gap: 0.6rem;
        width: 100%;
        height: 4rem;
        padding: 0 1.2rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 1.3rem;
        font-size: 1.35rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--text-color);
        cursor: pointer;
        user-select: none;
        @extend %transTheme;

        &:hover {
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
        }

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            padding: 0 1rem;
            font-size: 1.25rem;
        }
    }

    &[open] &__summary {
        border-color: var(--primary-color);
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px var(--primary-soft-bg);
    }

    &__summary-name {
        @extend %textEllipsisFlex;
        text-align: left;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-color);
    }

    &__summary-date {
        @extend %flexItemFixed;
        padding: 0.2rem 0.65rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 0.7rem;
        font-size: 1.15rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--primary-color);
        font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            font-size: 1.05rem;
            padding: 0.15rem 0.5rem;
        }
    }

    &__arrow {
        @extend %flexItemFixed;
        width: 1.5rem;
        height: 1.5rem;
        @extend %transBurgerBar;
    }

    &[open] &__arrow {
        transform: rotate(180deg);
    }

    &__popover {
        position: absolute;
        top: calc(100% + 0.8rem);
        right: 0;
        z-index: 80;
        width: 38rem;
        max-width: calc(100vw - 3.2rem);
        padding: 1.2rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1.5px solid var(--primary-color);
        border-radius: 2rem;
        box-shadow: 0 1.6rem 3.8rem var(--shadow-color);
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 1rem;
        @extend %animDropdownFadeIn;

        @include abs.screen(md) {
            left: 0;
            right: 0;
            width: 100%;
        }
    }

    &__search-box {
        width: 100%;
    }

    &__search-input {
        width: 100%;
        height: 3.8rem;
        padding: 0 1.2rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 1.1rem;
        font-size: 1.3rem;
        font-weight: 600;
        color: var(--text-color);

        &:focus {
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            background-color: var(--body-bg);
        }
    }

    &__tabs {
        display: grid;
        grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
        gap: 0.4rem;
        padding: 0.35rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 1.1rem;
    }

    &__tab {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        height: 3rem;
        border-radius: 0.8rem;
        font-size: 1.2rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--text-muted);
        @extend %transTheme;

        &:hover {
            color: var(--text-color);
        }

        &.is-active {
            background-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: #ffffff;
        }
    }

    &__list {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 0.35rem;
        max-height: 24rem;
        overflow-y: auto;
        padding-right: 0.3rem;
    }

    &__option {
        @extend %flexBetween;
        gap: 1rem;
        width: 100%;
        padding: 0.85rem 1.1rem;
        border-radius: 1rem;
        font-size: 1.35rem;
        font-weight: 600;
        color: var(--text-color);
        text-align: left;
        @extend %transTheme;

        &[hidden] {
            display: none !important;
        }

        &:hover,
        &:focus-visible {
            background-color: var(--bg-surface);
            color: var(--primary-color);
        }

        &.is-selected {
            background-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: #ffffff;

            .festival-picker__opt-name,
            .festival-picker__opt-date {
                color: #ffffff;
            }

            .festival-picker__opt-date {
                background-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2);
                border-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.35);
            }
        }
    }

    &__opt-name {
        @extend %textEllipsisFlex;
        font-weight: 700;
    }

    &__opt-date {
        @extend %flexItemFixed;
        padding: 0.2rem 0.65rem;
        border-radius: 0.6rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        font-size: 1.15rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-muted);
        font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
    }

    &__foot {
        padding-top: 0.6rem;
        border-top: 1px dashed var(--border-color);
        text-align: center;
        font-size: 1.15rem;
        font-weight: 600;
        color: var(--text-muted);
    }
}

// ==========================================================================
// CỘT TRÁI: THẺ HERO TIÊU ĐIỂM (MỞ KHÓA CẮT DÒNG TRÊN MOBILE)
// ==========================================================================
.festival-hero {
    position: relative;
    overflow: hidden;
    @extend %flexColumn;
    justify-content: space-between;
    min-height: 46rem;
    max-height: 46rem;
    gap: 1.4rem;
    padding: 2.6rem;
    background-color: var(--primary-color);
    border: 1px solid var(--primary-hover);
    border-radius: 2.6rem;
    color: #ffffff;
    box-shadow: 0 1rem 2.8rem var(--shadow-color);
    @extend %transTheme;

    @include abs.screen(lg) {
        min-height: auto;
        max-height: none;
    }

    @include abs.screen(sm) {
        padding: 1.8rem;
        border-radius: 2rem;
    }

    &__top {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 0.8rem;
        padding-bottom: 1.2rem;
        border-bottom: 1px solid var(--hero-border-color);
    }

    &__badges {
        display: grid;
        grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) auto;
        align-items: center;
        gap: 0.8rem;

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            grid-template-columns: 1fr;
            justify-items: start;
        }
    }

    &__pill {
        max-width: 100%;
        height: 3.2rem;
        line-height: 3.2rem;
        padding: 0 1.2rem;
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        font-size: 1.2rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        background-color: #ffffff;
        color: var(--primary-active);
        @extend %textEllipsis;
    }

    &__type-badge {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        gap: 0.6rem;
        height: 3.2rem;
        padding: 0 1.2rem;
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        font-size: 1.2rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        background-color: var(--hero-box-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--hero-border-color);
        color: #ffffff;
        @extend %textNoWrap;
    }

    &__date-row {
        @extend %flexItemCenter;
        align-items: baseline;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        gap: 1rem;
        margin-top: 0.2rem;
    }

    &__solar {
        font-size: clamp(4.4rem, 5vw, 5.6rem);
        font-weight: 800;
        line-height: 1;
        letter-spacing: -0.03em;
        font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
    }

    &__weekday-group {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 0.15rem;
    }

    &__weekday {
        font-size: 1.9rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        line-height: 1.2;
        color: #ffffff;
    }

    &__year {
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        font-weight: 600;
        color: var(--hero-text-soft);
    }

    &__title {
        font-size: 2rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        line-height: 1.3;
        min-height: 2.6rem;
        color: #ffffff;
        @extend %textEllipsis;

        @include abs.screen(lg) {
            white-space: normal;
            overflow: visible;
            min-height: 0;
        }
    }

    &__lunar {
        font-size: 1.35rem;
        color: var(--hero-text-soft);
        @extend %textEllipsis;

        @include abs.screen(lg) {
            white-space: normal;
            overflow: visible;
        }
    }

    &__detail-box {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        @extend %flexColumn;
        justify-content: space-between;
        gap: 0.8rem;
        padding: 1.2rem 1.4rem;
        background-color: var(--hero-box-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--hero-border-color);
        border-radius: 1.6rem;
    }

    &__desc {
        font-size: 1.3rem;
        line-height: 1.45;
        min-height: 3.8rem;
        color: #ffffff;
        @extend %textClamp2;

        @include abs.screen(lg) {
            display: block;
            min-height: 0;
        }
    }

    &__hours-line {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 0.2rem;
        padding-top: 0.6rem;
        border-top: 1px dashed var(--hero-border-color);
    }

    &__hours-label {
        font-size: 1.15rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        text-transform: uppercase;
        color: var(--hero-text-soft);
    }

    &__hours-val {
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: #ffffff;
        @extend %textEllipsis;

        @include abs.screen(lg) {
            white-space: normal;
            overflow: visible;
        }
    }

    &__footer {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        @extend %flexBetween;
        padding-top: 0.2rem;
    }

    &__link {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        gap: 0.8rem;
        height: 4rem;
        padding: 0 1.8rem;
        background-color: var(--hero-chip-bg);
        color: #ffffff;
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        font-size: 1.35rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        @extend %transInteractive;

        &:hover,
        &:focus-visible {
            background-color: var(--primary-active);
            transform: translateY(-2px);
        }
    }

    &__zodiac {
        position: absolute;
        right: -0.8rem;
        bottom: -0.8rem;
        width: 14rem;
        height: 14rem;
        object-fit: contain;
        opacity: 0.14;
        filter: var(--zodiac-filter-active);
        pointer-events: none;
        z-index: 1;
    }
}

// ==========================================================================
// CỘT PHẢI (TRÊN): KHỐI BỘ ĐIỀU KHIỂN & BENTO 4 QUÝ
// ==========================================================================
.festival-controls {
    @extend %flexColumn;
    justify-content: space-between;
    gap: 1.4rem;
    padding: 2.2rem 2.4rem;
    background-color: var(--body-bg);
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 2.6rem;
    box-shadow: 0 0.8rem 2.4rem var(--shadow-color);
    @extend %transTheme;

    @include abs.screen(sm) {
        padding: 1.6rem;
        border-radius: 2rem;
    }

    &__head {
        @extend %flexBetween;
        align-items: center;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        gap: 1.4rem;
    }

    &__intro {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 0.4rem;
    }

    &__eyebrow-row {
        @extend %flexWrapGap;
    }

    &__badge,
    &__count-pill {
        padding: 0.35rem 1.2rem;
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        color: var(--primary-color);
    }

    &__count-pill {
        color: var(--text-muted);
    }

    &__heading {
        font-size: clamp(2.1rem, 2.4vw, 2.6rem);
        font-weight: 800;
        line-height: 1.2;
        color: var(--text-color);
    }

    &__year-toolbar {
        @extend %flexItemCenter;
        gap: 0.6rem;
        padding: 0.45rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 1.5rem;
    }

    &__filter-row {
        @extend %flexBetween;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        gap: 1rem;
        padding-top: 1.2rem;
        border-top: 1px dashed var(--border-color);
    }

    &__filter-bar {
        @extend %flexWrapGap;
        gap: 0.8rem;
    }

    &__filter-btn {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        gap: 0.65rem;
        height: 3.8rem;
        padding: 0 1.5rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        font-size: 1.35rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--text-color);
        @extend %transInteractive;

        small {
            padding: 0.1rem 0.6rem;
            border-radius: 99.9rem;
            background-color: var(--body-bg);
            font-size: 1.15rem;
            font-weight: 800;
            color: var(--text-muted);
            font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
        }

        &:hover {
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: var(--primary-color);
        }

        &.is-active {
            background-color: var(--primary-color);
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: #ffffff;

            small {
                background-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.22);
                color: #ffffff;
            }
        }
    }

    &__reset-btn {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        gap: 0.5rem;
        height: 3.6rem;
        padding: 0 1.3rem;
        background-color: var(--primary-soft-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--primary-color);
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--primary-color);
        @extend %transInteractive;

        &[hidden] {
            display: none !important;
        }

        &:hover {
            background-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: #ffffff;
        }
    }
}

.festival-seasons-bento {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: auto repeat(4, minmax(0, 1fr));
    align-items: stretch;
    gap: 0.8rem;

    @include abs.screen(xxl) {
        grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
    }

    @include abs.screen(sm) {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }

    &__master {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        gap: 0.7rem;
        min-width: 10.6rem;
        padding: 1rem 1.4rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 1.6rem;
        font-size: 1.35rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-color);
        @extend %textNoWrap;
        @extend %transInteractive;

        @include abs.screen(xxl) {
            grid-column: 1 / -1;
            min-height: 4.2rem;
        }

        &:hover {
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: var(--primary-color);
        }

        &.is-active {
            background-color: var(--primary-color);
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: #ffffff;
            box-shadow: 0 0.6rem 1.6rem var(--shadow-color);

            .festival-seasons-bento__master-icon {
                filter: brightness(0) invert(1);
            }
        }
    }

    &__master-icon {
        width: 1.8rem;
```
