```scss
        height: 1.8rem;
        opacity: 0.85;
    }

    &__quarter {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        justify-content: space-between;
        gap: 0.55rem;
        padding: 0.8rem 0.9rem 0.9rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 1.6rem;
        @extend %transTheme;

        &:has(.is-active),
        &.is-quarter-active {
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            background-color: var(--primary-soft-bg, var(--bg-surface));
        }
    }

    &__q-label {
        display: block;
        text-align: center;
        font-size: 1.1rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-muted);
        @extend %textNoWrap;
    }

    &__q-grid {
        display: grid;
        grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr));
        gap: 0.45rem;
    }

    &__btn {
        position: relative;
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        height: 3.2rem;
        padding: 0 0.4rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 0.9rem;
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-color);
        font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
        @extend %transInteractive;

        &[data-has-major="true"]::after {
            content: "";
            position: absolute;
            top: 0.3rem;
            right: 0.3rem;
            width: 0.45rem;
            height: 0.45rem;
            border-radius: 50%;
            background-color: var(--primary-color);
        }

        &:hover {
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: var(--primary-color);
            transform: translateY(-1px);
        }

        &.is-active {
            background-color: var(--primary-color);
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: #ffffff;
            box-shadow: 0 0.4rem 1rem var(--shadow-color);

            &[data-has-major="true"]::after {
                background-color: #ffffff;
            }
        }
    }
}

// ==========================================================================
// CỘT PHẢI (DƯỚI): KHUNG BENTO SỰ KIỆN CÓ VÙNG CUỘN ĐỘC LẬP (54REM)
// ==========================================================================
.festival-bento-box {
    @extend %flexColumn;
    gap: 1.6rem;
    padding: 2.2rem 2.4rem;
    background-color: var(--body-bg);
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 2.6rem;
    box-shadow: 0 0.8rem 2.4rem var(--shadow-color);
    @extend %transTheme;

    @include abs.screen(sm) {
        padding: 1.6rem;
        border-radius: 2rem;
    }

    &__bar {
        @extend %flexBetween;
        padding-bottom: 1rem;
        border-bottom: 1px dashed var(--border-color);
    }

    &__hint {
        font-size: 1.3rem;
        font-weight: 600;
        color: var(--text-muted);
    }

    &__scroll-viewport {
        max-height: 54rem;
        overflow-y: auto;
        overflow-x: hidden;
        padding: 0.4rem 0.6rem 0.4rem 0.2rem;
        scroll-behavior: smooth;

        @include abs.screen(lg) {
            max-height: 50rem;
        }
    }

    &__empty {
        padding: 3.2rem 2rem;
        text-align: center;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px dashed var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 2rem;

        &[hidden] {
            display: none !important;
        }
    }

    &__empty-text {
        font-size: 1.45rem;
        font-weight: 600;
        color: var(--text-muted);
    }

    &__footer {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        align-items: center;
        gap: 1.2rem;
        padding-top: 0.4rem;
    }

    &__more-btn {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        gap: 0.8rem;
        width: 100%;
        max-width: 46rem;
        height: 4.4rem;
        padding: 0 2.2rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        font-size: 1.4rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-color);
        @extend %transInteractive;

        &[hidden] {
            display: none !important;
        }

        &:hover,
        &:focus-visible {
            background-color: var(--primary-color);
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: #ffffff;
            transform: translateY(-2px);
            box-shadow: 0 0.8rem 2rem var(--shadow-color);

            .festival-bento-box__more-arrow {
                filter: brightness(0) invert(1);
            }
        }

        &[aria-expanded="true"] .festival-bento-box__more-arrow {
            transform: rotate(180deg);
        }
    }

    &__more-arrow {
        width: 1.6rem;
        height: 1.6rem;
        transition: transform 0.25s ease;
    }

    &__subnote {
        @extend %flexBetween;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        width: 100%;
        gap: 1rem;
        padding-top: 1rem;
        border-top: 1px dashed var(--border-color);
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        color: var(--text-muted);
    }

    &__ext-link {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        gap: 0.4rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--primary-color);

        &:hover {
            text-decoration: underline;
        }
    }
}

.festival-bento-grid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr));
    grid-auto-rows: minmax(13.6rem, auto);
    grid-auto-flow: dense;
    gap: 1.4rem;

    @include abs.screen(md) {
        grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
    }

    @include abs.screen(sm) {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }
}

.festival-tile {
    position: relative;
    overflow: hidden;
    grid-column: span 1;
    grid-row: span 1;
    width: 100%;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    justify-content: space-between;
    gap: 1.2rem;
    padding: 1.6rem;
    background-color: var(--bg-surface);
    border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 2rem;
    text-align: left;
    color: inherit;
    cursor: pointer;
    @extend %transInteractive;

    &[hidden] {
        display: none !important;
    }

    &[data-fill-span="2"] {
        grid-column: span 2;

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            grid-column: span 1;
        }
    }

    &[data-fill-span="3"] {
        grid-column: span 3;

        @include abs.screen(md) {
            grid-column: span 2;
        }

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            grid-column: span 1;
        }
    }

    &:hover,
    &:focus-visible,
    &.is-active {
        background-color: var(--primary-soft-bg, var(--body-bg));
        border-color: var(--primary-color);
        transform: translateY(-2px);
        box-shadow: 0 0.8rem 2rem var(--shadow-color);
    }

    &.is-active {
        border-width: 2px;
    }

    &[data-major="true"],
    &--major {
        grid-column: span 2;
        grid-row: span 2;
        padding: 2.2rem;
        background-color: var(--primary-color);
        border-color: var(--primary-hover);
        color: #ffffff;
        box-shadow: 0 1rem 2.6rem var(--shadow-color);

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            grid-column: span 1;
            grid-row: span 1;
            padding: 1.8rem;
        }

        &:hover,
        &:focus-visible,
        &.is-active {
            background-color: var(--primary-hover);
            border-color: #ffffff;
            color: #ffffff;
            transform: translateY(-3px);

            .festival-tile__zodiac {
                opacity: 0.26;
                transform: scale(1.06) rotate(0deg);
            }
        }
    }

    &__head {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        @extend %flexBetween;
        gap: 0.8rem;
        width: 100%;
    }

    &__day-badge {
        padding: 0.35rem 1rem;
        border-radius: 1rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        font-size: 1.45rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--primary-color);
        font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;

        &--lunar {
            border-color: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.4);
            color: var(--color-danger);
        }
    }

    &__badge {
        padding: 0.25rem 0.9rem;
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        font-size: 1.15rem;
        font-weight: 800;

        &--solar {
            background-color: rgba(37, 99, 235, 0.12);
            color: #1d4ed8;
        }

        &--lunar {
            background-color: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.12);
            color: #b91c1c;
        }
    }

    &__body {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 0.35rem;
        width: 100%;
        margin-top: auto;
    }

    &__name {
        font-size: 1.55rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        line-height: 1.35;
        color: var(--text-color);
        @extend %textClamp2;
    }

    &__meta {
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        font-weight: 600;
        color: var(--text-muted);
    }

    &__major-tag {
        padding: 0.4rem 1.2rem;
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        background-color: #ffffff;
        color: var(--primary-active);
        font-size: 1.2rem;
        font-weight: 800;
    }

    &__type-pill {
        padding: 0.35rem 1.1rem;
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        background-color: var(--hero-box-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--hero-border-color);
        color: #ffffff;
        font-size: 1.2rem;
        font-weight: 700;
    }

    &__main {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 0.6rem;
        margin: auto 0;
    }

    &__title {
        font-size: clamp(1.9rem, 2.1vw, 2.4rem);
        font-weight: 800;
        line-height: 1.25;
        color: #ffffff;
    }

    &__desc {
        font-size: 1.35rem;
        line-height: 1.5;
        color: var(--hero-text-soft);
        @extend %textClamp3;
    }

    &__foot {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        @extend %flexBetween;
        align-items: flex-end;
        width: 100%;
        padding-top: 1.2rem;
        border-top: 1px solid var(--hero-border-color);
    }

    &__big-date {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 0.2rem;
    }

    &__big-num {
        font-size: clamp(3.2rem, 3.8vw, 4.2rem);
        font-weight: 800;
        line-height: 1;
        letter-spacing: -0.03em;
        color: #ffffff;
        font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
    }

    &__big-sub {
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--hero-text-soft);
    }

    &__cta {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        width: 3.6rem;
        height: 3.6rem;
        border-radius: 50%;
        background-color: var(--hero-chip-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--hero-border-color);
        font-size: 1.5rem;
        color: #ffffff;
    }

    &__zodiac {
        position: absolute;
        right: -0.6rem;
        bottom: -0.6rem;
        width: 14rem;
        height: 14rem;
        object-fit: contain;
        opacity: 0.16;
        filter: var(--zodiac-filter-active);
        transform: rotate(-8deg);
        pointer-events: none;
        z-index: 1;
        transition:
            transform 0.35s ease,
            opacity 0.35s ease;
    }
}
```

## layout folder

### index.scss

```scss
@forward "./header";
@forward "./footer";
@forward "./sidebar";
```

### header.scss

```scss
// 1. scss/layout/_header.scss
@use "../abstracts" as abs;

.header {
    @extend %stickyTop;
    top: abs.$header-top-offset;
    margin-top: abs.$header-top-offset;
    margin-bottom: abs.$gap-header-main;
    z-index: 50;
    height: abs.$header-height-pc;
    background-color: transparent;
    border-bottom: none;

    @include abs.screen(md) {
        height: abs.$header-height-mobile;
    }

    &__container {
        @extend %flexBetween;
        height: 100%;
        padding-left: 2.4rem;
        padding-right: 2.4rem;
        gap: 2rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 2.4rem;
        box-shadow: 0 0.4rem 1.6rem var(--shadow-color);
        @extend %transHeaderScroll;

        @include abs.screen(xl) {
            padding-left: 2rem;
            padding-right: 2rem;
        }

        @include abs.screen(md) {
            padding-left: 1.6rem;
            padding-right: 1.6rem;
            border-radius: 1.6rem;
            gap: 1.2rem;
        }

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            padding-left: 1.2rem !important;
            padding-right: 1.2rem !important;
            border-radius: 1.4rem;
            gap: 0.8rem;
        }
    }

    &--scrolled &__container {
        box-shadow: 0 1rem 2.8rem var(--shadow-color);
        border-color: var(--primary-color);
        backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
    }

    &__left,
    &__right {
        @extend %flexItemCenter;
        gap: 1.2rem;

        @include abs.screen(md) {
            gap: 1rem;
        }

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            gap: 0.6rem;
        }
    }

    &__lang-details,
    &__theme-details {
        position: relative;
    }

    &__lang-summary,
    &__theme-summary {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        gap: 0.6rem;
        height: 4.2rem;
        padding: 0 1.2rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 1.2rem;
        font-size: 1.4rem;
        font-weight: 600;
        cursor: pointer;
        user-select: none;
        @extend %transTheme;

        &:hover {
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
        }

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            height: 3.8rem;
            padding: 0 0.8rem;
        }
    }

    &__lang-arrow {
        width: 1.6rem;
        height: 1.6rem;
        @extend %transBurgerBar;
    }

    &__lang-details[open] &__lang-arrow {
        transform: rotate(180deg);
    }

    &__lang-list,
    &__theme-list {
        position: absolute;
        top: calc(100% + 1rem);
        right: 0;
        z-index: 60;
        min-width: 14rem;
        padding: 0.6rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 1.4rem;
        box-shadow: 0 1rem 2.5rem var(--shadow-color);
        @extend %animDropdownFadeIn;
    }

    &__lang-link,
    &__theme-btn {
        @extend %flexYCenterLeft;
        gap: 1rem;
        width: 100%;
        padding: 0.8rem 1.2rem;
        border-radius: 0.8rem;
        font-size: 1.4rem;
        font-weight: 500;
        color: var(--text-color);
        @extend %transTheme;

        &:hover {
            background-color: var(--bg-surface);
            color: var(--primary-color);
        }
    }

    &__lang-link--active,
    &__theme-btn.is-active {
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        color: var(--primary-color);
        font-weight: 700;
    }

    &__burger {
        display: none;
        flex-direction: column;
        justify-content: center;
        align-items: center;
        gap: 0.5rem;
        width: 4.4rem;
        height: 4.4rem;
        border-radius: 1.2rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        @extend %transTheme;

        @include abs.screen(md) {
            display: inline-flex;
        }

        &-bar {
            width: 2rem;
            height: 0.2rem;
            background-color: var(--text-color);
            border-radius: 0.2rem;
            @extend %transBurgerBar;
        }

        &.is-active &-bar:nth-child(1) {
            transform: translateY(0.7rem) rotate(45deg);
        }
        &.is-active &-bar:nth-child(2) {
            opacity: 0;
        }
        &.is-active &-bar:nth-child(3) {
            transform: translateY(-0.7rem) rotate(-45deg);
        }
    }
}
```

### footer.scss

```scss
// scss/layout/_footer.scss
@use "../abstracts" as abs;

.footer {
    margin-top: abs.$gap-section-default;
    padding-bottom: 4rem;
    color: var(--text-color);

    &__bento-row {
        --grid-gutter-y: 2.4rem;
    }

    // Khung chuẩn cho mọi đảo Bento trong Footer
    &__card {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        justify-content: space-between;
        height: 100%;
        padding: 2.8rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 2.4rem;
        box-shadow: 0 0.4rem 1.6rem var(--shadow-color);
        @extend %transTheme;

        &:hover {
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
        }

        @include abs.screen(md) {
            padding: 2rem;
            border-radius: 1.8rem;
        }

        &--links {
            @extend %gridCols2;
            gap: 2rem;

            @include abs.screen(sm) {
                grid-template-columns: 1fr;
            }
        }
    }

    &__brand-head {
        @extend %flexBetween;
        gap: 1.2rem;
        margin-bottom: 1.4rem;
    }

    &__logo-link {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        gap: 1rem;
        font-size: 1.8rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--text-color);
    }

    &__logo-img {
        height: 3.6rem;
        width: auto;
    }

    &__version-badge {
        padding: 0.4rem 1rem;
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        font-size: 1.2rem;
        font-weight: 600;
        color: var(--primary-color);
        @extend %textNoWrap;
    }

    &__desc,
    &__sub-desc {
        font-size: 1.4rem;
        line-height: 1.6;
        color: var(--text-muted);
        margin-bottom: 1.8rem;
    }

    // Cụm chọn nhanh Ngũ Hành trong Footer
    &__elements {
        @extend %flexItemCenter;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        gap: 1rem;
        padding: 1rem 1.2rem;
        margin-bottom: 1.6rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border-radius: 1.4rem;
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);

        &-label {
            font-size: 1.3rem;
            font-weight: 600;
            color: var(--text-muted);
        }

        &-list {
            @extend %flexWrapGap;
        }
    }

    &__element-dot {
        padding: 0.4rem 1rem;
        border-radius: 0.8rem;
        font-size: 1.2rem;
        font-weight: 600;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        color: var(--text-color);
        @extend %transInteractive;

        &:hover,
        &.is-active {
            background-color: var(--primary-color);
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: #ffffff;
            transform: translateY(-1px);
        }
    }

    &__contact {
        @extend %flexBetween;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        gap: 1rem;
        font-style: normal;
        font-size: 1.3rem;
        color: var(--text-muted);
        padding-top: 1.4rem;
        border-top: 1px dashed var(--border-color);

        &-link {
            font-weight: 600;
            color: var(--primary-color);

            &:hover {
                text-decoration: underline;
            }
        }
    }

    // Cột điều hướng
    &__heading {
        font-size: 1.5rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--text-color);
        margin-bottom: 1.4rem;
    }

    &__list {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 1rem;
    }

    &__link {
        font-size: 1.4rem;
        color: var(--text-muted);
        @extend %transTheme;

        &:hover {
            color: var(--primary-color);
        }

        &--btn {
            font-weight: 600;
            color: var(--primary-color);
        }
    }

    // Form nhận tin & Mạng xã hội
    &__sr-only {
        @extend %srOnly;
    }

    &__input-group {
        @extend %flexItemCenter;
        gap: 0.8rem;
        padding: 0.4rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 1.4rem;
        margin-bottom: 1.2rem;

        &:focus-within {
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
        }
    }

    &__input {
        @extend %flexItemFluid;
        padding: 0.8rem 1rem;
        font-size: 1.3rem;
        color: var(--text-color);
    }

    &__submit-btn {
        height: 3.6rem;
        padding: 0 1.4rem;
        background-color: var(--primary-color);
        color: #ffffff;
        border-radius: 1rem;
        font-size: 1.3rem;

        &:hover {
            background-color: var(--primary-hover);
        }
    }

    &__feedback {
        font-size: 1.2rem;
        font-weight: 600;
        color: var(--color-success);
        margin-bottom: 1.2rem;
    }

    &__social {
        @extend %flexBetween;
        padding-top: 1.4rem;
        border-top: 1px dashed var(--border-color);

        &-label {
            font-size: 1.3rem;
            color: var(--text-muted);
        }

        &-list {
            @extend %flexItemCenter;
            gap: 0.8rem;
        }

        &-link {
            @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
            width: 3.6rem;
            height: 3.6rem;
            border-radius: 1rem;
            background-color: var(--bg-surface);
            border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
            color: var(--text-color);
            @extend %transInteractive;

            &:hover {
                background-color: var(--primary-color);
                border-color: var(--primary-color);
                color: #ffffff;
                transform: translateY(-2px);
            }
        }
    }

    // Thanh Bento Bar dưới cùng
    &__bottom-bar {
        @extend %flexBetween;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        gap: 1.6rem;
        margin-top: 2.4rem;
        padding: 1.6rem 2.4rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 2rem;
        box-shadow: 0 0.4rem 1.6rem var(--shadow-color);
        @extend %transTheme;

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            padding: 1.4rem 1.6rem;
            border-radius: 1.6rem;
        }
    }

    &__copyright {
        font-size: 1.3rem;
        color: var(--text-muted);
    }

    &__top-btn {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        gap: 0.6rem;
        padding: 0.8rem 1.4rem;
        border-radius: 1.2rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        font-size: 1.3rem;
        font-weight: 600;
        color: var(--text-color);
        @extend %transInteractive;

        &:hover {
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: var(--primary-color);
            transform: translateY(-2px);
        }
    }
}
```

### sidebar.scss

```scss
@use "../abstracts" as abs;

#sidebar-root {
    display: contents;
}

.sidebar {
    position: fixed;
    left: 2rem;
    top: 50%;
    transform: translateY(-50%);
    z-index: 40;
    padding: 1rem 0.8rem;
    background-color: var(--body-bg);
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 2rem;
    box-shadow: 0 0.8rem 2.4rem var(--shadow-color);
    @extend %transSidebarDrawer;

    &__list {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 0.8rem;
    }

    &__link {
        position: relative;
        @extend %flexCenter;
        width: 4.6rem;
        height: 4.6rem;
        border-radius: 1.2rem;
        color: var(--text-muted);
        @extend %transTheme;

        &:hover,
        &:focus-visible {
            background-color: var(--bg-surface);
            color: var(--primary-color);
        }

        &--active {
            background-color: var(--bg-surface);
            color: var(--primary-color);
            box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 0.2rem var(--primary-color);
        }
    }

    &__tooltip {
        @extend %absCenterY;
        left: calc(100% + 1.4rem);
        transform: translate(-0.8rem, -50%);
        padding: 0.6rem 1.2rem;
        background-color: var(--tooltip-bg, var(--bg-surface));
        color: var(--tooltip-text, var(--text-color));
        border: 1.5px solid var(--tooltip-border, var(--primary-color));
        font-size: 1.3rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        border-radius: 1rem;
        @extend %textNoWrap;
        box-shadow: 0 0.6rem 1.6rem var(--shadow-color);
        opacity: 0;
        visibility: hidden;
        pointer-events: none;
        @extend %transTooltip;

        &::before {
            @extend %absPseudo;
            right: 100%;
            top: 50%;
            transform: translateY(-50%);
            border-width: 0.55rem;
            border-style: solid;
            border-color: transparent var(--tooltip-border, var(--primary-color)) transparent transparent;
        }
    }

    &__link:hover &__tooltip,
    &__link:focus-visible &__tooltip {
        opacity: 1;
        visibility: visible;
        transform: translate(0, -50%);
    }

    @include abs.screen(md) {
        top: calc(#{abs.$header-top-offset} + #{abs.$header-height-mobile} + 1.5rem);
        left: 1.6rem;
        bottom: 1.6rem;
        width: 26rem;
        transform: translateX(calc(-100% - 3rem));
        border-radius: 2rem;
        padding: 1.6rem;
        z-index: 55;
        overflow-y: auto;

        &.is-open {
            transform: translateX(0);
        }

        &__link {
            justify-content: flex-start;
            width: 100%;
            height: 4.8rem;
            padding: 0 1.6rem;
            gap: 1.4rem;
        }

        &__tooltip {
            position: static;
            transform: none;
            opacity: 1;
            visibility: visible;
            background-color: transparent;
            border: none;
            color: inherit;
            padding: 0;
            font-size: 1.5rem;
            box-shadow: none;
            pointer-events: auto;

            &::before {
                display: none;
            }
        }
    }
}

.sidebar-backdrop {
    @extend %fixedCover;
    @extend %transBackdrop;
    display: none;
    background-color: var(--overlay-bg);
    backdrop-filter: blur(2px);
    z-index: 45;
    opacity: 0;
    visibility: hidden;

    @include abs.screen(md) {
        display: block;

        &.is-active {
            opacity: 1;
            visibility: visible;
        }
    }
}
```

### home.scss

```scss
// scss/pages/_home.scss
@use "../abstracts" as abs;

.home-page {
    padding-bottom: abs.$gap-section-default;

    &__row {
        --grid-gutter-y: 2.4rem;
        align-items: flex-start;
    }

    &__left {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 1.6rem;
    }

    &__right {
        position: sticky;
        top: calc(#{abs.$header-top-offset} + #{abs.$header-height-pc} + 2rem);

        @include abs.screen(lg) {
            position: static;
        }
    }
}
```

### clock.scss

```scss
// scss/pages/_clock.scss
@use "../abstracts" as abs;

.clock-page {
    padding-bottom: abs.$gap-section-default;

    &__sr-only {
        @extend %srOnly;
    }

    &__wrapper {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 2.4rem;
    }

    &__mid-row {
        --grid-gutter-y: 2.4rem;
        align-items: stretch;
    }
}
```

### day.scss

```scss
// scss/pages/_day.scss
@use "../abstracts" as abs;

.day-page {
    padding-bottom: abs.$gap-section-default;

    // 1. Thanh điều hướng Ngày trước / Hôm nay / Ngày sau
    &__toolbar {
        @extend %flexBetween;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        gap: 1.4rem;
        margin-bottom: 2.4rem;
        padding: 1.4rem 2rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 2.2rem;
        box-shadow: 0 0.4rem 1.6rem var(--shadow-color);
        @extend %transTheme;

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            padding: 1.2rem;
            border-radius: 1.8rem;
        }
    }

    &__nav-btn {
        height: 4.2rem;
        padding: 0 1.8rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 1.2rem;
        font-size: 1.4rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--text-color);

        &:hover {
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: var(--primary-color);
            transform: translateY(-1px);
        }
    }

    &__center-group {
        @extend %flexItemCenter;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        justify-content: center;
        gap: 1.2rem;
    }

    &__today-btn {
        height: 4rem;
        padding: 0 1.6rem;
        background-color: var(--primary-color);
        color: #ffffff;
        border-radius: 1.2rem;
        font-size: 1.35rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        box-shadow: 0 0.4rem 1.2rem var(--shadow-color);

        &:hover {
            background-color: var(--primary-hover);
        }
    }

    &__current-heading {
        font-size: 2rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-color);

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            font-size: 1.6rem;
        }
    }

    // ==========================================================================
    // 2. HỆ THỐNG 3 CẶP HÀNG NGANG KHÓA CHẶT 2 NGÃ TƯ (CHỐNG LỆCH VỚI MỌI ĐỘ DÀI CONTENT)
    // ==========================================================================
    &__pairs-wrapper {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 2rem;
    }

    &__pair-row {
        --grid-gutter-y: 2rem;
        align-items: stretch;
    }

    &__pair-col {
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        gap: 2rem;
    }

    &__fill-card {
        flex: 1;
        height: 100%;
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        justify-content: space-between;
    }

    &__hours-boxes {
        align-items: stretch;

        .fengshui-hours__box {
            display: flex;
            flex-direction: column;
            justify-content: space-between;
            height: 100%;
        }

        .fengshui-hours__box--good .day-page__hours-icon {
            @extend %animHoangDaoSun;
        }

        .fengshui-hours__box--bad .day-page__hours-icon {
            @extend %animHacDaoMoon;
        }
    }

    &__hours-icon {
        width: 1.9rem;
        height: 1.9rem;
        object-fit: contain;
        filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
        @extend %flexItemFixed;
        will-change: transform;
    }

    &__actions-card {
        flex: 1;
        height: 100%;
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        justify-content: flex-start;
        gap: 1.6rem;
    }

    &__actions-grid {
        flex: 1;
        align-items: stretch;
        padding-bottom: 0;
        border-bottom: none;

        .fengshui-actions__box {
            height: 100%;
            justify-content: flex-start;
        }

        .fengshui-actions__list {
            align-content: flex-start;
        }
    }

    &__quote-box {
        margin-top: auto;
        padding: 1.4rem 1.8rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px dashed var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 1.6rem;
    }

    &__sun-card {
        justify-content: flex-start;
        gap: 1.5rem;
    }
}

.day-bento-card {
    position: relative;
    overflow: visible;
    @extend %flexColumn;
    gap: 1.5rem;
    padding: 2.2rem 2.4rem;
    background-color: var(--body-bg);
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 2.4rem;
    box-shadow: 0 0.4rem 1.6rem var(--shadow-color);
    @extend %transTheme;

    &--grow {
        flex: 1;
        justify-content: space-between;
    }

    @include abs.screen(sm) {
        padding: 1.6rem;
        border-radius: 1.8rem;
    }

    &__head {
        @extend %flexBetween;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        gap: 1rem;
        padding-bottom: 1.2rem;
        border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    }

    &__title {
        font-size: 1.85rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-color);
    }

    &__sub {
        margin-top: 0.3rem;
        font-size: 1.3rem;
        font-weight: 600;
        color: var(--text-muted);
    }

    &__tag,
    &__badge {
        padding: 0.4rem 1.2rem;
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        font-size: 1.2rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        @extend %textNoWrap;
    }

    &__tag {
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        color: var(--primary-color);
    }

    &__badge {
        background-color: var(--primary-color);
        color: #ffffff;
    }
}

// ==========================================================================
// COMPONENT DÙNG CHUNG: BENTO TOOLTIP 3 TẦNG (KẾ THỪA %transBentoTooltip)
// ==========================================================================
.has-bento-tooltip {
    position: relative;
    overflow: visible;

    .bento-tooltip {
        position: absolute;
        bottom: calc(100% + 1.1rem);
        left: 50%;
        transform: translateX(-50%) translateY(0.6rem);
        z-index: 80;
        width: 31rem;
        padding: 1.3rem 1.5rem;
        background-color: var(--tooltip-bg, var(--body-bg));
        color: var(--tooltip-text, var(--text-color));
        border: 1.5px solid var(--tooltip-border, var(--primary-color));
        border-radius: 1.6rem;
        box-shadow:
            0 1.4rem 3rem var(--shadow-color),
            0 0 0 3px var(--body-bg);
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        align-items: flex-start;
        gap: 0.65rem;
        text-align: left;
        white-space: normal;
        opacity: 0;
        visibility: hidden;
        pointer-events: none;
        @extend %transBentoTooltip;

        &::after {
            content: "";
            position: absolute;
            top: 100%;
            left: 50%;
            transform: translateX(-50%);
            border-width: 0.65rem;
            border-style: solid;
            border-color: var(--tooltip-border, var(--primary-color)) transparent transparent transparent;
        }

        &__title {
            display: block;
            font-size: 1.35rem;
            font-weight: 800;
            line-height: 1.3;
            color: var(--primary-color);
        }

        &__desc {
            display: block;
            font-size: 1.25rem;
            font-weight: 600;
            line-height: 1.5;
            color: var(--tooltip-text, var(--text-color));
        }

        &__tag {
            display: inline-flex;
            align-items: center;
            gap: 0.45rem;
            margin-top: 0.2rem;
            padding: 0.35rem 1.1rem;
            background-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: #ffffff;
            border-radius: 99.9rem;
            font-size: 1.15rem;
            font-weight: 800;
            line-height: 1.2;
            box-shadow: 0 0.3rem 0.8rem var(--shadow-color);
        }
    }

    @media (hover: hover) {
        &:hover,
        &:focus-visible {
            z-index: 45;

            > .bento-tooltip {
                opacity: 1;
                visibility: visible;
                transform: translateX(-50%) translateY(0);
                transition-delay: 0.14s;
            }
        }
    }
}

// ==========================================================================
// 1. HƯỚNG XUẤT HÀNH (3 CARD ĐỒNG ĐỀU + ICON 4.2REM + WATERMARK + TOOLTIP TAG)
// ==========================================================================
.day-directions-grid {
    flex: 1;
    @extend %gridCols3;
    align-items: stretch;
    gap: 1.4rem;

    @include abs.screen(sm) {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }
}

.day-dir-box {
    position: relative;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    justify-content: space-between;
    height: 100%;
    min-height: 12.8rem;
    gap: 0.8rem;
    padding: 1.5rem 1.6rem;
    border-radius: 1.8rem;
    border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
    text-decoration: none;
    color: inherit;
    cursor: pointer;
    @extend %transInteractive;

    &__card-bg {
        @extend %absCover;
        border-radius: inherit;
        overflow: hidden;
        pointer-events: none;
        z-index: 1;
    }

    &__watermark {
        position: absolute;
        right: -0.6rem;
        bottom: -0.6rem;
        width: 6.2rem;
        height: 6.2rem;
        object-fit: contain;
        opacity: 0.12;
        filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
        transform: rotate(-10deg);
        @extend %transWatermark;
    }

    &:hover {
        transform: translateY(-2px);
        box-shadow: 0 0.8rem 2rem var(--shadow-color);

        .day-dir-box__watermark {
            opacity: 0.25;
            transform: scale(1.08) rotate(0deg);
        }
    }

    &__top {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        @extend %flexBetween;
        gap: 0.8rem;
    }

    &__icon-wrap {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        @extend %flexItemFixed;
        width: 4.2rem;
        height: 4.2rem;
        border-radius: 1.2rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        box-shadow: 0 0.4rem 1rem var(--shadow-color);

        img {
            width: 2.6rem;
            height: 2.6rem;
            object-fit: contain;
            filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
        }
    }

    &__body {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 0.25rem;
        margin-top: auto;
        padding-right: 2.4rem;
    }

    &--hy {
        background-color: rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.08);
        border-color: rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.35);

        .day-dir-box__label,
        .day-dir-box__value {
            color: #1d4ed8;
        }
    }

    &--tai {
        background-color: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.1);
        border-color: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.38);

        .day-dir-box__label,
        .day-dir-box__value {
            color: #047857;
        }
    }

    &--hac {
        background-color: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.08);
        border-color: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.35);

        .day-dir-box__label,
        .day-dir-box__value {
            color: #b91c1c;
        }
    }

    &__label {
        font-size: 1.2rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        text-transform: uppercase;
        letter-spacing: 0.03em;
    }

    &__value {
        font-size: 2.1rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        line-height: 1.2;
    }

    &__sub {
        font-size: 1.2rem;
        font-weight: 600;
        color: var(--text-muted);
    }
}

// ==========================================================================
// 2. THÔNG TIN TIẾT KHÍ (24 ICON WEATHER + 6 NHÓM ANIMATION THỜI TIẾT PHƯƠNG ÁN 1)
// ==========================================================================
.day-solarterm-grid {
    flex: 1;
    @extend %gridCols3;
    align-items: stretch;
    gap: 1.4rem;

    @include abs.screen(sm) {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }
}

.day-solarterm-box {
    position: relative;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    justify-content: space-between;
    height: 100%;
    min-height: 11.4rem;
    gap: 0.8rem;
    padding: 1.5rem 1.6rem;
    background-color: var(--bg-surface);
    border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 1.8rem;
    text-decoration: none;
    color: inherit;
    cursor: pointer;
    @extend %transInteractive;

    &--primary {
        background-color: var(--primary-soft-bg, var(--bg-surface));
        border-color: var(--primary-color);
    }

    &__card-bg {
        @extend %absCover;
        border-radius: inherit;
        overflow: hidden;
        pointer-events: none;
        z-index: 1;
    }

    &__watermark {
        position: absolute;
        right: -0.8rem;
        bottom: -0.8rem;
        width: 5.2rem;
        height: 5.2rem;
        object-fit: contain;
        opacity: 0.11;
        filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
        transform: rotate(-8deg);
        @extend %transWatermark;
    }

    &:hover {
        border-color: var(--primary-color);
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        transform: translateY(-2px);
        box-shadow: 0 0.8rem 1.8rem var(--shadow-color);

        .day-solarterm-box__watermark {
            opacity: 0.24;
            transform: scale(1.08) rotate(0deg);
        }
    }

    &__top {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        @extend %flexBetween;
        gap: 0.8rem;
    }

    &__icon-wrap {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        @extend %flexItemFixed;
        width: 3.8rem;
        height: 3.8rem;
        border-radius: 1.1rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);

        img {
            width: 2.4rem;
            height: 2.4rem;
            object-fit: contain;
            filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
            will-change: transform;
        }
    }

    // PHƯƠNG ÁN 1: Ô số 1 (Tiết khí hiện tại) chuyển động liên tục, 2 ô phụ chuyển động khi Hover
    &--primary[data-term-anim="wind"] &__icon-wrap img,
    &:hover[data-term-anim="wind"] &__icon-wrap img {
        @extend %animTermWind;
    }

    &--primary[data-term-anim="rain"] &__icon-wrap img,
    &:hover[data-term-anim="rain"] &__icon-wrap img {
        @extend %animTermRain;
    }

    &--primary[data-term-anim="sun"] &__icon-wrap img,
    &:hover[data-term-anim="sun"] &__icon-wrap img {
        @extend %animTermSun;
    }

    &--primary[data-term-anim="thunder"] &__icon-wrap img,
    &:hover[data-term-anim="thunder"] &__icon-wrap img {
        @extend %animTermThunder;
    }

    &--primary[data-term-anim="sprout"] &__icon-wrap img,
    &:hover[data-term-anim="sprout"] &__icon-wrap img {
        @extend %animTermSprout;
    }

    &--primary[data-term-anim="snow"] &__icon-wrap img,
    &:hover[data-term-anim="snow"] &__icon-wrap img {
        @extend %animTermSnow;
    }

    &__body {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 0.2rem;
        margin-top: auto;
        padding-right: 2.8rem;
    }

    &__label {
        font-size: 1.2rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--text-muted);
    }

    &__val {
        font-size: 1.75rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-color);
        font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
    }

    &__sub {
        font-size: 1.15rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--primary-color);
    }
}

// ==========================================================================
// 3. KHỔNG MINH LỤC DIỆU (12 Ô TỰ GIÃN + KẾ THỪA 6 %animLucDieu* TỪ ABSTRACTS)
// ==========================================================================
.day-lucdieu-grid {
    flex: 1;
    @extend %gridCols4;
    grid-auto-rows: 1fr;
    align-items: stretch;
    gap: 1.1rem;

    @include abs.screen(md) {
        grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
    }

    @include abs.screen(sm) {
        grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);
    }
}

.day-lucdieu-cell {
    position: relative;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    justify-content: space-between;
    height: 100%;
    min-height: 10.6rem;
    gap: 0.5rem;
    padding: 1.25rem 1.35rem;
    border-radius: 1.6rem;
    border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
    text-decoration: none;
    color: inherit;
    cursor: pointer;
    @extend %transInteractive;

    &__card-bg {
        @extend %absCover;
        border-radius: inherit;
        overflow: hidden;
        pointer-events: none;
        z-index: 1;
    }

    &__watermark {
        position: absolute;
        right: -0.4rem;
        bottom: -0.4rem;
        width: 4.6rem;
        height: 4.6rem;
        object-fit: contain;
        opacity: 0.1;
        filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
        transform: rotate(-8deg);
        @extend %transWatermark;
    }

    &:hover {
        transform: translateY(-2px);
        box-shadow: 0 0.8rem 1.8rem var(--shadow-color);

        .day-lucdieu-cell__watermark {
            opacity: 0.22;
            transform: scale(1.08) rotate(0deg);
        }
    }

    &--good {
        background-color: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.11);
        border-color: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.38);

        .day-lucdieu-cell__name {
            color: #065f46;
        }
    }

    &--bad {
        background-color: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.08);
        border-color: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.32);

        .day-lucdieu-cell__name {
            color: #991b1b;
        }
    }

    &__head {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        @extend %flexBetween;
        gap: 0.6rem;
    }

    &__icon {
        width: 2.3rem;
        height: 2.3rem;
        object-fit: contain;
        filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
        @extend %flexItemFixed;
        will-change: transform;
    }

    &[data-lucdieu-slug="dai-an"] &__icon {
        @extend %animLucDieuDaiAn;
    }

    &[data-lucdieu-slug="luu-nien"] &__icon {
        @extend %animLucDieuLuuNien;
    }

    &[data-lucdieu-slug="toc-hy"] &__icon {
        @extend %animLucDieuTocHy;
    }

    &[data-lucdieu-slug="xich-khau"] &__icon {
        @extend %animLucDieuXichKhau;
    }

    &[data-lucdieu-slug="tieu-cat"] &__icon {
        @extend %animLucDieuTieuCat;
    }

    &[data-lucdieu-slug="khong-vong"] &__icon {
        @extend %animLucDieuKhongVong;
    }

    &__name {
        font-size: 1.45rem;
        font-weight: 800;
    }

    &__time {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        font-size: 1.2rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--text-color);
    }

    &__harmony {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        display: inline-block;
        width: fit-content;
        padding: 0.2rem 0.7rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        font-size: 1.1rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--text-muted);

        strong {
            color: var(--primary-color);
            font-weight: 800;
        }
    }
}

// ==========================================================================
// 4. THÔNG TIN MẶT TRỜI (4 ICON WEATHER 20X20PX + TỰ DÀN ĐỀU CHIỀU CAO 4 MIỀN)
// ==========================================================================
.day-sun-summary {
    @extend %gridCols2;
    gap: 1.2rem;

    @include abs.screen(sm) {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }

    &__pill {
        @extend %flexBetween;
        padding: 1.15rem 1.6rem;
        background-color: var(--primary-soft-bg, rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.1));
        border: 1px solid var(--primary-color);
        border-radius: 1.4rem;
        font-size: 1.35rem;

        span {
            font-weight: 600;
            color: var(--text-muted);
        }

        strong {
            font-weight: 800;
            color: var(--text-color);
        }
    }
}

.day-sun-table {
    flex: 1;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 1.6rem;
    overflow: hidden;

    &__head,
    &__row {
        display: grid;
        grid-template-columns: 0.85fr 2.2fr 1.05fr 1.05fr;
        align-items: center;
        gap: 1rem;
        padding: 1.25rem 1.6rem;

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            grid-template-columns: 0.8fr 1.5fr 1fr 1fr;
            padding: 1rem;
            font-size: 1.2rem;
        }
    }

    &__head {
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        font-size: 1.2rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-muted);
        text-transform: uppercase;
    }

    &__th {
        @extend %flexItemCenter;
        gap: 0.55rem;
    }

    &__th-icon {
        width: 1.8rem;
        height: 1.8rem;
        object-fit: contain;
        filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
        @extend %flexItemFixed;
    }

    .day-sun-rows-wrap {
        flex: 1;
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
    }

    &__row {
        flex: 1;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        font-size: 1.35rem;

        &:last-child {
            border-bottom: none;
        }
    }

    &__region {
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--primary-color);
    }

    &__place {
        font-weight: 600;
        color: var(--text-color);
    }

    &__time {
        @extend %flexItemCenter;
        gap: 0.6rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
        color: var(--text-color);
    }

    &__time-icon {
        width: 2rem;
        height: 2rem;
        object-fit: contain;
        filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
        @extend %flexItemFixed;
    }
}

// ==========================================================================
// 5. TẦNG DƯỚI (1 CỘT LỚN FULL-WIDTH 12 DÒNG): ĐỒNG BỘ ANIMATION LỤC DIỆU & ĐẠO
// ==========================================================================
.day-hourly-section {
    margin-top: 2.8rem;

    &__header {
        @extend %flexBetween;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        gap: 1.4rem;
        padding-bottom: 1.4rem;
        border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    }

    &__filters {
        @extend %flexWrapGap;
    }

    &__filter-btn {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        gap: 0.7rem;
        height: 4rem;
        padding: 0 1.6rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        font-size: 1.35rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--text-color);
        @extend %transInteractive;

        &[data-day-hour-filter="good"] .day-hourly-section__dao-icon {
            @extend %animHoangDaoSun;
        }

        &[data-day-hour-filter="bad"] .day-hourly-section__dao-icon {
            @extend %animHacDaoMoon;
        }

        &:hover,
        &.is-active {
            background-color: var(--primary-color);
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: #ffffff;

            .day-hourly-section__dao-icon {
                filter: brightness(0) invert(1);
            }
        }
    }

    &__dao-icon {
        width: 1.8rem;
        height: 1.8rem;
        object-fit: contain;
        filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
        @extend %flexItemFixed;
        will-change: transform;
    }
}

.day-hourly-table {
    @extend %flexColumn;
    gap: 1.2rem;

    &__head {
        display: grid;
        grid-template-columns: 1.15fr 1.45fr 1.25fr 1.15fr 1.35fr;
        align-items: center;
        gap: 1.6rem;
        padding: 1.2rem 2.2rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 1.6rem;
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-muted);
        text-transform: uppercase;
        letter-spacing: 0.03em;

        span:nth-child(4) {
            text-align: center;
        }

        @include abs.screen(lg) {
            display: none;
        }
    }

    &__list {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 1.4rem;
    }
}

.day-hour-row {
    position: relative;
    overflow: visible;
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: 1.15fr 1.45fr 1.25fr 1.15fr 1.35fr;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 1.6rem;
    min-height: 10.8rem;
    padding: 1.6rem 2.2rem;
    background-color: var(--bg-surface);
    border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 2.2rem;
    @extend %transInteractive;

    &:hover {
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border-color: var(--primary-color);
        box-shadow: 0 1rem 2.6rem var(--shadow-color);
        z-index: 20;
    }

    &.is-dimmed {
        opacity: 0.22;
        transform: scale(0.99);
    }

    @include abs.screen(lg) {
        grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);
        padding: 1.6rem;
    }

    @include abs.screen(sm) {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }

    &__col-link {
        position: relative;
        padding: 0.8rem 1rem;
        border-radius: 1.4rem;
        text-decoration: none;
        color: inherit;
        cursor: pointer;
        @extend %transInteractive;

        &:hover,
        &:focus-visible {
            background-color: var(--tooltip-bg, var(--bg-surface));
            box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px var(--tooltip-border, var(--primary-color));
            z-index: 30;
        }
    }

    &__col-time {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 0.35rem;

        strong {
            font-size: 1.8rem;
            font-weight: 800;
            color: var(--text-color);
            @extend %transTheme;
        }

        span:not(.bento-tooltip):not(.bento-tooltip__desc):not(.bento-tooltip__tag) {
            display: inline-block;
            width: fit-content;
            padding: 0.25rem 0.9rem;
            background-color: var(--body-bg);
            border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
            border-radius: 99.9rem;
            font-size: 1.25rem;
            font-weight: 700;
            color: var(--primary-color);
            font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
        }

        &:hover > strong {
            color: var(--primary-color);
        }
    }

    &__col-napam {
        @extend %flexItemCenter;
        gap: 1.2rem;
    }

    &__napam-icon-box {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        @extend %flexItemFixed;
        width: 4.6rem;
        height: 4.6rem;
        border-radius: 1.4rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
        box-shadow: 0 0.4rem 1rem var(--shadow-color);
        @extend %transTheme;

        img {
            width: 2.6rem;
            height: 2.6rem;
            object-fit: contain;
        }
    }

    &__napam-info {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 0.2rem;

        strong {
            font-size: 1.55rem;
            font-weight: 800;
            color: var(--text-color);
        }

        small {
            font-size: 1.2rem;
            font-weight: 600;
            color: var(--text-muted);
        }
    }

    // Cột 3: Đồng bộ 100% 6 Animation Khổng Minh Lục Diệu & 2 Animation Hoàng/Hắc Đạo
    &__col-lucdieu {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        align-items: flex-start;
        gap: 0.45rem;
    }

    &__badge {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        gap: 0.55rem;
        padding: 0.4rem 1.2rem;
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        font-size: 1.35rem;
        font-weight: 800;

        img {
            width: 1.7rem;
            height: 1.7rem;
            object-fit: contain;
            filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
            will-change: transform;
        }

        &[data-lucdieu-slug="dai-an"] img {
            @extend %animLucDieuDaiAn;
        }

        &[data-lucdieu-slug="luu-nien"] img {
            @extend %animLucDieuLuuNien;
        }

        &[data-lucdieu-slug="toc-hy"] img {
            @extend %animLucDieuTocHy;
        }

        &[data-lucdieu-slug="xich-khau"] img {
            @extend %animLucDieuXichKhau;
        }

        &[data-lucdieu-slug="tieu-cat"] img {
            @extend %animLucDieuTieuCat;
        }

        &[data-lucdieu-slug="khong-vong"] img {
            @extend %animLucDieuKhongVong;
        }

        &--good {
            background-color: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.15);
            border: 1px solid rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.35);
            color: #047857;
        }

        &--bad {
            background-color: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.12);
            border: 1px solid rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.32);
            color: #b91c1c;
        }
    }

    &__dao-sub {
        @extend %flexItemCenter;
        gap: 0.5rem;
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--text-muted);
    }

    &__dao-icon {
        width: 1.8rem;
        height: 1.8rem;
        object-fit: contain;
        filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
        @extend %flexItemFixed;
        will-change: transform;

        &[data-dao-type="good"] {
            @extend %animHoangDaoSun;
        }

        &[data-dao-type="bad"] {
            @extend %animHacDaoMoon;
        }
    }

    &__col-wuxing {
        @extend %flexCenter;
    }

    &__col-bagua {
        @extend %flexItemCenter;
        justify-content: flex-start;
        gap: 1.2rem;
    }

    &__bagua-ring {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        @extend %flexItemFixed;
        width: 5.6rem;
        height: 5.6rem;
        border-radius: 50%;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 2px dashed var(--primary-color);
        padding: 0.8rem;
        box-shadow: 0 0.6rem 1.4rem var(--shadow-color);
        @extend %transInteractive;

        img {
            width: 100%;
            height: 100%;
            object-fit: contain;
            filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
        }
    }

    &__col-bagua:hover &__bagua-ring {
        transform: scale(1.08) rotate(6deg);
        border-style: solid;
    }

    &__bagua-text {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 0.25rem;

        strong {
            font-size: 1.55rem;
            font-weight: 800;
            color: var(--text-color);
        }

        small {
            font-size: 1.25rem;
            color: var(--primary-color);
            font-weight: 700;
        }
    }
}

// VÒNG TRÒN SVG NGŨ HÀNH ĐỘNG (KẾ THỪA %animWuXingSpin, %animWuXingPulse & %transWuXingNode)
.wuxing-mini {
    width: 9.2rem;
    height: 9.2rem;
    overflow: visible;
    @extend %flexItemFixed;

    &--lg {
        width: 14rem;
        height: 14rem;
    }

    &__cycle {
        fill: none;
        stroke: #10b981;
        stroke-width: 1.8;
        stroke-dasharray: 6 4;
        opacity: 0.65;
        transform-origin: 60px 60px;
        @extend %animWuXingSpin;
    }

    &__star {
        fill: none;
        stroke: #ef4444;
        stroke-width: 1.3;
        opacity: 0.45;
    }

    &__node {
        @extend %transWuXingNode;

        text {
            font-size: 10.5px;
            font-weight: 800;
            text-anchor: middle;
            dominant-baseline: central;
            user-select: none;
        }

        &.is-dimmed {
            opacity: 0.18;
        }

        &.is-active {
            opacity: 1;
            filter: drop-shadow(0 3px 6px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.22));
            @extend %animWuXingPulse;
        }

        &.is-hour-element {
            circle {
                stroke: #ffffff;
                stroke-width: 2;
            }
        }

        &--fire {
            transform-origin: 60px 21px;
            circle {
                fill: #ef4444;
            }
            text {
                fill: #ffffff;
            }
        }

        &--earth {
            transform-origin: 97px 48px;
            circle {
                fill: #f59e0b;
            }
            text {
                fill: #ffffff;
            }
        }

        &--metal {
            transform-origin: 83px 91px;
            circle {
                fill: #e2e8f0;
                stroke: #64748b;
                stroke-width: 1.2;
            }
            text {
                fill: #0f172a;
            }
        }

        &--water {
            transform-origin: 37px 91px;
            circle {
                fill: #3b82f6;
            }
            text {
                fill: #ffffff;
            }
        }

        &--wood {
            transform-origin: 23px 48px;
            circle {
                fill: #10b981;
            }
            text {
                fill: #ffffff;
            }
        }
    }
}

// ==========================================================================
// 6. BENTO MODAL QUICK-VIEW GIẢI NGHĨA CHUYÊN SÂU (#day-term-modal)
// ==========================================================================
.day-term-modal {
    @extend %fixedCover;
    z-index: 120;
    @extend %flexCenterSafe;
    padding: 2rem;

    &[hidden] {
        display: none !important;
    }

    &__backdrop {
        @extend %absCover;
        background-color: var(--overlay-bg);
        backdrop-filter: blur(6px);
    }

    &__dialog {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 1;
        width: 100%;
        max-width: 68rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 2px solid var(--primary-color);
        border-radius: 2.8rem;
        box-shadow: 0 2.4rem 5rem var(--shadow-color);
        overflow: hidden;
        @extend %animDropdownFadeIn;
    }

    &__header {
        @extend %flexBetween;
        gap: 1.6rem;
        padding: 2rem 2.6rem;
        background-color: var(--tooltip-bg, var(--bg-surface));
        border-bottom: 1px solid var(--tooltip-border, var(--border-color));
    }

    &__title-group {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 0.4rem;
    }

    &__category {
        display: inline-block;
        width: fit-content;
        padding: 0.3rem 1.2rem;
        background-color: var(--primary-color);
        color: #ffffff;
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        font-size: 1.15rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        text-transform: uppercase;
        letter-spacing: 0.04em;
    }

    &__title {
        font-size: 2.4rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--tooltip-text, var(--text-color));
    }

    &__close {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        width: 4.2rem;
        height: 4.2rem;
        border-radius: 50%;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        font-size: 1.6rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-color);
        @extend %transInteractive;

        &:hover {
            background-color: var(--primary-color);
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: #ffffff;
        }
    }

    &__body {
        display: grid;
        grid-template-columns: 18rem 1fr;
        gap: 2.2rem;
        padding: 2.4rem 2.6rem;
        align-items: center;

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            grid-template-columns: 1fr;
            padding: 1.8rem;
        }
    }

    &__visual {
        @extend %flexColumnCenterAll;
        gap: 1rem;
        min-height: 18rem;
        padding: 1.6rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1.5px dashed var(--primary-color);
        border-radius: 2.2rem;
        text-align: center;

        img {
            width: 8.4rem;
            height: 8.4rem;
            object-fit: contain;
        }

        &.is-zodiac-filtered img {
            filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
        }

        strong {
            font-size: 1.4rem;
            font-weight: 800;
            color: var(--primary-color);
        }
    }

    &__content {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 1.4rem;
    }

    &__meta-pills {
        @extend %flexWrapGap;
    }

    &__pill {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        gap: 0.5rem;
        padding: 0.45rem 1.2rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--text-color);

        img {
            width: 1.6rem;
            height: 1.6rem;
            object-fit: contain;
            filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
        }

        &--highlight {
            background-color: var(--tooltip-bg, var(--bg-surface));
            border-color: var(--tooltip-border, var(--primary-color));
            color: var(--tooltip-text, var(--primary-color));
        }
    }

    &__box {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 0.5rem;
        padding: 1.4rem 1.6rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 1.6rem;

        &--advice {
            background-color: var(--tooltip-bg, var(--bg-surface));
            border-color: var(--tooltip-border, var(--primary-color));
        }
    }

    &__box-title {
        font-size: 1.3rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        text-transform: uppercase;
        color: var(--primary-color);
    }

    &__desc,
    &__advice {
        font-size: 1.45rem;
        line-height: 1.6;
        color: var(--text-color);
    }

    &__footer {
        @extend %flexBetween;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        gap: 1.2rem;
        padding: 1.6rem 2.6rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border-top: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    }

    &__btn-close {
        height: 4.2rem;
        padding: 0 1.8rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 1.2rem;
        font-size: 1.4rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--text-color);

        &:hover {
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: var(--primary-color);
        }
    }

    &__btn-seo {
        height: 4.2rem;
        padding: 0 2rem;
        background-color: var(--primary-color);
        color: #ffffff;
        border-radius: 1.2rem;
        font-size: 1.4rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        box-shadow: 0 0.4rem 1.4rem var(--shadow-color);

        &:hover {
            background-color: var(--primary-hover);
            transform: translateY(-1px);
        }
    }
}
```

```scss
@use "../abstracts" as abs;

.week-page {
```
