### year.scss

```scss
// scss/pages/_year.scss
@use "../abstracts" as abs;

.year-page {
    padding-bottom: abs.$gap-section-default;
}

.year-cal-app {
    @extend %flexColumn;
    gap: 2.4rem;

    &__toolbar-card {
        padding: 1.6rem 2.4rem;
    }

    &__toolbar {
        display: grid;
        grid-template-columns: 1fr auto 1fr;
        align-items: center;
        gap: 1.6rem;

        @include abs.screen(md) {
            grid-template-columns: 1fr;
            justify-items: center;
        }
    }

    &__side {
        @extend %flexItemCenter;
        gap: 1rem;

        &--left {
            justify-self: start;
        }

        &--right {
            justify-self: end;
        }

        @include abs.screen(md) {
            justify-self: center;
        }
    }

    &__heading {
        font-size: 2.6rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-color);
        text-align: center;
        font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
    }

    // Lưới Bento 12 Tháng: 4 cột trên PC -> 3 cột (xl) -> 2 cột (lg) -> 1 cột (sm)
    &__grid {
        @extend %gridCols4;
        gap: 2rem;

        @include abs.screen(xl) {
            grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
        }

        @include abs.screen(lg) {
            grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);
        }

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            grid-template-columns: 1fr;
        }
    }

    &__legend {
        flex-direction: row;
        padding: 1.6rem 2.4rem;
    }
}

// Biến thể thu gọn của .month-cal dành cho 12 thẻ tháng trong Lịch Năm
// Biến thể thu gọn của .month-cal dành cho 12 thẻ tháng trong Lịch Năm
.month-cal--mini {
    gap: 1.2rem;
    padding: 1.8rem 1.4rem;
    border-radius: 2rem;

    .month-cal__mini-title {
        font-size: 1.7rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--primary-color);
        text-align: center;
    }

    .month-cal__weekdays,
    .month-cal__grid {
        gap: 0.4rem;
    }

    .month-cal__weekday {
        height: 2.8rem;
        font-size: 1.15rem;
        border-radius: 0.6rem;
        background-color: transparent;
        border: none;
    }

    .month-cal__cell {
        @extend %flexColumnCenterAll;
        min-height: 3.8rem;
        padding: 0.4rem 0.2rem;
        border-radius: 0.8rem;
        background-color: transparent;
        border: 1px solid transparent;

        // Khi rê chuột hoặc focus bàn phím vào ô ngày
        &:hover,
        &:focus-visible {
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        }

        // NGÀY HIỆN TẠI / ĐANG CHỌN (ACTIVE): Có border và chữ theo màu Theme đang chọn
        &--today,
        &--active,
        &[aria-current="date"] {
            background-color: var(--body-bg);
            border: 2px solid var(--primary-color);
            color: var(--primary-color);
            box-shadow: 0 0.4rem 1.2rem var(--shadow-color);

            .month-cal__solar {
                color: var(--primary-color);
                font-weight: 800;
            }
        }
    }

    .month-cal__solar {
        font-size: 1.35rem;
        font-weight: 600;
        color: var(--text-color);
    }

    .month-cal__dots {
        margin-top: 0.2rem;
        justify-content: center;
        gap: 0.3rem;
    }

    .month-cal__dot {
        width: 0.55rem;
        height: 0.55rem;
        box-shadow: none;
    }
}
```

### detail.scss

```scss
// scss/pages/_detail.scss
@use "../abstracts" as abs;

.detail-page {
    padding-bottom: abs.$gap-section-default;

    // 1. Thanh điều hướng đầu trang (Back + Breadcrumb + Prev/Next Sự kiện)
    &__top-bar {
        @extend %flexBetween;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        gap: 1.4rem;
        margin-bottom: 2.2rem;
        padding: 1.4rem 1.8rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 2rem;
        box-shadow: 0 0.4rem 1.6rem var(--shadow-color);
        @extend %transTheme;
    }

    &__top-left,
    &__top-right,
    &__badges {
        @extend %flexItemCenter;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        gap: 1rem;
    }

    &__back-btn,
    &__step-btn,
    &__share-btn {
        height: 4rem;
        padding: 0 1.6rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 1.2rem;
        font-size: 1.35rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--text-color);

        &:hover {
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: var(--primary-color);
            transform: translateY(-1px);
        }
    }

    &__share-btn {
        background-color: var(--primary-color);
        border-color: var(--primary-color);
        color: #ffffff;

        &:hover {
            background-color: var(--primary-hover);
            color: #ffffff;
        }
    }

    &__step-text {
        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            display: none;
        }
    }

    &__cat-pill,
    &__date-pill {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        height: 3.6rem;
        padding: 0 1.4rem;
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        font-size: 1.3rem;
        font-weight: 700;
    }

    &__cat-pill {
        background-color: var(--primary-color);
        color: #ffffff;
    }

    &__date-pill {
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        color: var(--text-color);
    }

    &__content-row {
        --grid-gutter-y: 2.4rem;
        margin-top: 2.4rem;
        align-items: flex-start;
    }
}

// 2. Tầng 1: Khối Hero Bento (Ảnh 16:9 + Thông tin chính + 4 Bento Stat Cards có Con Giáp)
.detail-hero {
    @extend %flexColumn;
    gap: 2.2rem;
    padding: 2.6rem;
    background-color: var(--body-bg);
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 2.8rem;
    box-shadow: 0 0.8rem 2.4rem var(--shadow-color);
    @extend %transTheme;

    @include abs.screen(sm) {
        padding: 1.6rem;
        border-radius: 2rem;
        gap: 1.6rem;
    }

    &__row {
        --grid-gutter-y: 2.2rem;
        align-items: stretch;
    }

    &__media {
        position: relative;
        width: 100%;
        height: 100%;
        aspect-ratio: 16 / 9;
        border-radius: 2rem;
        overflow: hidden;
        border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        cursor: zoom-in;
        @extend %transTheme;

        &:hover {
            border-color: var(--primary-color);

            .detail-hero__img {
                transform: scale(1.03);
            }
        }
    }

    &__img {
        @extend %imgCover;
        transition: transform 0.45s ease;
    }

    &__zoom-badge {
        position: absolute;
        right: 1.2rem;
        bottom: 1.2rem;
        z-index: 3;
        padding: 0.6rem 1.2rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--primary-color);
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        font-size: 1.2rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--text-color);
        box-shadow: 0 0.4rem 1.2rem var(--shadow-color);
        backdrop-filter: blur(8px);
        @extend %transInteractive;

        &:hover {
            background-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: #ffffff;
        }
    }

    &__info {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        justify-content: space-between;
        height: 100%;
        gap: 1.8rem;
    }

    &__eyebrow-group {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 1.2rem;
    }

    &__occasion-tag {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        gap: 0.6rem;
        width: fit-content;
        padding: 0.55rem 1.4rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        font-size: 1.3rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--primary-color);

        &-icon {
            width: 1.6rem;
            height: 1.6rem;
        }
    }

    &__title {
        font-size: clamp(2.6rem, 3.2vw, 3.6rem);
        font-weight: 800;
        line-height: 1.2;
        color: var(--text-color);
    }

    &__quote-box {
        padding: 1.8rem 2rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border-left: 4px solid var(--primary-color);
        border-radius: 0 1.8rem 1.8rem 0;
    }

    &__quote-text {
        font-size: 1.55rem;
        font-style: italic;
        line-height: 1.6;
        color: var(--text-color);
    }

    &__quote-author {
        display: block;
        margin-top: 0.8rem;
        font-size: 1.35rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--primary-color);
    }

    &__action-row {
        @extend %flexItemCenter;
        gap: 1rem;
    }

    &__calendar-btn {
        height: 4.4rem;
        padding: 0 2rem;
        background-color: var(--primary-color);
        color: #ffffff;
        font-size: 1.4rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        box-shadow: 0 0.6rem 1.6rem var(--shadow-color);

        .icon {
            filter: brightness(0) invert(1);
            width: 1.8rem;
            height: 1.8rem;
        }

        &:hover {
            background-color: var(--primary-hover);
            transform: translateY(-2px);
        }
    }

    // Dải 4 Bento Stat Cards ở chân Hero
    &__stats-grid {
        @extend %gridCols4;
        gap: 1.6rem;
        padding-top: 2rem;
        border-top: 1px solid var(--border-color);

        @include abs.screen(lg) {
            grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);
        }

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            grid-template-columns: 1fr;
        }
    }
}

.detail-stat-card {
    position: relative;
    overflow: hidden;
    @extend %flexItemCenter;
    min-height: 11rem;
    gap: 1.5rem;
    padding: 1.6rem 1.8rem;
    background-color: var(--bg-surface);
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 2rem;
    @extend %transInteractive;

    &:hover {
        border-color: var(--primary-color);
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        transform: translateY(-2px);
        box-shadow: 0 0.8rem 2rem var(--shadow-color);

        .detail-stat-card__bg-zodiac {
            opacity: 0.22;
            transform: scale(1.08) rotate(0deg);
        }
    }

    &__icon-box {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        @extend %flexItemFixed;
        width: 5.2rem;
        height: 5.2rem;
        border-radius: 1.5rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        box-shadow: 0 0.4rem 1.2rem var(--shadow-color);
    }

    &__zodiac {
        width: 3.4rem;
        height: 3.4rem;
        object-fit: contain;
        filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
    }

    &__icon {
        width: 2.6rem;
        height: 2.6rem;
        object-fit: contain;
    }

    &__content {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 0.35rem;
        min-width: 0;
    }

    &__label {
        font-size: 1.2rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--text-muted);
    }

    &__value {
        font-size: 1.75rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-color);
    }

    &__sub {
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--primary-color);
    }

    &__bg-zodiac {
        position: absolute;
        right: 0.6rem;
        bottom: 0.4rem;
        width: 5.6rem;
        height: 5.6rem;
        object-fit: contain;
        opacity: 0.1;
        filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
        transform: rotate(-10deg);
        pointer-events: none;
        transition:
            transform 0.35s ease,
            opacity 0.35s ease;
    }
}

// 3. Cột Trái (8 cột): Nội dung Bài viết & Thư viện Ảnh
.detail-article {
    @extend %flexColumn;
    gap: 2.4rem;
    padding: 3rem;
    background-color: var(--body-bg);
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 2.6rem;
    box-shadow: 0 0.4rem 1.6rem var(--shadow-color);

    @include abs.screen(sm) {
        padding: 1.8rem;
        border-radius: 2rem;
    }

    &__head {
        @extend %flexBetween;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        gap: 1rem;
        padding-bottom: 1.4rem;
        border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    }

    &__heading {
        font-size: 2.3rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-color);
    }

    &__type-tag {
        padding: 0.45rem 1.2rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--primary-color);
    }

    &__body {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 1.6rem;
        font-size: 1.65rem;
        line-height: 1.8;
        color: var(--text-color);

        p:first-of-type::first-letter {
            font-size: 3.4rem;
            font-weight: 800;
            color: var(--primary-color);
            float: left;
            line-height: 1;
            margin-right: 1rem;
            margin-top: 0.4rem;
        }
    }
}

.detail-gallery {
    @extend %flexColumn;
    gap: 1.6rem;
    padding: 2.2rem;
    background-color: var(--bg-surface);
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 2.2rem;

    @include abs.screen(sm) {
        padding: 1.4rem;
        border-radius: 1.8rem;
    }

    &__head {
        @extend %flexBetween;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        gap: 1rem;
    }

    &__title {
        font-size: 1.8rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-color);
    }

    &__sub {
        margin-top: 0.2rem;
        font-size: 1.3rem;
        color: var(--text-muted);
    }

    // Badge hướng dẫn phóng to thay cho đường dẫn code cũ
    &__hint-badge {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        gap: 0.6rem;
        padding: 0.5rem 1.2rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--primary-color);
    }

    &__grid {
        @extend %gridCols2;
        gap: 1.6rem;

        &--single {
            grid-template-columns: 1fr;
        }

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            grid-template-columns: 1fr;
        }
    }

    // Khung ảnh khóa tỷ lệ 16:9 và định vị tương đối để ảnh chiếm trọn 100% diện tích
    &__item {
        position: relative;
        width: 100%;
        aspect-ratio: 16 / 9;
        overflow: hidden;
        border-radius: 1.8rem;
        border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        cursor: zoom-in;
        @extend %transInteractive;

        &:hover {
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            transform: translateY(-2px);
            box-shadow: 0 1rem 2.4rem var(--shadow-color);

            .detail-gallery__img {
                transform: scale(1.03);
            }

            .detail-gallery__caption {
                background-color: var(--primary-color);
                border-color: var(--primary-hover);
                color: #ffffff;

                .detail-gallery__caption-badge {
                    background-color: #ffffff;
                    color: var(--primary-color);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // Ảnh phủ kín 100% toàn bộ khung (chiếm luôn cả không gian của caption cũ)
    &__img {
        @extend %imgCover;
        transition: transform 0.45s ease;
    }

    // Caption nằm đè lên trên ảnh ở góc dưới phải (Đồng bộ với Bento Overlay của trang chủ)
    &__caption {
        position: absolute;
        right: 1.2rem;
        bottom: 1.2rem;
        z-index: 3;
        @extend %flexItemCenter;
        gap: 0.8rem;
        max-width: calc(100% - 2.4rem);
        padding: 0.65rem 1.2rem 0.65rem 0.8rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        color: var(--text-color);
        border: 1.5px solid var(--primary-color);
        border-radius: 1.2rem;
        box-shadow: 0 0.6rem 1.8rem var(--shadow-color);
        backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
        font-size: 1.3rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        line-height: 1.3;
        pointer-events: none;
        @extend %transTheme;

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            right: 0.8rem;
            bottom: 0.8rem;
            max-width: calc(100% - 1.6rem);
            padding: 0.5rem 0.9rem 0.5rem 0.6rem;
            font-size: 1.15rem;
        }

        &-badge {
            @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
            @extend %flexItemFixed;
            padding: 0.25rem 0.7rem;
            background-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: #ffffff;
            border-radius: 0.7rem;
            font-size: 1.05rem;
            font-weight: 800;
            text-transform: uppercase;
            letter-spacing: 0.03em;
            @extend %transTheme;
        }

        &-text {
            @extend %textClamp2;
        }
    }
}
.detail-sources {
    padding: 2rem 2.2rem;
    background-color: var(--bg-surface);
    border: 1px dashed var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 2rem;

    &__title {
        font-size: 1.65rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-color);
        margin-bottom: 1.2rem;
    }

    &__list {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 0.8rem;
    }

    &__item {
        font-size: 1.45rem;
        color: var(--text-color);
        padding-left: 1.6rem;
        position: relative;

        &::before {
            content: "•";
            position: absolute;
            left: 0;
            color: var(--primary-color);
            font-weight: 800;
        }
    }
}

// 4. Cột Phải Sticky: Danh sách sự kiện cùng tháng & Bộ lọc
.detail-sidebar {
    position: sticky;
    top: calc(#{abs.$header-top-offset} + #{abs.$header-height-pc} + 2rem);

    @include abs.screen(lg) {
        position: static;
    }

    &__card {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 1.6rem;
        padding: 2.4rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 2.6rem;
        box-shadow: 0 0.4rem 1.6rem var(--shadow-color);
    }

    &__head {
        @extend %flexBetween;
        padding-bottom: 1.2rem;
        border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    }

    &__title {
        font-size: 1.9rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-color);
    }

    &__count {
        padding: 0.35rem 1.1rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--primary-color);
    }

    &__filters {
        @extend %gridCols3;
        gap: 0.6rem;
        padding: 0.4rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 1.2rem;
    }

    &__filter-btn {
        height: 3.4rem;
        border-radius: 0.9rem;
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--text-muted);
        @extend %transTheme;

        &:hover {
            color: var(--text-color);
        }

        &.is-active {
            background-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: #ffffff;
        }
    }

    &__list {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 1rem;
        max-height: 60rem;
        overflow-y: auto;
        padding-right: 0.4rem;
    }

    &__item {
        @extend %flexItemCenter;
        gap: 1.2rem;
        padding: 1.1rem 1.2rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 1.6rem;
        @extend %transInteractive;

        &:hover,
        &.is-current {
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            background-color: var(--body-bg);
            transform: translateY(-2px);
            box-shadow: 0 0.6rem 1.4rem var(--shadow-color);
        }

        &.is-current {
            border-width: 1.5px;
        }
    }

    &__date-box {
        @extend %flexColumnCenterAll;
        @extend %flexItemFixed;
        width: 5rem;
        height: 5rem;
        border-radius: 1.4rem;
        background-color: var(--primary-color);
        color: #ffffff;
        font-weight: 800;
        line-height: 1.1;

        small {
            font-size: 1.05rem;
            opacity: 0.9;
        }

        strong {
            font-size: 1.7rem;
        }
    }

    &__item-info {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 0.3rem;
        min-width: 0;
    }

    &__item-name {
        font-size: 1.45rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-color);
        @extend %textEllipsis;
    }

    &__item-occasion {
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        color: var(--text-muted);
        @extend %textClamp2;
    }
}

// 5. Hộp thoại Lightbox phóng to ảnh tư liệu
.detail-lightbox {
    @extend %fixedCover;
    z-index: 95;
    @extend %flexCenter;
    padding: 2.4rem;

    &[hidden] {
        display: none !important;
    }

    &__backdrop {
        @extend %absCover;
        background-color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.82);
        backdrop-filter: blur(6px);
    }

    &__figure {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        max-width: 102rem;
        width: 100%;
        @extend %flexColumnCenterAll;
        gap: 1.4rem;
        @extend %animDropdownFadeIn;
    }

    &__close {
        position: absolute;
        top: -1.6rem;
        right: -1.6rem;
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        width: 4.4rem;
        height: 4.4rem;
        border-radius: 50%;
        background-color: var(--primary-color);
        color: #ffffff;
        font-size: 1.8rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        box-shadow: 0 0.6rem 1.8rem rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4);

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            top: 0.8rem;
            right: 0.8rem;
        }
    }

    &__img {
        max-height: 78vh;
        width: auto;
        border-radius: 2rem;
        border: 2px solid var(--primary-color);
        box-shadow: 0 2rem 5rem rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5);
    }

    &__caption {
        padding: 0.8rem 2rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        font-size: 1.45rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--text-color);
    }
}
```

```scss
// scss/pages/_festival.scss
@use "../abstracts" as abs;

// Tắt sticky của Header tổng chỉ riêng trên trang Festival (các trang khác giữ nguyên 100%)
body[data-page="festival"] .header,
.festival-body .header {
    position: relative;
    top: 0;
}

.festival-page {
    padding-bottom: abs.$gap-section-default;
}
```

### bazi.scss

```scss
// scss/pages/_bazi.scss
// BẢN FULL 100% ĐÃ FIX TRIỆT ĐỂ LỖI TRÀN CỘT TỨ TRỤ (minmax(0, 1fr) + min-width: 0)
@use "../abstracts" as abs;

.bazi-page {
    padding-bottom: abs.$gap-section-default;
    overflow-x: clip;

    // Hệ thống Cặp Hàng Ngang khóa chặt chiều cao 2 cột Trái/Phải chống lệch ngã tư 100%
    &__pairs-wrapper {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 2.2rem;
        margin-top: 2.2rem;
    }

    &__pair-row {
        --grid-gutter-y: 2.2rem;
        align-items: stretch;
    }

    &__pair-col {
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        gap: 2.2rem;
        min-width: 0; // Khóa chống tràn Flex/Grid từ cấp cột Bootstrap
    }

    // Bento Tooltip 3 tầng độc lập trên trang Bát Tự
    .has-bento-tooltip {
        position: relative;
        overflow: visible;

        .bento-tooltip {
            position: absolute;
            bottom: calc(100% + 1.1rem);
            left: 50%;
            transform: translateX(-50%) translateY(0.6rem);
            z-index: 80;
            width: 31rem;
            padding: 1.3rem 1.5rem;
            background-color: var(--tooltip-bg, var(--body-bg));
            color: var(--tooltip-text, var(--text-color));
            border: 1.5px solid var(--tooltip-border, var(--primary-color));
            border-radius: 1.6rem;
            box-shadow:
                0 1.4rem 3rem var(--shadow-color),
                0 0 0 3px var(--body-bg);
            display: flex;
            flex-direction: column;
            align-items: flex-start;
            gap: 0.65rem;
            text-align: left;
            white-space: normal;
            opacity: 0;
            visibility: hidden;
            pointer-events: none;
            transition:
                opacity 0.2s ease 0s,
                transform 0.2s ease 0s,
                visibility 0.2s ease 0s;

            &::after {
                content: "";
                position: absolute;
                top: 100%;
                left: 50%;
                transform: translateX(-50%);
                border-width: 0.65rem;
                border-style: solid;
                border-color: var(--tooltip-border, var(--primary-color)) transparent transparent transparent;
            }

            &__title {
                display: block;
                font-size: 1.35rem;
                font-weight: 800;
                line-height: 1.3;
                color: var(--primary-color);
            }

            &__desc {
                display: block;
                font-size: 1.25rem;
                font-weight: 600;
                line-height: 1.5;
                color: var(--tooltip-text, var(--text-color));
            }

            &__tag {
                display: inline-flex;
                align-items: center;
                gap: 0.45rem;
                margin-top: 0.2rem;
                padding: 0.35rem 1.1rem;
                background-color: var(--primary-color);
                color: #ffffff;
                border-radius: 99.9rem;
                font-size: 1.15rem;
                font-weight: 800;
                line-height: 1.2;
                box-shadow: 0 0.3rem 0.8rem var(--shadow-color);
            }
        }

        @media (hover: hover) {
            &:hover,
            &:focus-visible {
                z-index: 45;

                > .bento-tooltip {
                    opacity: 1;
                    visibility: visible;
                    transform: translateX(-50%) translateY(0);
                    transition-delay: 0.14s;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

.bazi-card {
    position: relative;
    overflow: visible;
    min-width: 0;
    @extend %flexColumn;
    gap: 1.8rem;
    padding: 2.2rem 2.4rem;
    background-color: var(--body-bg);
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 2.6rem;
    box-shadow: 0 0.6rem 2rem var(--shadow-color);
    @extend %transTheme;

    &--grow {
        flex: 1;
        height: 100%;
        justify-content: space-between;
    }

    @include abs.screen(sm) {
        padding: 1.6rem;
        border-radius: 2rem;
        gap: 1.4rem;
    }

    // FIX HEADER CARD: Giữ nhãn Badge luôn nằm thẳng hàng ở góc phải trên cùng, không bị rớt dòng
    &__head {
        @extend %flexBetween;
        align-items: flex-start;
        flex-wrap: nowrap;
        gap: 1.4rem;
        padding-bottom: 1.2rem;
        border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border-color);

        > div:first-child {
            flex: 1 1 0%;
            min-width: 0;
        }

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            flex-wrap: wrap;
        }
    }

    &__title {
        font-size: 1.9rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        line-height: 1.25;
        color: var(--text-color);
    }

    &__sub {
        margin-top: 0.35rem;
        font-size: 1.3rem;
        font-weight: 600;
        line-height: 1.45;
        color: var(--text-muted);
    }

    &__tag,
    &__badge {
        @extend %flexItemFixed;
        padding: 0.45rem 1.3rem;
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        font-size: 1.2rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        @extend %textNoWrap;
    }

    &__tag {
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        color: var(--primary-color);
    }

    &__badge {
        background-color: var(--primary-color);
        color: #ffffff;
    }
}

// ==========================================================================
// 1. THANH CÔNG CỤ LẬP LÁ SỐ BÁT TỰ (.bazi-toolbar) & CUSTOM BENTO PICKER
// ==========================================================================
.bazi-toolbar {
    gap: 2rem;
    z-index: 35;

    &__head {
        @extend %flexBetween;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        gap: 1.6rem;
        padding-bottom: 1.6rem;
        border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    }

    &__title-wrap {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 0.6rem;
    }

    &__eyebrow {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        gap: 0.6rem;
        width: fit-content;
        padding: 0.4rem 1.2rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        font-size: 1.2rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--primary-color);
        text-transform: uppercase;
        letter-spacing: 0.04em;

        &-icon {
            width: 1.6rem;
            height: 1.6rem;
        }
    }

    &__title {
        font-size: clamp(2.1rem, 2.5vw, 2.7rem);
        font-weight: 800;
        line-height: 1.2;
        color: var(--text-color);
    }

    &__quick-actions {
        @extend %flexWrapGap;
        gap: 1rem;
    }

    &__preset-btn {
        height: 4.4rem;
        padding: 0 1.8rem 0 0.8rem;
        border-radius: 1.4rem;
        background-color: var(--primary-soft-bg, var(--bg-surface));
        border: 1.5px solid var(--primary-color);
        font-size: 1.4rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--primary-color);
        box-shadow: 0 0.4rem 1.2rem var(--shadow-color);

        &:hover {
            background-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: #ffffff;
            transform: translateY(-2px);

            .bazi-toolbar__preset-icon-box {
                background-color: #ffffff;
            }

            .bazi-toolbar__preset-icon {
                transform: rotate(360deg) scale(1.1);
            }
        }
    }

    &__preset-icon-box {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        width: 3rem;
        height: 3rem;
        border-radius: 1rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        @extend %transTheme;
    }

    &__preset-icon {
        width: 1.8rem;
        height: 1.8rem;
        filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
        transition: transform 0.5s cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1);
    }

    &__share-btn {
        height: 4.4rem;
        padding: 0 2rem;
        border-radius: 1.4rem;
        background-color: var(--primary-color);
        color: #ffffff;
        font-size: 1.4rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        box-shadow: 0 0.4rem 1.2rem var(--shadow-color);

        &:hover {
            background-color: var(--primary-hover);
            transform: translateY(-2px);
        }
    }

    &__form {
        display: grid;
        grid-template-columns: 0.85fr 0.9fr 0.9fr 1.35fr 1.25fr 1.45fr;
        align-items: end;
        gap: 1.4rem;

        @include abs.screen(xl) {
            grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr));
        }

        @include abs.screen(md) {
            grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
        }

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            grid-template-columns: 1fr;
        }
    }

    &__field {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 0.6rem;
        min-width: 0;
        position: relative;
    }

    &__label {
        font-size: 1.2rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-muted);
        text-transform: uppercase;
        letter-spacing: 0.03em;
        padding-left: 0.2rem;
    }

    &__gender-group {
        display: grid;
        grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
        gap: 0.5rem;
        height: 4.8rem;
        padding: 0.45rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 1.4rem;
    }

    &__gender-btn {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        gap: 0.6rem;
        height: 100%;
        border-radius: 1rem;
        font-size: 1.35rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-muted);
        @extend %textNoWrap;
        @extend %transTheme;

        &:hover {
            color: var(--text-color);
        }

        &.is-active {
            background-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: #ffffff;
            box-shadow: 0 0.3rem 0.8rem var(--shadow-color);

            .bazi-toolbar__gender-dot {
                background-color: #ffffff;
                box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.3);
            }
        }
    }

    &__gender-dot {
        width: 0.75rem;
        height: 0.75rem;
        border-radius: 50%;
        background-color: var(--text-muted);
        @extend %flexItemFixed;
    }
}

// CUSTOM BENTO DROPDOWN PICKER
.bazi-picker {
    position: relative;
    min-width: 0;

    &__summary {
        @extend %flexBetween;
        gap: 0.8rem;
        height: 4.8rem;
        padding: 0 1.4rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 1.4rem;
        font-size: 1.4rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--text-color);
        cursor: pointer;
        user-select: none;
        @extend %transTheme;

        &:hover {
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            background-color: var(--body-bg);
        }
    }

    &__summary-text {
        @extend %textEllipsis;
    }

    &__arrow {
        width: 1.5rem;
        height: 1.5rem;
        @extend %flexItemFixed;
        @extend %transBurgerBar;
    }

    &[open] &__summary {
        border-color: var(--primary-color);
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px var(--primary-soft-bg);
    }

    &[open] &__arrow {
        transform: rotate(180deg);
    }

    &__popover {
        position: absolute;
        top: calc(100% + 0.8rem);
        left: 0;
        z-index: 90;
        width: max-content;
        min-width: 28rem;
        max-width: calc(100vw - 3.2rem);
        padding: 1.4rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1.5px solid var(--primary-color);
        border-radius: 1.8rem;
        box-shadow: 0 1.6rem 3.6rem var(--shadow-color);
        @extend %animDropdownFadeIn;

        &--days {
            min-width: 31rem;
        }

        &--hours {
            min-width: 34rem;
        }

        &--regions {
            right: 0;
            left: auto;
            min-width: 31rem;

            @include abs.screen(md) {
                left: 0;
                right: auto;
            }
        }

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            min-width: 100%;
            width: 100%;
        }
    }

    &__popover-head {
        font-size: 1.2rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-muted);
        text-align: center;
        text-transform: uppercase;
        letter-spacing: 0.03em;
        margin-bottom: 1rem;
        padding-bottom: 0.6rem;
        border-bottom: 1px dashed var(--border-color);
    }

    &__year-nav {
        @extend %flexBetween;
        margin-bottom: 1rem;
        padding-bottom: 0.6rem;
        border-bottom: 1px dashed var(--border-color);
    }

    &__year-step {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        width: 3.2rem;
        height: 3.2rem;
        border-radius: 0.8rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-color);
        @extend %transInteractive;

        &:hover {
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: var(--primary-color);
        }
    }

    &__year-range {
        font-size: 1.35rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--primary-color);
    }

    &__grid {
        display: grid;
        gap: 0.55rem;

        &--days {
            grid-template-columns: repeat(7, 1fr);
        }

        &--months {
            grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
        }

        &--years {
            grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr);
        }

        &--hours {
            grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);

            @include abs.screen(sm) {
                grid-template-columns: 1fr;
            }
        }
    }

    &__list {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 0.5rem;
    }

    &__option {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        width: 100%;
        min-height: 3.8rem;
        padding: 0.6rem 0.8rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 1rem;
        font-size: 1.3rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--text-color);
        font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
        @extend %transInteractive;

        &--row {
            justify-content: space-between;
            padding: 0.9rem 1.2rem;
            gap: 1rem;
        }

        small {
            font-size: 1.15rem;
            font-weight: 600;
            color: var(--text-muted);
        }

        &:hover {
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: var(--primary-color);
            transform: translateY(-1px);
        }

        &.is-selected {
            background-color: var(--primary-color);
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: #ffffff;
            box-shadow: 0 0.4rem 1rem var(--shadow-color);

            small {
                color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.88);
            }
        }
    }
}

// ==========================================================================
// 2. CỘT TRÁI CẶP 1: HỒ SƠ BẢN MỆNH & NHẬT CHỦ (.bazi-master-card)
// ==========================================================================
.bazi-master-card {
    flex: 1;
    height: 100%;
    overflow: hidden;
    justify-content: space-between;
    background-color: var(--primary-color);
    border-color: var(--primary-hover);
    color: #ffffff;
    box-shadow: 0 1rem 2.8rem var(--shadow-color);

    &__top {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 1.4rem;
    }

    &__badges {
        @extend %flexBetween;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        gap: 0.8rem;
    }

    &__pill,
    &__term-pill {
        padding: 0.4rem 1.2rem;
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        font-size: 1.2rem;
        font-weight: 800;
    }

    &__pill {
        background-color: #ffffff;
        color: var(--primary-active);
    }

    &__term-pill {
        background-color: var(--hero-box-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--hero-border-color);
        color: #ffffff;
    }

    &__hero {
        @extend %flexItemCenter;
        gap: 1.5rem;
        padding-top: 0.4rem;
    }

    &__icon-box {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        @extend %flexItemFixed;
        width: 6.4rem;
        height: 6.4rem;
        border-radius: 2rem;
        background-color: #ffffff;
        box-shadow: 0 0.8rem 2rem rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.18);
    }

    &__elem-icon {
        width: 3.8rem;
        height: 3.8rem;
        object-fit: contain;
    }

    &__title-group {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 0.25rem;
        min-width: 0;
    }

    &__label {
        font-size: 1.2rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        text-transform: uppercase;
        letter-spacing: 0.04em;
        color: var(--hero-text-soft);
    }

    &__name {
        font-size: 2.6rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        line-height: 1.15;
        color: #ffffff;
    }

    &__archetype {
        font-size: 1.3rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--hero-text-soft);
    }

    &__desc {
        padding: 1.3rem 1.5rem;
        background-color: var(--hero-box-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--hero-border-color);
        border-radius: 1.6rem;
        font-size: 1.35rem;
        line-height: 1.55;
        color: #ffffff;
    }

    &__meta-grid {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        display: grid;
        grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
        gap: 1rem;

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            grid-template-columns: 1fr;
        }
    }

    &__meta-item {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 0.25rem;
        min-width: 0;
        padding: 1.1rem 1.3rem;
        background-color: var(--hero-box-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--hero-border-color);
        border-radius: 1.4rem;
    }

    &__meta-label {
        font-size: 1.15rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--hero-text-soft);
    }

    &__meta-val {
        font-size: 1.35rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: #ffffff;
    }

    &__footer {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        @extend %flexBetween;
        padding-top: 0.4rem;
    }

    &__day-link {
        height: 4.2rem;
        padding: 0 1.8rem;
        background-color: var(--hero-chip-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--hero-border-color);
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        color: #ffffff;
        font-size: 1.35rem;
        font-weight: 800;

        img {
            width: 1.8rem;
            height: 1.8rem;
            filter: brightness(0) invert(1);
        }

        &:hover {
            background-color: #ffffff;
            color: var(--primary-active);

            img {
                filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
            }
        }
    }

    &__watermark {
        position: absolute;
        right: -1rem;
        bottom: -1rem;
        width: 14rem;
        height: 14rem;
        object-fit: contain;
        opacity: 0.15;
        filter: var(--zodiac-filter-active);
        pointer-events: none;
        z-index: 1;
    }
}

// ==========================================================================
// 3. CỘT PHẢI CẶP 1: BÀN LÁ SỐ TỨ TRỤ 4 CỘT (ĐÃ KHÓA minmax(0, 1fr) CHỐNG TRÀN 100%)
// ==========================================================================
.bazi-pillars-grid {
    flex: 1;
    width: 100%;
    display: grid;
    // Khóa cứng minmax(0, 1fr) để 4 cột luôn nằm gọn 100% bên trong .bazi-pillars-board
    grid-template-columns: repeat(4, minmax(0, 1fr));
    align-items: stretch;
    gap: 1.2rem;

    // Khi màn hình dưới 1200px (xl) mà 2 cột lớn Trái/Phải vẫn đứng cạnh nhau, chuyển thành lưới 2x2 thoáng đẹp
    @include abs.screen(xl) {
        grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
    }

    @include abs.screen(sm) {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }
}

.bazi-pillar-col {
    position: relative;
    min-width: 0; // Bắt buộc có để thẻ con không đẩy rộng cột Grid
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    justify-content: flex-start;
    height: 100%;
    gap: 1rem;
    padding: 1.4rem 1.2rem;
    background-color: var(--bg-surface);
    border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 2rem;
    text-align: center;
    cursor: pointer;
    @extend %transInteractive;

    &--daymaster {
        background-color: var(--primary-soft-bg, var(--bg-surface));
        border: 2px solid var(--primary-color);
        box-shadow: 0 0.8rem 2.2rem var(--shadow-color);
    }

    &__bg-wrap {
        @extend %absCover;
        border-radius: inherit;
        overflow: hidden;
        pointer-events: none;
        z-index: 1;
    }

    &__watermark {
        position: absolute;
        right: -0.6rem;
        bottom: -0.6rem;
        width: 6rem;
        height: 6rem;
        object-fit: contain;
        opacity: 0.1;
        filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
        transform: rotate(-10deg);
        transition:
            transform 0.35s ease,
            opacity 0.35s ease;
    }

    &:hover {
        border-color: var(--primary-color);
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        transform: translateY(-3px);
        box-shadow: 0 1rem 2.4rem var(--shadow-color);

        .bazi-pillar-col__watermark {
            opacity: 0.24;
            transform: scale(1.1) rotate(0deg);
        }

        .bazi-pillar-col__char-icon img {
            transform: scale(1.14) rotate(8deg);
        }
    }

    &__head {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        @extend %flexColumnCenterAll;
        gap: 0.45rem;
        padding-bottom: 0.9rem;
        border-bottom: 1px dashed var(--border-color);
    }

    &__pillar-name {
        font-size: 1.3rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        text-transform: uppercase;
        letter-spacing: 0.04em;
        color: var(--text-color);
        @extend %textNoWrap;
    }

    &__ten-god {
        padding: 0.25rem 1.1rem;
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        font-size: 1.2rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--primary-color);
        @extend %textNoWrap;

        &--master {
            background-color: var(--primary-color);
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: #ffffff;
        }
    }

    &__main-chars {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 0.8rem;
        min-width: 0;
    }

    &__stem-box,
    &__branch-box {
        @extend %flexBetween;
        gap: 0.6rem;
        min-width: 0;
        padding: 0.9rem 1rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 1.4rem;
        text-align: left;
    }

    // Đã gỡ bỏ %textNoWrap trên small để chữ "Thiên Can (Dương Thủy)" tự co giãn mềm mại, không đẩy tràn cột 4
    &__char-info {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 0.2rem;
        flex: 1 1 0%;
        min-width: 0;

        small {
            font-size: 1.05rem;
            font-weight: 700;
            line-height: 1.25;
            color: var(--text-muted);
            white-space: normal;
        }

        strong {
            font-size: 2rem;
            font-weight: 800;
            line-height: 1.1;
            color: var(--text-color);
        }
    }

    &__char-icon {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        @extend %flexItemFixed;
        width: 3.4rem;
        height: 3.4rem;
        border-radius: 1rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);

        img {
            width: 2.1rem;
            height: 2.1rem;
            object-fit: contain;
            transition: transform 0.3s cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1);
        }

        &--zodiac img {
            filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
        }
    }

    &__hidden-stems {
        flex: 1;
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        min-width: 0;
        @extend %flexColumn;
        justify-content: flex-start;
        gap: 0.5rem;
        padding: 0.9rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px dashed var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 1.4rem;
        text-align: left;
    }

    &__section-label {
        font-size: 1.05rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        text-transform: uppercase;
        color: var(--text-muted);
    }

    &__hidden-list {
        @extend %flexWrapGap;
        gap: 0.4rem;
    }

    &__hidden-chip {
        padding: 0.2rem 0.65rem;
        border-radius: 0.75rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        font-size: 1.1rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--text-color);

        em {
            font-style: normal;
            color: var(--primary-color);
            font-weight: 800;
        }
    }

    &__foot {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 0.4rem;
        margin-top: auto;
        padding-top: 0.8rem;
        border-top: 1px dashed var(--border-color);
    }

    &__napam {
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-color);
    }

    &__stage-pill {
        display: inline-block;
        width: fit-content;
        max-width: 100%;
        margin: 0 auto;
        padding: 0.2rem 0.9rem;
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        font-size: 1.1rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--primary-color);
        @extend %textEllipsis;
    }
}

// ==========================================================================
// 4. CỘT TRÁI CẶP 2: BIỂU ĐỒ NGŨ HÀNH & THẬP THẦN (CÓ SCROLL ANIMATION)
// ==========================================================================
.bazi-wuxing-card {
    &__stage {
        display: grid;
        grid-template-columns: auto 1fr;
        align-items: center;
        gap: 1.8rem;
        padding: 1.4rem 1.6rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 2rem;

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            grid-template-columns: 1fr;
            justify-items: center;
            text-align: center;
        }
    }

    &__svg-box {
        @extend %flexCenter;
    }

    &__legend {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 0.8rem;
        min-width: 0;
    }

    &__legend-item {
        @extend %flexItemCenter;
        gap: 0.8rem;
        font-size: 1.3rem;
        color: var(--text-color);

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            justify-content: center;
        }
    }

    &__dot {
        width: 1.2rem;
        height: 1.2rem;
        border-radius: 50%;
        @extend %flexItemFixed;

        &--gen {
            border: 2px dashed #10b981;
        }

        &--ctrl {
            background-color: #ef4444;
        }
    }

    &__summary-note {
        padding-top: 0.6rem;
        border-top: 1px dashed var(--border-color);
        font-size: 1.3rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--primary-color);
    }
}

.bazi-elements-bars {
    @extend %flexColumn;
    gap: 0.9rem;

    &.is-in-view .bazi-element-row__fill {
        width: var(--target-w, 8%);
    }
}

.bazi-element-row {
    @extend %flexColumn;
    gap: 0.45rem;
    padding: 0.95rem 1.3rem;
    background-color: var(--bg-surface);
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 1.4rem;
    @extend %transInteractive;

    &:hover {
        border-color: var(--primary-color);
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        transform: translateX(3px);

        .bazi-element-row__name img {
            transform: scale(1.18) rotate(10deg);
        }
    }

    &__top {
        @extend %flexBetween;
        gap: 0.8rem;
        font-size: 1.3rem;
    }

    &__name {
        @extend %flexItemCenter;
        gap: 0.7rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-color);

        img {
            width: 1.9rem;
            height: 1.9rem;
            object-fit: contain;
            transition: transform 0.3s ease;
        }
    }

    &__meta {
        @extend %flexItemCenter;
        gap: 0.8rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
    }

    &__state {
        padding: 0.15rem 0.8rem;
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        font-size: 1.15rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--primary-color);
    }

    &__track {
        width: 100%;
        height: 0.9rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        overflow: hidden;
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    }

    &__fill {
        position: relative;
        width: 0;
        height: 100%;
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        transition: width 1.05s cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1);

        &--metal {
            background: linear-gradient(90deg, #ca8a04, #facc15);
        }
        &--wood {
            background: linear-gradient(90deg, #059669, #34d399);
        }
        &--water {
            background: linear-gradient(90deg, #2563eb, #60a5fa);
        }
        &--fire {
            background: linear-gradient(90deg, #dc2626, #f87171);
        }
        &--earth {
            background: linear-gradient(90deg, #b45309, #fbbf24);
        }
    }
}

// KHỐI CẤU TRÚC 5 NHÓM THẬP THẦN
.bazi-tengods-summary {
    @extend %flexColumn;
    gap: 0.9rem;
    margin-top: auto;
    padding: 1.3rem 1.4rem;
    background-color: var(--bg-surface);
    border: 1px dashed var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 1.8rem;

    &__head {
        @extend %flexBetween;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        gap: 0.6rem;
    }

    &__title {
        font-size: 1.35rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-color);
    }

    &__hint {
        font-size: 1.15rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--primary-color);
    }

    &__grid {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 0.65rem;

        &.is-in-view .bazi-tengods-row__fill {
            width: var(--target-w, 10%);
        }
    }
}

.bazi-tengods-row {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: 14.5rem 1fr auto;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 1rem;
    font-size: 1.2rem;

    @include abs.screen(sm) {
        grid-template-columns: 12rem 1fr auto;
    }

    &__label {
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--text-color);
        @extend %textNoWrap;

        small {
            font-weight: 600;
            color: var(--text-muted);
        }
    }

    &__track {
        height: 0.7rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        overflow: hidden;
    }

    &__fill {
        width: 0;
        height: 100%;
        background-color: var(--primary-color);
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        transition: width 1.1s cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1);
    }

    &__val {
        min-width: 3.4rem;
        text-align: right;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--primary-color);
        font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
    }
}

// ==========================================================================
// 5. CỘT PHẢI CẶP 2: DỤNG THẦN, HỶ THẦN, KỴ THẦN & 4 HỘP CẢI VẬN (KHÓA minmax(0, 1fr))
// ==========================================================================
.bazi-gods-grid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr));
    align-items: stretch;
    gap: 1.2rem;

    @include abs.screen(sm) {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }
}

.bazi-god-box {
    position: relative;
    min-width: 0;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    justify-content: space-between;
    height: 100%;
    gap: 0.8rem;
    padding: 1.5rem;
    border-radius: 1.8rem;
    border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
    cursor: pointer;
    @extend %transInteractive;

    &__bg-wrap {
        @extend %absCover;
        border-radius: inherit;
        overflow: hidden;
        pointer-events: none;
        z-index: 1;
    }

    &__watermark {
        position: absolute;
        right: -0.6rem;
        bottom: -0.6rem;
        width: 6.2rem;
        height: 6.2rem;
        object-fit: contain;
        opacity: 0.1;
        transform: rotate(-10deg);
        transition:
            transform 0.35s ease,
            opacity 0.35s ease;
    }

    &:hover {
        transform: translateY(-3px);
        box-shadow: 0 0.8rem 2rem var(--shadow-color);

        .bazi-god-box__icon-wrap img {
            animation: baziIconHoverBounce 0.85s ease-in-out infinite;
        }

        .bazi-god-box__watermark {
            opacity: 0.24;
            transform: scale(1.1) rotate(0deg);
        }
    }

    &--yong {
        background-color: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.1);
        border-color: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.4);

        .bazi-god-box__role,
        .bazi-god-box__elem {
            color: #047857;
        }
    }

    &--xi {
        background-color: rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.08);
        border-color: rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.35);

        .bazi-god-box__role,
        .bazi-god-box__elem {
            color: #1d4ed8;
        }
    }

    &--ji {
        background-color: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.08);
        border-color: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.35);

        .bazi-god-box__role,
        .bazi-god-box__elem {
            color: #b91c1c;
        }
    }

    &__top {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        @extend %flexBetween;
        gap: 0.8rem;
    }

    &__icon-wrap {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        @extend %flexItemFixed;
        width: 4.2rem;
        height: 4.2rem;
        border-radius: 1.2rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        box-shadow: 0 0.4rem 1rem var(--shadow-color);

        img {
            width: 2.5rem;
            height: 2.5rem;
            object-fit: contain;
            transition: transform 0.3s ease;
        }
    }

    &__role {
        font-size: 1.15rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        line-height: 1.3;
        text-transform: uppercase;
        letter-spacing: 0.02em;
        text-align: right;
    }

    &__elem {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        font-size: 1.95rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        line-height: 1.2;
    }

    &__desc {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        font-weight: 600;
        line-height: 1.5;
        color: var(--text-color);
    }
}

.bazi-remedy-grid {
    flex: 1;
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
    align-items: stretch;
    gap: 1.2rem;

    @include abs.screen(sm) {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }
}

.bazi-remedy-box {
    position: relative;
    overflow: hidden;
    min-width: 0;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    justify-content: space-between;
    height: 100%;
    gap: 0.8rem;
    padding: 1.5rem 1.6rem;
    background-color: var(--bg-surface);
    border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 1.8rem;
    @extend %transInteractive;

    &:hover {
        border-color: var(--primary-color);
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        transform: translateY(-2px);
        box-shadow: 0 0.8rem 1.8rem var(--shadow-color);

        .bazi-remedy-box__icon-wrap img {
            animation: baziIconHoverBounce 0.85s ease-in-out infinite;
        }

        .bazi-remedy-box__watermark {
            opacity: 0.22;
            transform: scale(1.1) rotate(0deg);
        }
    }

    &__head {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        @extend %flexBetween;
        gap: 0.8rem;
    }

    &__title-wrap {
        @extend %flexItemCenter;
        gap: 0.8rem;
        min-width: 0;
    }

    &__icon-wrap {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        @extend %flexItemFixed;
        width: 3.6rem;
        height: 3.6rem;
        border-radius: 1rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);

        img {
            width: 2rem;
            height: 2rem;
            object-fit: contain;
            filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
        }
    }

    &__label {
        font-size: 1.2rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        text-transform: uppercase;
        color: var(--text-muted);
    }

    &__tag {
        @extend %flexItemFixed;
        padding: 0.25rem 0.9rem;
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        font-size: 1.1rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--primary-color);
        @extend %textNoWrap;
    }

    &__val {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        font-size: 1.6rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-color);
    }

    &__sub {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        font-weight: 600;
        line-height: 1.45;
        color: var(--text-muted);
    }

    &__watermark {
        position: absolute;
        right: -0.5rem;
        bottom: -0.5rem;
        width: 5.4rem;
        height: 5.4rem;
        object-fit: contain;
        opacity: 0.08;
        filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
        transform: rotate(-10deg);
        pointer-events: none;
        z-index: 1;
        transition:
            transform 0.35s ease,
            opacity 0.35s ease;
    }
}

@keyframes baziIconHoverBounce {
    0%,
    100% {
        transform: translateY(0) scale(1) rotate(0deg);
    }
    50% {
        transform: translateY(-2.5px) scale(1.15) rotate(8deg);
    }
}

// ==========================================================================
// 6. TẦNG 4: CHU KỲ 8 ĐẠI VẬN (BENTO SOLE 3 - 4 - 1 TRÊN PC + 2 CỘT Ở 690PX + 1 CỘT Ở 520PX)
// ==========================================================================
.bazi-luck-section {
    margin-top: 2.2rem;
}

.bazi-luck-grid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(12, minmax(0, 1fr));
    align-items: stretch;
    gap: 1.5rem;

    @include abs.screen(sm) {
        gap: 1.2rem;
    }
}

.bazi-luck-card {
    position: relative;
    min-width: 0;
    grid-column: span 3;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    justify-content: space-between;
    height: 100%;
    min-height: 17.4rem;
    gap: 1rem;
    padding: 1.8rem 2rem;
    background-color: var(--bg-surface);
    border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 2.2rem;
    text-align: left;
    cursor: pointer;
    @extend %transInteractive;

    &:nth-child(-n + 3) {
        grid-column: span 4;
    }

    @include abs.screen(lg) {
        grid-column: span 6;

        &:nth-child(-n + 3) {
            grid-column: span 6;
        }

        &:nth-child(7) {
            grid-column: span 12;
        }
    }

    @include abs.screen(sm) {
        grid-column: span 12;
        min-height: auto;
        padding: 1.6rem;

        &:nth-child(-n + 3),
        &:nth-child(7) {
            grid-column: span 12;
        }
    }

    &--wide,
    &:nth-child(8) {
        grid-column: span 12;
        display: grid;
        grid-template-columns: 1.15fr 1fr;
        align-items: center;
        gap: 2.4rem;
        min-height: 14rem;
        padding: 2rem 2.4rem;

        @include abs.screen(md) {
            grid-template-columns: 1fr;
            gap: 1.2rem;
            padding: 1.6rem;
        }
    }

    &__bg-wrap {
        @extend %absCover;
        border-radius: inherit;
        overflow: hidden;
        pointer-events: none;
        z-index: 1;
    }

    &__watermark {
        position: absolute;
        right: 0.6rem;
        bottom: 0.4rem;
        width: 6.4rem;
        height: 6.4rem;
        object-fit: contain;
        opacity: 0.12;
        filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
        transform: scale(0.9) rotate(-10deg);
        transition:
            transform 0.35s ease,
            opacity 0.35s ease;
    }

    &.is-current-luck {
        background-color: var(--primary-soft-bg, var(--body-bg));
        border: 2px solid var(--primary-color);
        box-shadow: 0 0.8rem 2.2rem var(--shadow-color);
    }

    &:hover {
        border-color: var(--primary-color);
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        transform: translateY(-3px);
        box-shadow: 0 1rem 2.4rem var(--shadow-color);

        .bazi-luck-card__watermark {
            opacity: 0.32;
            transform: scale(1.1) rotate(0deg);
        }

        .bazi-luck-card__zodiac-box img {
            transform: scale(1.15) rotate(8deg);
        }
    }

    &__top {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        @extend %flexBetween;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        gap: 0.8rem;
        width: 100%;
    }

    &__step {
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        text-transform: uppercase;
        letter-spacing: 0.03em;
        color: var(--text-muted);
        @extend %textNoWrap;
    }

    &__ages {
        padding: 0.3rem 1rem;
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        font-size: 1.2rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--primary-color);
        font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
        @extend %textNoWrap;
    }

    &__mid {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        @extend %flexItemCenter;
        gap: 1.3rem;
        margin: 0.2rem 0;
    }

    &__zodiac-box {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        @extend %flexItemFixed;
        width: 4.8rem;
        height: 4.8rem;
        border-radius: 1.4rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        box-shadow: 0 0.4rem 1rem var(--shadow-color);

        img {
            width: 3rem;
            height: 3rem;
            object-fit: contain;
            filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
            transition: transform 0.3s cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1);
        }
    }

    &__canchi-group {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 0.2rem;
        min-width: 0;
    }

    &__canchi {
        font-size: 2.2rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        line-height: 1.15;
        color: var(--text-color);
        @extend %textNoWrap;
    }

    &__tengod {
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--primary-color);
    }

    &__foot {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        @extend %flexBetween;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        gap: 0.8rem;
        width: 100%;
        margin-top: auto;
        padding-top: 0.9rem;
        border-top: 1px dashed var(--border-color);
    }

    &__napam {
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--text-color);
    }

    &__stage {
        padding: 0.2rem 0.85rem;
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        font-size: 1.15rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--text-muted);
        @extend %textNoWrap;
    }

    &__wide-left {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 1rem;
        padding-right: 2rem;
        border-right: 1px dashed var(--border-color);

        @include abs.screen(md) {
            padding-right: 0;
            border-right: none;
        }
    }

    &__wide-right {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 0.6rem;

        strong {
            font-size: 1.45rem;
            font-weight: 800;
            color: var(--primary-color);
        }

        p {
            font-size: 1.35rem;
            line-height: 1.55;
            color: var(--text-color);
        }
    }
}
```

### utilities.scss

```scss
// scss/pages/_utilities.scss
// BẢN FULL 100% HOÀN CHỈNH CHO CẢ 10 CHUYÊN MỤC TIỆN ÍCH (Copy và dán đè toàn bộ tệp này)
@use "../abstracts" as abs;

.util-page {
    padding-bottom: abs.$gap-section-default;
    overflow-x: clip;

    &__spotlight-row {
        --grid-gutter-y: 2.2rem;
        align-items: stretch;
    }

    &__col {
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        gap: 2rem;
        min-width: 0;
    }
}

// ==========================================================================
// 1. HEADER & BENTO DUAL-NAVBAR 2 TẦNG (.util-header) CHUẨN CHECKLIST
// ==========================================================================
.util-header {
    position: relative;
    z-index: 30;
    @extend %flexColumn;
    gap: 1.2rem;
    margin-bottom: 2.4rem;

    &__top-card {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 1.5rem;
        padding: 2rem 2.4rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 2.6rem;
        box-shadow: 0 0.6rem 2rem var(--shadow-color);
        @extend %transHeaderScroll;

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            padding: 1.5rem 1.6rem;
            border-radius: 2rem;
        }
    }

    &--scrolled &__top-card {
        box-shadow: 0 1.2rem 3rem var(--shadow-color);
        border-color: var(--primary-color);
        backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
    }

    &__brand-row {
        @extend %flexBetween;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        gap: 1.6rem;
        padding-bottom: 1.4rem;
        border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    }

    // Đã đổi từ <a> sang <div> để H1 không hiện con trỏ bàn tay khi không phải link
    &__brand-box {
        @extend %flexItemCenter;
        gap: 1.4rem;
        min-width: 0;
        cursor: default;
    }

    &__logo-box {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        @extend %flexItemFixed;
        width: 5.2rem;
        height: 5.2rem;
        border-radius: 1.5rem;
        background-color: var(--primary-soft-bg, var(--bg-surface));
        border: 1.5px solid var(--primary-color);
        box-shadow: 0 0.4rem 1.2rem var(--shadow-color);
        cursor: pointer;
        @extend %transInteractive;

        &:hover {
            transform: translateY(-2px);

            .util-header__logo-img {
                animation: baziIconHoverBounce 0.85s ease-in-out infinite;
            }
        }
    }

    &__logo-img {
        width: 2.8rem;
        height: 2.8rem;
        filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
    }

    &__title-group {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 0.3rem;
        min-width: 0;
        cursor: default;
    }

    &__eyebrow {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        gap: 0.6rem;
        width: fit-content;
        font-size: 1.2rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--primary-color);
        text-transform: uppercase;
        letter-spacing: 0.04em;

        &-icon {
            width: 1.6rem;
            height: 1.6rem;
            filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
        }
    }

    &__title {
        font-size: clamp(2rem, 2.4vw, 2.5rem);
        font-weight: 800;
        line-height: 1.2;
        color: var(--text-color);
        cursor: default;
        @extend %textEllipsis;
    }

    &__actions {
        @extend %flexItemCenter;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        gap: 1rem;
    }

    &__modal-trigger {
        height: 4.4rem;
        padding: 0 1.8rem;
        border-radius: 1.4rem;
        background-color: var(--primary-soft-bg, var(--bg-surface));
        border: 1.5px solid var(--primary-color);
        font-size: 1.35rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--primary-color);

        .icon {
            width: 1.8rem;
            height: 1.8rem;
            filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
        }

        &:hover {
            background-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: #ffffff;
            transform: translateY(-2px);

            .icon {
                filter: brightness(0) invert(1);
            }
        }

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            span {
                display: none;
            }
            width: 4.4rem;
            height: 4.4rem;
            padding: 0;
        }
    }

    // Nút Hamburger 44x44px chuẩn Checklist trên Mobile (< 768px)
    &__burger {
        display: none;
        flex-direction: column;
        justify-content: center;
        align-items: center;
        gap: 0.5rem;
        width: 4.4rem;
        height: 4.4rem;
        border-radius: 1.2rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
        @extend %transTheme;

        @include abs.screen(md) {
            display: inline-flex;
        }

        &-bar {
            width: 2rem;
            height: 0.2rem;
            background-color: var(--text-color);
            border-radius: 0.2rem;
            @extend %transBurgerBar;
        }

        &.is-active {
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
        }
        &.is-active &-bar:nth-child(1) {
            transform: translateY(0.7rem) rotate(45deg);
        }
        &.is-active &-bar:nth-child(2) {
            opacity: 0;
        }
        &.is-active &-bar:nth-child(3) {
            transform: translateY(-0.7rem) rotate(-45deg);
        }
    }

    &__sub-bar {
        @extend %flexCenter;

        &[hidden] {
            display: none !important;
        }
    }
}

.util-primary-nav-shell {
    position: relative;
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: auto minmax(0, 1fr) auto;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 0.8rem;

    @include abs.screen(md) {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }
}

.util-primary-nav {
    width: 100%;
    min-width: 0;

    // Đặt @extend %animDropdownFadeIn ở cấp gốc (bên ngoài @media)
    &.is-open {
        @extend %animDropdownFadeIn;
    }

    &__step {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        @extend %flexItemFixed;
        width: 3.8rem;
        height: 4.6rem;
        border-radius: 1.2rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
        font-size: 1.3rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-color);
        @extend %transInteractive;

        &:hover {
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: var(--primary-color);
            background-color: var(--body-bg);
        }

        @include abs.screen(md) {
            display: none;
        }
    }

    &__list {
        display: flex;
        align-items: center;
        justify-content: flex-start;
        gap: 1rem;
        overflow-x: auto;
        overflow-y: hidden;
        padding: 0.3rem 0.2rem;
        scrollbar-width: none;
        cursor: grab;
        user-select: none;
        scroll-behavior: smooth;

        &::-webkit-scrollbar {
            display: none;
        }

        &.is-dragging {
            cursor: grabbing;
            scroll-behavior: auto;

            .util-primary-nav__link {
                pointer-events: none;
            }
        }
    }

    &__item {
        @extend %flexItemFixed;
    }

    &__link {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        gap: 0.9rem;
        height: 4.8rem;
        padding: 0 1.6rem 0 1rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 1.5rem;
        font-size: 1.4rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-color);
        -webkit-user-drag: none;
        @extend %textNoWrap;
        @extend %transInteractive;

        &:hover,
        &:focus-visible {
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            background-color: var(--body-bg);
            color: var(--primary-color);
            transform: translateY(-2px);

            .util-primary-nav__icon {
                animation: baziIconHoverBounce 0.85s ease-in-out infinite;
            }
        }

        &.is-active,
        &[aria-current="page"] {
            background-color: var(--primary-color);
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: #ffffff;
            box-shadow: 0 0.6rem 1.6rem var(--shadow-color);

            .util-primary-nav__icon-box {
                background-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2);
                border-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.35);
            }

            .util-primary-nav__icon {
                filter: brightness(0) invert(1);
            }
        }
    }

    &__icon-box {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        @extend %flexItemFixed;
        width: 3.4rem;
        height: 3.4rem;
        border-radius: 1rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        pointer-events: none;
        @extend %transTheme;
    }

    // Phóng to icon trên thanh Navbar lên 2.1rem sắc nét
    &__icon {
        width: 2.1rem;
        height: 2.1rem;
        object-fit: contain;
        filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
        pointer-events: none;
    }

    @include abs.screen(md) {
        display: none;
        padding-top: 0.8rem;

        &.is-open {
            display: block;
        }

        &__list {
            display: grid;
            grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
            overflow: visible;
            cursor: default;
            gap: 0.8rem;
        }

        &__link {
            width: 100%;
            justify-content: flex-start;
        }
    }

    @include abs.screen(sm) {
        &__list {
            grid-template-columns: 1fr;
        }
    }
}

.util-mobile-backdrop {
    @extend %fixedCover;
    z-index: 25;
    background-color: var(--overlay-bg);
    backdrop-filter: blur(2px);
    opacity: 0;
    visibility: hidden;
    @extend %transBackdrop;

    &.is-active {
        opacity: 1;
        visibility: visible;
    }
}

.util-sub-nav {
    display: inline-flex;
    max-width: 100%;
    padding: 0.55rem;
    background-color: var(--body-bg);
    border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 99.9rem;
    box-shadow: 0 0.6rem 1.8rem var(--shadow-color);

    &__list {
        display: flex;
        align-items: center;
        justify-content: center;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        gap: 0.5rem;
    }

    &__link {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        height: 3.8rem;
        padding: 0 1.8rem;
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        font-size: 1.35rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-muted);
        @extend %textNoWrap;
        @extend %transTheme;

        &:hover {
            color: var(--text-color);
        }

        &.is-active,
        &[aria-current="page"] {
            background-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: #ffffff;
            box-shadow: 0 0.4rem 1.2rem var(--shadow-color);
        }
    }
}

// ==========================================================================
// 2. KHUNG CARD CHUNG & HỆ THỐNG FORM / BENTO STEPPER
// ==========================================================================
.util-panel,
.util-subpanel {
    &[hidden] {
        display: none !important;
    }
}

.util-card {
    position: relative;
    overflow: visible;
    min-width: 0;
    @extend %flexColumn;
    gap: 2.2rem;
    padding: 2.6rem 2.8rem;
    background-color: var(--body-bg);
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 2.6rem;
    box-shadow: 0 0.6rem 2rem var(--shadow-color);
    @extend %transTheme;

    @include abs.screen(sm) {
        padding: 1.6rem;
        border-radius: 2rem;
        gap: 1.6rem;
    }

    &__head {
        @extend %flexBetween;
        align-items: flex-start;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        gap: 1.4rem;
        padding-bottom: 1.4rem;
        border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    }

    &__title {
        font-size: 2rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-color);
    }

    &__sub {
        margin-top: 0.35rem;
        font-size: 1.35rem;
        font-weight: 600;
        line-height: 1.5;
        color: var(--text-muted);
    }

    &__tag {
        padding: 0.5rem 1.5rem;
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        background-color: var(--primary-soft-bg, var(--bg-surface));
        border: 1.5px solid var(--primary-color);
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--primary-color);
        @extend %textNoWrap;
    }
}

.util-section-subtitle {
    font-size: 1.55rem;
    font-weight: 800;
    color: var(--text-color);
    margin-bottom: 1rem;
}

.util-form-grid {
    display: grid;
    gap: 1.4rem;
    align-items: end;

    &--2cols {
        grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            grid-template-columns: 1fr;
        }
    }

    &--4cols {
        grid-template-columns: repeat(4, minmax(0, 1fr));

        @include abs.screen(lg) {
            grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
        }

        @include abs.screen(sm) {
            grid-template-columns: 1fr;
        }
    }
}

.util-field {
    @extend %flexColumn;
    gap: 0.6rem;
    min-width: 0;

    &__label {
        font-size: 1.2rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        text-transform: uppercase;
        letter-spacing: 0.03em;
        color: var(--text-muted);
    }

    &__input {
        width: 100%;
        height: 4.8rem;
        padding: 0 1.5rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 1.4rem;
        font-size: 1.45rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--text-color);
        font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
```
