```scss
        @extend %transTheme;

        &:focus {
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            background-color: var(--body-bg);
            box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px var(--primary-soft-bg);
        }
    }

    &__hint {
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--primary-color);
    }
}

.util-btn {
    height: 4.8rem;
    padding: 0 2.4rem;
    border-radius: 1.4rem;
    font-size: 1.45rem;
    font-weight: 800;

    &--sm {
        height: 3.8rem;
        padding: 0 1.4rem;
        border-radius: 1.1rem;
        font-size: 1.25rem;
    }

    &__svg-icon {
        width: 2rem;
        height: 2rem;
        filter: brightness(0) invert(1);
    }

    &--primary {
        background-color: var(--primary-color);
        color: #ffffff;
        box-shadow: 0 0.6rem 1.6rem var(--shadow-color);

        &:hover:not(:disabled) {
            background-color: var(--primary-hover);
            transform: translateY(-2px);
        }
    }

    &--secondary {
        background-color: var(--primary-soft-bg, var(--bg-surface));
        border: 1.5px solid var(--primary-color);
        color: var(--primary-color);

        &:hover:not(:disabled) {
            background-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: #ffffff;
        }
    }

    &--outline {
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
        color: var(--text-color);

        &:hover:not(:disabled) {
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: var(--primary-color);
        }
    }

    &:disabled {
        opacity: 0.45;
        cursor: not-allowed;
    }
}

// Hộp Bento Stepper (− / +) thay thế input number thô cứng
.util-stepper-grid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr));
    gap: 1.2rem;

    &--2cols {
        grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
    }

    &--4cols {
        grid-template-columns: repeat(4, minmax(0, 1fr));

        @include abs.screen(lg) {
            grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
        }
    }

    @include abs.screen(sm) {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }
}

.util-stepper-box {
    @extend %flexColumn;
    gap: 0.8rem;
    padding: 1.4rem;
    background-color: var(--bg-surface);
    border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 1.8rem;
    text-align: center;
    @extend %transTheme;

    &:hover {
        border-color: var(--primary-color);
    }

    &__label {
        font-size: 1.15rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        text-transform: uppercase;
        letter-spacing: 0.04em;
        color: var(--text-muted);
    }

    &__ctrl {
        display: grid;
        grid-template-columns: 3.8rem 1fr 3.8rem;
        align-items: center;
        gap: 0.6rem;
    }

    &__btn {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        width: 3.8rem;
        height: 3.8rem;
        border-radius: 1.1rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        font-size: 1.8rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-color);
        @extend %transInteractive;

        &:hover {
            background-color: var(--primary-color);
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: #ffffff;
        }
    }

    &__input {
        width: 100%;
        text-align: center;
        font-size: 2.4rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--primary-color);
        font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
        appearance: textfield;

        &::-webkit-outer-spin-button,
        &::-webkit-inner-spin-button {
            appearance: none;
            margin: 0;
        }
    }
}

// ==========================================================================
// 3. MỤC 1: ĐỒNG HỒ BẤM GIỜ, ĐẾM NGƯỢC BENTO & BÁO THỨC ĐA NĂNG
// ==========================================================================
.util-timer-bento {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: 1.05fr 1fr;
    align-items: stretch;
    gap: 2rem;

    @include abs.screen(lg) {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }

    &__left,
    &__right {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        justify-content: space-between;
        gap: 1.4rem;
        min-width: 0;
    }
}

.util-clock-stage {
    flex: 1;
    @extend %flexColumnCenterAll;
    gap: 1.2rem;
    padding: 3.2rem 2rem;
    background-color: var(--bg-surface);
    border: 2px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 2.4rem;
    text-align: center;

    &--compact {
        padding: 2.4rem 1.8rem;
    }

    // Đã fix lỗi chữ quá sát viền ở Ảnh 2
    &__tag {
        display: inline-flex;
        align-items: center;
        justify-content: center;
        min-height: 3.4rem;
        padding: 0.6rem 1.8rem;
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1.5px solid var(--primary-color);
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        line-height: 1.3;
        color: var(--primary-color);
        text-transform: uppercase;
        letter-spacing: 0.05em;
    }

    &__digits {
        font-size: clamp(4.4rem, 7.5vw, 7.8rem);
        font-weight: 800;
        line-height: 1;
        color: var(--text-color);
        font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
        letter-spacing: -0.02em;

        small {
            font-size: 0.48em;
            color: var(--primary-color);
            margin-left: 0.2rem;
        }
    }
}

.util-sound-bento {
    @extend %flexColumn;
    gap: 1.4rem;
    padding: 1.6rem 1.8rem;
    background-color: var(--bg-surface);
    border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 2rem;

    &__row {
        @extend %flexBetween;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        gap: 1.2rem;
        padding-top: 1.2rem;
        border-top: 1px dashed var(--border-color);
    }

    &__info {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 0.25rem;
        min-width: 0;
    }

    &__name {
        font-size: 1.35rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--primary-color);
        @extend %textEllipsis;
    }

    &__actions {
        @extend %flexWrapGap;
        gap: 0.8rem;
    }
}

.util-clock-controls {
    @extend %flexCenter;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    gap: 1.2rem;
}

.util-preset-chips {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(4, minmax(0, 1fr));
    gap: 0.8rem;

    @include abs.screen(sm) {
        grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
    }
}

.util-preset-chip {
    height: 4rem;
    border-radius: 1.2rem;
    background-color: var(--bg-surface);
    border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
    font-size: 1.35rem;
    font-weight: 800;
    color: var(--text-color);
    @extend %transInteractive;

    &:hover,
    &.is-active {
        background-color: var(--primary-color);
        border-color: var(--primary-color);
        color: #ffffff;
        transform: translateY(-2px);
    }
}

.util-data-table {
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 1.8rem;
    overflow: hidden;

    &__head,
    &__row {
        display: grid;
        align-items: center;
        gap: 1.2rem;
        padding: 1.3rem 1.8rem;
    }

    &__head--3cols,
    &__row--3cols {
        grid-template-columns: 0.8fr 1.2fr 1.2fr;
    }

    &__head--4cols,
    &__row--4cols {
        grid-template-columns: 0.5fr 1.5fr 1fr 1fr;
    }

    &__head {
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        font-size: 1.2rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        text-transform: uppercase;
        color: var(--text-muted);
    }

    &__row {
        border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        font-size: 1.4rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;

        &:last-child {
            border-bottom: none;
        }
    }

    &__empty {
        padding: 2.4rem;
        text-align: center;
        font-size: 1.35rem;
        color: var(--text-muted);
    }
}

.util-alarm-ring-banner {
    @extend %flexBetween;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    gap: 1.4rem;
    padding: 1.8rem 2.2rem;
    background-color: var(--primary-soft-bg);
    border: 2px solid var(--primary-color);
    border-radius: 2rem;
    animation: pulse 1.2s ease-in-out infinite;

    &[hidden] {
        display: none !important;
    }

    &__info {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 0.3rem;

        strong {
            font-size: 2rem;
            font-weight: 800;
            color: var(--text-color);
        }
    }

    &__tag {
        font-size: 1.2rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        text-transform: uppercase;
        color: var(--primary-color);
    }

    &__btns {
        @extend %flexWrapGap;
        gap: 1rem;
    }
}

.util-alarms-list {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
    gap: 1.4rem;

    @include abs.screen(md) {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }
}

.util-alarm-item {
    @extend %flexBetween;
    gap: 1.4rem;
    padding: 1.8rem 2rem;
    background-color: var(--bg-surface);
    border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 2rem;
    @extend %transInteractive;

    &.is-disabled {
        opacity: 0.52;
    }

    &:hover {
        border-color: var(--primary-color);
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
    }

    &__time {
        font-size: 2.8rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        line-height: 1;
        color: var(--primary-color);
        font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
    }

    &__title {
        display: block;
        margin-top: 0.4rem;
        font-size: 1.45rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-color);
    }

    &__meta {
        display: block;
        margin-top: 0.25rem;
        font-size: 1.2rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--text-muted);
    }

    &__right {
        @extend %flexItemCenter;
        gap: 1rem;
    }

    // Nút gạt Bật/Tắt Báo thức
    &__toggle {
        position: relative;
        width: 4.8rem;
        height: 2.6rem;
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        background-color: var(--border-color);
        @extend %transTheme;

        &::after {
            content: "";
            position: absolute;
            top: 0.3rem;
            left: 0.3rem;
            width: 2rem;
            height: 2rem;
            border-radius: 50%;
            background-color: #ffffff;
            transition: transform 0.25s ease;
        }

        &.is-on {
            background-color: var(--primary-color);

            &::after {
                transform: translateX(2.2rem);
            }
        }
    }

    // Nút Icon Thùng Rác SVG thay cho chữ "Xóa"
    &__del {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        width: 4rem;
        height: 4rem;
        border-radius: 1.2rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        color: var(--color-danger);
        @extend %transInteractive;

        svg {
            width: 1.9rem;
            height: 1.9rem;
        }

        &:hover {
            background-color: var(--color-danger);
            border-color: var(--color-danger);
            color: #ffffff;
            transform: scale(1.06);
        }
    }
}

// ==========================================================================
// 4. MỤC 2 & 3: ĐỔI NGÀY BENTO DASHBOARD & 24 TIẾT KHÍ ĐỒNG BỘ TRANG DAY
// ==========================================================================
.util-conv-dashboard {
    @extend %flexColumn;
    gap: 1.8rem;
}

.util-season-filters {
    @extend %flexWrapGap;
    gap: 0.8rem;
}

.util-subfilter-btn {
    @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
    height: 3.8rem;
    padding: 0 1.6rem;
    background-color: var(--bg-surface);
    border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 99.9rem;
    font-size: 1.3rem;
    font-weight: 800;
    color: var(--text-color);
    @extend %transInteractive;

    &:hover,
    &.is-active {
        background-color: var(--primary-color);
        border-color: var(--primary-color);
        color: #ffffff;
        transform: translateY(-1px);
    }
}

.util-year-Step-bar {
    @extend %flexItemCenter;
    gap: 0.8rem;
    padding: 0.45rem;
    background-color: var(--bg-surface);
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 1.4rem;
}

.util-year-step {
    &__btn {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        width: 3.6rem;
        height: 3.6rem;
        border-radius: 1rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        font-weight: 800;

        &:hover {
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: var(--primary-color);
        }
    }

    &__label {
        padding: 0 0.8rem;
        font-size: 1.45rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-color);
    }

    &__today {
        height: 3.8rem;
        padding: 0 1.6rem;
        border-radius: 1.1rem;
        background-color: var(--primary-color);
        color: #ffffff;
        font-size: 1.3rem;
        font-weight: 800;
    }
}

.util-solarterms-grid,
.util-catalog-grid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr));
    align-items: stretch;
    gap: 1.5rem;

    @include abs.screen(lg) {
        grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
    }

    @include abs.screen(sm) {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }
}

// Card Tiết Khí & Từ Điển Cổ Học thiết kế giống hệt trang Day (Có Watermark + 6 Animation Thời tiết + Hover Tooltip + Click Modal)
.util-bento-item {
    position: relative;
    overflow: visible;
    min-width: 0;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    justify-content: space-between;
    height: 100%;
    min-height: 16.8rem;
    gap: 1rem;
    padding: 1.8rem 2rem;
    background-color: var(--bg-surface);
    border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 2.2rem;
    text-align: left;
    cursor: pointer;
    @extend %transInteractive;

    &.is-current-highlight {
        background-color: var(--primary-soft-bg, var(--body-bg));
        border: 2px solid var(--primary-color);
        box-shadow: 0 0.8rem 2.2rem var(--shadow-color);
    }

    &__bg-wrap {
        @extend %absCover;
        border-radius: inherit;
        overflow: hidden;
        pointer-events: none;
        z-index: 1;
    }

    &__watermark {
        position: absolute;
        right: -0.6rem;
        bottom: -0.6rem;
        width: 6.4rem;
        height: 6.4rem;
        object-fit: contain;
        opacity: 0.11;
        filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
        transform: rotate(-10deg);
        @extend %transWatermark;
    }

    &:hover {
        border-color: var(--primary-color);
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        transform: translateY(-3px);
        box-shadow: 0 1rem 2.4rem var(--shadow-color);

        .util-bento-item__watermark {
            opacity: 0.25;
            transform: scale(1.1) rotate(0deg);
        }
    }

    // Kế thừa 6 nhóm Animation Thời tiết & 6 nhóm Lục Diệu từ trang Day
    &[data-term-anim="wind"]:hover &__icon-box img,
    &.is-current-highlight[data-term-anim="wind"] &__icon-box img {
        @extend %animTermWind;
    }
    &[data-term-anim="rain"]:hover &__icon-box img,
    &.is-current-highlight[data-term-anim="rain"] &__icon-box img {
        @extend %animTermRain;
    }
    &[data-term-anim="sun"]:hover &__icon-box img,
    &.is-current-highlight[data-term-anim="sun"] &__icon-box img {
        @extend %animTermSun;
    }
    &[data-term-anim="thunder"]:hover &__icon-box img,
    &.is-current-highlight[data-term-anim="thunder"] &__icon-box img {
        @extend %animTermThunder;
    }
    &[data-term-anim="sprout"]:hover &__icon-box img,
    &.is-current-highlight[data-term-anim="sprout"] &__icon-box img {
        @extend %animTermSprout;
    }
    &[data-term-anim="snow"]:hover &__icon-box img,
    &.is-current-highlight[data-term-anim="snow"] &__icon-box img {
        @extend %animTermSnow;
    }

    &:hover &__icon-box img {
        animation: baziIconHoverBounce 0.85s ease-in-out infinite;
    }

    &__top {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        @extend %flexBetween;
        gap: 1rem;
    }

    &__icon-box {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        @extend %flexItemFixed;
        width: 4.6rem;
        height: 4.6rem;
        border-radius: 1.4rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        box-shadow: 0 0.4rem 1rem var(--shadow-color);

        img,
        svg {
            width: 2.7rem;
            height: 2.7rem;
            object-fit: contain;
        }

        &--filtered img {
            filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
        }
    }

    &__badge {
        padding: 0.35rem 1.1rem;
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        font-size: 1.15rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--primary-color);
        font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
    }

    &__body {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 0.3rem;
    }

    &__title {
        font-size: 1.85rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-color);
    }

    &__sub {
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--primary-color);
    }

    &__desc {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        font-size: 1.3rem;
        line-height: 1.55;
        color: var(--text-muted);
    }

    &__foot {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        @extend %flexBetween;
        margin-top: auto;
        padding-top: 0.8rem;
        border-top: 1px dashed var(--border-color);
        font-size: 1.2rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--primary-color);
    }
}

// ==========================================================================
// 5. MỤC 4: GIEO QUẺ ĐỒNG XU 3D, MOUSE ENTROPY & BÀI TAROT XÒE QUẠT
// ==========================================================================
.util-divination-hero {
    @extend %flexColumnCenterAll;
    gap: 1.2rem;
    text-align: center;
    padding-bottom: 2rem;
    border-bottom: 1px dashed var(--border-color);
}

.util-entropy-meter {
    width: 100%;
    max-width: 42rem;
    @extend %flexColumn;
    gap: 0.45rem;

    &__label {
        font-size: 1.2rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--primary-color);
    }

    &__track {
        width: 100%;
        height: 0.8rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        overflow: hidden;
    }

    &__fill {
        height: 100%;
        background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--primary-color), var(--secondary-color));
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        transition: width 0.25s ease;
    }
}

// 3 Đồng Xu Cổ Thông Bảo nhảy & xoay 3D
.util-coins-stage {
    @extend %flexCenter;
    gap: 2.2rem;
    padding: 1rem 0;
    perspective: 800px;
}

.util-coin {
    position: relative;
    @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
    width: 6.4rem;
    height: 6.4rem;
    border-radius: 50%;
    background: radial-gradient(circle at 30% 30%, #fde047, #ca8a04 75%, #854d0e);
    border: 3px solid #fef08a;
    box-shadow: 0 0.8rem 2rem rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.22);
    color: #713f12;
    font-size: 1.1rem;
    font-weight: 800;
    transform-style: preserve-3d;

    &__hole {
        width: 1.8rem;
        height: 1.8rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 2px solid #a16207;
        border-radius: 0.3rem;
    }

    span {
        position: absolute;
        bottom: 0.4rem;
        font-size: 0.95rem;
    }

    &.is-tossing {
        animation: utilCoinToss3D 0.9s cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1) infinite;
    }
}

@keyframes utilCoinToss3D {
    0% {
        transform: translateY(0) rotateY(0deg) rotateX(0deg) scale(1);
    }
    45% {
        transform: translateY(-2.6rem) rotateY(540deg) rotateX(180deg) scale(1.14);
    }
    100% {
        transform: translateY(0) rotateY(1080deg) rotateX(360deg) scale(1);
    }
}

.util-iching-bento {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: 28rem 1fr;
    align-items: stretch;
    gap: 2.2rem;

    @include abs.screen(lg) {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }

    &.is-sliding {
        animation: utilSlotSlide 0.35s ease-in-out infinite;
    }
}

@keyframes utilSlotSlide {
    0%,
    100% {
        transform: translateY(0);
        opacity: 1;
    }
    50% {
        transform: translateY(-0.8rem);
        opacity: 0.55;
    }
}

.util-iching-master-card {
    @extend %flexColumnCenterAll;
    justify-content: space-between;
    gap: 1.4rem;
    padding: 2.4rem 2rem;
    background-color: var(--bg-surface);
    border: 2px solid var(--primary-color);
    border-radius: 2.4rem;
    text-align: center;

    &__sub {
        font-size: 1.3rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--text-muted);
    }
}

.util-iching-result {
    &__visual {
        width: 100%;
        max-width: 18rem;
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 1rem;
        padding: 1.8rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
        border-radius: 1.8rem;
    }

    &__num {
        padding: 0.4rem 1.4rem;
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        background-color: var(--primary-color);
        color: #ffffff;
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        font-weight: 800;
    }

    &__name {
        font-size: 2.2rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        line-height: 1.25;
        color: var(--primary-color);
    }
}

.util-hex-line {
    display: flex;
    justify-content: space-between;
    gap: 1.4rem;
    height: 1.3rem;

    span {
        flex: 1;
        height: 100%;
        background-color: var(--text-color);
        border-radius: 0.4rem;
    }

    &--yang span {
        background-color: var(--primary-color);
    }
}

.util-iching-cards-stack {
    @extend %flexColumn;
    justify-content: space-between;
    gap: 1.4rem;
}

.util-div-detail-card {
    position: relative;
    overflow: hidden;
    @extend %flexColumn;
    gap: 0.8rem;
    padding: 1.8rem 2rem;
    background-color: var(--bg-surface);
    border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 2rem;
    @extend %transInteractive;

    &--highlight {
        background-color: var(--primary-soft-bg, var(--bg-surface));
        border-color: var(--primary-color);
    }

    &:hover {
        border-color: var(--primary-color);
        transform: translateY(-2px);
    }

    &__head {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        @extend %flexItemCenter;
        gap: 1rem;

        strong {
            font-size: 1.65rem;
            font-weight: 800;
            color: var(--text-color);
        }
    }

    &__icon {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        @extend %flexItemFixed;
        width: 3.8rem;
        height: 3.8rem;
        border-radius: 1.1rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);

        img {
            width: 2.2rem;
            height: 2.2rem;
            filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
        }
    }

    p {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        font-size: 1.4rem;
        line-height: 1.6;
        color: var(--text-color);
    }

    &__watermark {
        position: absolute;
        right: -0.6rem;
        bottom: -0.6rem;
        width: 5.8rem;
        height: 5.8rem;
        opacity: 0.09;
        filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
        pointer-events: none;
    }
}

// Xấp bài Tarot 7 lá xòe quạt
.util-tarot-fan {
    display: flex;
    align-items: flex-end;
    justify-content: center;
    padding: 3.4rem 1.6rem 2.2rem;
    min-height: 22rem;
    width: 100%;
    overflow: visible;

    @include abs.screen(md) {
        padding: 2.6rem 0.8rem 1.6rem;
        min-height: 17.5rem;
    }

    &[data-deck-state="shuffling"] {
        pointer-events: none;

        .util-tarot-back {
            animation: tarotShufflePulse 0.68s cubic-bezier(0.34, 1.56, 0.64, 1) both;
        }
    }
}

.util-tarot-back {
    --fan-angle: 0deg;
    --fan-y: 0rem;
    --shuffle-dx: 0px;

    position: relative;
    width: 10.6rem;
    aspect-ratio: 7 / 12;
    margin-left: -3.2rem;
    border-radius: 1.1rem;
    overflow: hidden;
    background: transparent;
    border: 2px solid transparent;
    box-shadow: 0 0.8rem 2rem rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.22);
    transform-origin: bottom center;
    transform: translateY(var(--fan-y)) rotate(var(--fan-angle));
    transition:
        transform 0.35s cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1),
        box-shadow 0.3s ease,
        border-color 0.25s ease,
        filter 0.25s ease;
    cursor: pointer;

    &:first-child {
        margin-left: 0;
    }

    img {
        width: 100%;
        height: 100%;
        object-fit: cover;
        display: block;
        pointer-events: none;
    }

    // Góc xòe vòng cung tự nhiên cho 9 lá bài
    &[data-fan-idx="0"] {
        --fan-angle: -20deg;
        --fan-y: 2.6rem;
        --shuffle-dx: 120px;
        z-index: 1;
    }
    &[data-fan-idx="1"] {
        --fan-angle: -15deg;
        --fan-y: 1.5rem;
        --shuffle-dx: 90px;
        z-index: 2;
    }
    &[data-fan-idx="2"] {
        --fan-angle: -10deg;
        --fan-y: 0.7rem;
        --shuffle-dx: 60px;
        z-index: 3;
    }
    &[data-fan-idx="3"] {
        --fan-angle: -5deg;
        --fan-y: 0.2rem;
        --shuffle-dx: 30px;
        z-index: 4;
    }
    &[data-fan-idx="4"] {
        --fan-angle: 0deg;
        --fan-y: 0rem;
        --shuffle-dx: 0px;
        z-index: 5;
    }
    &[data-fan-idx="5"] {
        --fan-angle: 5deg;
        --fan-y: 0.2rem;
        --shuffle-dx: -30px;
        z-index: 4;
    }
    &[data-fan-idx="6"] {
        --fan-angle: 10deg;
        --fan-y: 0.7rem;
        --shuffle-dx: -60px;
        z-index: 3;
    }
    &[data-fan-idx="7"] {
        --fan-angle: 15deg;
        --fan-y: 1.5rem;
        --shuffle-dx: -90px;
        z-index: 2;
    }
    &[data-fan-idx="8"] {
        --fan-angle: 20deg;
        --fan-y: 2.6rem;
        --shuffle-dx: -120px;
        z-index: 1;
    }

    &:hover,
    &:focus-visible {
        z-index: 20;
        border-color: #facc15;
        transform: translateY(calc(var(--fan-y) - 2.2rem)) rotate(var(--fan-angle)) scale(1.08);
        box-shadow:
            0 1.4rem 3rem rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.35),
            0 0 1.6rem rgba(250, 204, 21, 0.55);
    }

    &.is-picked {
        z-index: 18;
        border-color: var(--primary-color);
        transform: translateY(calc(var(--fan-y) - 2.6rem)) rotate(var(--fan-angle)) scale(1.1);
        box-shadow:
            0 1.6rem 3.2rem var(--shadow-color),
            0 0 1.8rem rgba(250, 204, 21, 0.65);
    }

    // Tablet & Mobile: Ẩn 2 lá rìa ngoài để giữ đúng 7 lá to, thoáng, dễ chạm
    @include abs.screen(md) {
        width: 8.6rem;
        margin-left: -3rem;

        &--edge {
            display: none;
        }

        &[data-fan-idx="1"] {
            margin-left: 0;
        }
    }

    @include abs.screen(sm) {
        width: 7.2rem;
        margin-left: -2.6rem;
    }
}

// Badge nổi bật phân biệt lá bài Ngược
#tr-card-orientation[data-orientation="reversed"] {
    background-color: var(--color-danger, #dc2626);
}

// Ảnh Tarot phóng to bên trong Singleton Modal (#util-detail-modal)
.util-modal-tarot-img {
    width: 15.4rem !important;
    height: auto !important;
    aspect-ratio: 7 / 12;
    border-radius: 1.4rem;
    box-shadow: 0 1rem 2.4rem var(--shadow-color);

    &.is-reversed {
        transform: rotate(180deg);
    }
}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
    .util-tarot-back,
    .util-tarot-stage__inner,
    .util-tarot-bento.is-sliding .util-iching-cards-stack {
        animation: none !important;
        transition-duration: 0.01ms !important;
    }
}

.util-tarot-bento {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: 24rem 1fr;
    align-items: start;
    gap: 2.6rem;

    &.is-sliding .util-iching-cards-stack {
        animation: tarotResultSlideIn 0.45s cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1);
    }

    @include abs.screen(md) {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
        justify-items: center;
    }
}

.util-tarot-stage {
    @extend %flexColumnCenterAll;
    gap: 1.2rem;
    width: 100%;
    max-width: 24rem;
    perspective: 120rem;
    cursor: pointer;

    &__inner {
        position: relative;
        width: 100%;
        aspect-ratio: 7 / 12;
        transform-style: preserve-3d;
        transition: transform 0.72s cubic-bezier(0.34, 1.32, 0.64, 1);
        border-radius: 1.8rem;
    }

    &[data-flipped="true"] &__inner {
        transform: rotateY(180deg);
    }

    &:hover &__inner {
        filter: drop-shadow(0 1.4rem 2.8rem var(--shadow-color));
    }

    &__face {
        position: absolute;
        inset: 0;
        backface-visibility: hidden;
        -webkit-backface-visibility: hidden;
        border-radius: 1.8rem;
        overflow: hidden;
        border: 2px solid var(--primary-color);
        box-shadow: 0 1.2rem 2.8rem var(--shadow-color);
        background-color: transparent;

        img {
            width: 100%;
            height: 100%;
            object-fit: cover;
            display: block;
            transition: transform 0.5s ease;
        }

        &--front {
            transform: rotateY(180deg);
        }
    }

    // Tự động xoay ngược 180 độ khi rút trúng lá bài Ngược (Reversed)
    &[data-orientation="reversed"] &__face--front img {
        transform: rotate(180deg);
    }

    &__hint {
        @extend %flexColumnCenterAll;
        gap: 0.3rem;
        text-align: center;

        span {
            padding: 0.35rem 1.2rem;
            border-radius: 99.9rem;
            background-color: var(--bg-surface);
            border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
            font-size: 1.2rem;
            font-weight: 800;
            color: var(--primary-color);
        }

        small {
            font-size: 1.15rem;
            font-weight: 600;
            color: var(--text-muted);
        }
    }
}

.util-tarot-card-art {
    @extend %flexColumnCenterAll;
    justify-content: space-between;
    min-height: 32rem;
    padding: 2.6rem 2rem;
    background: linear-gradient(145deg, var(--primary-color), var(--primary-active));
    border: 3px solid #ffffff;
    border-radius: 2.4rem;
    color: #ffffff;
    box-shadow: 0 1.4rem 3rem var(--shadow-color);
    text-align: center;

    &__roman {
        padding: 0.35rem 1.4rem;
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        background-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2);
        font-size: 1.4rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        letter-spacing: 0.08em;
    }

    &__svg-box {
        width: 9.6rem;
        height: 9.6rem;
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;

        svg {
            width: 100%;
            height: 100%;
            stroke: #ffffff;
        }
    }

    &__title {
        font-size: 1.6rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        letter-spacing: 0.08em;
        text-transform: uppercase;
    }
}

// ==========================================================================
// 6. MỤC 5: NGÀY NÀY NĂM XƯA (CARD CÓ BACKGROUND ẢNH + HOVER PROFILE + PHÂN TRANG)
// ==========================================================================
.util-history-controls {
    @extend %flexItemCenter;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    gap: 1.2rem;
}

.util-history-grid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr));
    gap: 1.8rem;

    @include abs.screen(lg) {
        grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
    }

    @include abs.screen(sm) {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }
}

.util-history-card {
    position: relative;
    overflow: visible;
    min-height: 25rem;
    border-radius: 2.2rem;
    border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
    cursor: pointer;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    justify-content: flex-end;
    padding: 1.8rem;
    color: #ffffff;
    @extend %transInteractive;

    &__media {
        @extend %absCover;
        border-radius: inherit;
        overflow: hidden;
        z-index: 1;

        img {
            @extend %imgCover;
            filter: brightness(0.68);
            transition:
                transform 0.5s ease,
                filter 0.4s ease;
        }

        &::after {
            content: "";
            @extend %absCover;
            background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.15) 0%, rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.86) 100%);
        }
    }

    &:hover {
        border-color: var(--primary-color);
        transform: translateY(-4px);
        box-shadow: 0 1.2rem 2.8rem var(--shadow-color);

        .util-history-card__media img {
            transform: scale(1.06);
            filter: brightness(0.88);
        }
    }

    &__top {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        @extend %flexBetween;
        margin-bottom: auto;
    }

    &__date-pill {
        padding: 0.4rem 1.2rem;
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        background-color: var(--primary-color);
        color: #ffffff;
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        font-weight: 800;
    }

    &__scope-pill {
        padding: 0.35rem 1rem;
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        background-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2);
        backdrop-filter: blur(6px);
        font-size: 1.15rem;
        font-weight: 700;
    }

    &__body {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 0.45rem;
    }

    &__title {
        font-size: 2rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        line-height: 1.25;
        color: #ffffff;
    }

    &__sub {
        font-size: 1.3rem;
        line-height: 1.45;
        color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.88);
        @extend %textClamp2;
    }
}

.util-pagination {
    @extend %flexCenter;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    gap: 0.8rem;
    padding-top: 0.8rem;

    &__btn {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        min-width: 4rem;
        height: 4rem;
        padding: 0 1.2rem;
        border-radius: 1.2rem;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
        font-size: 1.35rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-color);
        @extend %transInteractive;

        &:hover,
        &.is-active {
            background-color: var(--primary-color);
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
            color: #ffffff;
        }
    }
}

// ==========================================================================
// 7. MỤC 6, 7, 8, 9: HOROSCOPE LINH VẬT, TÍNH THỜI GIAN, THẦN SỐ HỌC & NHỊP SINH HỌC
// ==========================================================================
.util-inline-custom-pickers {
    @extend %flexItemCenter;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    gap: 1rem;
}

.util-date-picker-card {
    @extend %flexColumn;
    gap: 1rem;
    padding: 1.8rem 2rem;
    background-color: var(--bg-surface);
    border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 2rem;
}

.util-horoscope-hero {
    position: relative;
    overflow: hidden;
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: auto 1fr;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 2.2rem;
    padding: 2.4rem 2.6rem;
    background-color: var(--primary-soft-bg, var(--bg-surface));
    border: 1.5px solid var(--primary-color);
    border-radius: 2.4rem;

    @include abs.screen(sm) {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
        text-align: center;
    }

    &__bg-wrap {
        @extend %absCover;
        border-radius: inherit;
        overflow: hidden;
        pointer-events: none;
        z-index: 1;

        img {
            position: absolute;
            right: -1rem;
            bottom: -1rem;
            width: 14rem;
            height: 14rem;
            object-fit: contain;
            opacity: 0.12;
            filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
            transform: rotate(-10deg);
            @extend %transWatermark;
        }
    }

    &:hover &__bg-wrap img {
        opacity: 0.22;
        transform: scale(1.06) rotate(0deg);
    }

    &__badge {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        width: 8.8rem;
        height: 8.8rem;
        border-radius: 2.4rem;
        background-color: var(--primary-color);
        color: #ffffff;
        margin: 0 auto;
        box-shadow: 0 0.8rem 2rem var(--shadow-color);

        svg,
        img {
            width: 5.2rem;
            height: 5.2rem;
            filter: brightness(0) invert(1);
        }
    }

    &__main {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 0.7rem;
    }

    &__pills {
        @extend %flexWrapGap;
    }

    &__pill {
        padding: 0.35rem 1.2rem;
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        font-size: 1.2rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--primary-color);
    }

    &__name {
        font-size: 2.4rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-color);
    }

    &__traits {
        font-size: 1.4rem;
        line-height: 1.6;
        color: var(--text-color);
    }

    &__daily {
        padding: 1.2rem 1.5rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border-radius: 1.4rem;
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        font-size: 1.35rem;
        line-height: 1.55;
    }

    &__watermark {
        position: absolute;
        right: -1rem;
        bottom: -1rem;
        width: 14rem;
        height: 14rem;
        opacity: 0.12;
        pointer-events: none;
        z-index: 1;

        svg,
        img {
            width: 100%;
            height: 100%;
            filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
        }
    }
}

.util-horoscope-grid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(4, minmax(0, 1fr));
    gap: 1.3rem;

    @include abs.screen(lg) {
        grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr));
    }

    @include abs.screen(sm) {
        grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
    }
}

// 6 Bento Stat Cards lớn cho Tính Thời Gian
.util-diff-stats-grid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr));
    gap: 1.4rem;

    @include abs.screen(md) {
        grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
    }

    @include abs.screen(sm) {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }
}

.util-diff-stat {
    position: relative;
    overflow: hidden;
    @extend %flexColumn;
    justify-content: space-between;
    min-height: 13.6rem;
    gap: 0.6rem;
    padding: 2rem 2.2rem;
    background-color: var(--bg-surface);
    border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 2.2rem;
    @extend %transInteractive;

    &--highlight {
        background-color: var(--primary-soft-bg, var(--body-bg));
        border: 2px solid var(--primary-color);
    }

    &:hover {
        border-color: var(--primary-color);
        transform: translateY(-2px);
    }

    &__tag {
        width: fit-content;
        padding: 0.25rem 0.9rem;
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        font-size: 1.15rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-muted);
    }

    strong {
        font-size: 3.6rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        line-height: 1;
        color: var(--primary-color);
        font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
    }

    span:last-child {
        font-size: 1.35rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-color);
    }
}

.util-date-result-banner {
    @extend %flexBetween;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    gap: 1.6rem;
    padding: 2.2rem 2.4rem;
    background-color: var(--primary-soft-bg, var(--bg-surface));
    border: 1.5px solid var(--primary-color);
    border-radius: 2.2rem;

    &__main {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 0.45rem;
    }

    &__tag {
        font-size: 1.2rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        text-transform: uppercase;
        color: var(--primary-color);
    }

    &__solar {
        font-size: 2.2rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-color);
    }

    &__lunar {
        font-size: 1.4rem;
        font-weight: 600;
        color: var(--text-muted);
    }
}

// Thần số học Pythagoras & Nhịp sinh học
.util-num-hero {
    @extend %flexColumn;
    justify-content: space-between;
    height: 100%;
    gap: 1.6rem;
    padding: 2.4rem;
    background-color: var(--bg-surface);
    border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 2.2rem;

    &__top {
        @extend %flexItemCenter;
        gap: 1.8rem;
    }

    &__circle {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        @extend %flexItemFixed;
        width: 7.6rem;
        height: 7.6rem;
        border-radius: 2.2rem;
        background-color: var(--primary-color);
        color: #ffffff;
        font-size: 4rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        box-shadow: 0 0.8rem 2rem var(--shadow-color);
    }

    &__eyebrow {
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--primary-color);
    }

    &__title {
        font-size: 2.1rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-color);
    }

    &__desc {
        font-size: 1.4rem;
        line-height: 1.6;
        color: var(--text-color);
    }
}

.util-num-Cycles-grid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr));
    gap: 1rem;

    @include abs.screen(sm) {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }
}

.util-num-cycle-box {
    position: relative;
    overflow: visible;
    @extend %flexColumn;
    gap: 0.25rem;
    padding: 1.3rem;
    background-color: var(--body-bg);
    border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 1.4rem;
    cursor: pointer;
    @extend %transInteractive;

    &:hover {
        border-color: var(--primary-color);
        transform: translateY(-2px);
    }

    span:first-child {
        font-size: 1.15rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--text-muted);
    }

    strong {
        font-size: 2rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--primary-color);
    }

    small {
        font-size: 1.2rem;
        font-weight: 600;
        color: var(--text-color);
    }
}

.util-birth-chart-box {
    @extend %flexColumn;
    justify-content: space-between;
    height: 100%;
    gap: 1.2rem;
    padding: 2.2rem;
    background-color: var(--bg-surface);
    border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 2.2rem;

    &__note {
        font-size: 1.3rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--primary-color);
    }
}

.util-pythagoras-grid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr));
    gap: 0.8rem;
}

.util-pythagoras-cell {
    position: relative;
    overflow: visible;
    @extend %flexColumnCenterAll;
    min-height: 7.4rem;
    padding: 0.8rem;
    background-color: var(--body-bg);
    border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 1.4rem;
    cursor: pointer;
    @extend %transInteractive;

    &.has-val {
        border-color: var(--primary-color);
        background-color: var(--primary-soft-bg);
    }

    &:hover {
        border-color: var(--primary-color);
        transform: translateY(-2px);
    }

    small {
        font-size: 1.05rem;
        color: var(--text-muted);
    }

    strong {
        font-size: 2rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--primary-color);
        letter-spacing: 0.08em;
    }
}

.util-num-month-grid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(7, minmax(0, 1fr));
    gap: 0.8rem;
}

.util-num-day-cell {
    @extend %flexColumnCenterAll;
    gap: 0.3rem;
    min-height: 7.4rem;
    padding: 0.8rem;
    background-color: var(--bg-surface);
    border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 1.4rem;
    @extend %transInteractive;

    small {
        font-size: 1.2rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--text-muted);
    }

    strong {
        font-size: 2.2rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--primary-color);
    }

    &.is-muted {
        opacity: 0.35;
    }

    &:hover,
    &.is-active {
        background-color: var(--primary-color);
        border-color: var(--primary-color);
        color: #ffffff;

        small,
        strong {
            color: #ffffff;
        }
    }
}

.util-bio-stats-grid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(4, minmax(0, 1fr));
    gap: 1.2rem;

    @include abs.screen(lg) {
        grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
    }

    @include abs.screen(sm) {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }
}

.util-bio-stat-card {
    position: relative;
    overflow: visible;
    @extend %flexColumn;
    gap: 0.5rem;
    padding: 1.6rem 1.8rem;
    background-color: var(--bg-surface);
    border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 1.8rem;
    cursor: pointer;
    @extend %transInteractive;

    &:hover {
        border-color: var(--primary-color);
        transform: translateY(-2px);
    }

    &__head {
        @extend %flexBetween;
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-muted);
    }

    &__val {
        font-size: 2.8rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
    }

    &__advice {
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--text-color);
    }
}

// Hiệu ứng vẽ đường sóng Biorhythm SVG mượt mà
.util-bio-chart-stage {
    width: 100%;
    padding: 1.8rem;
    background-color: var(--bg-surface);
    border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 2.2rem;
    overflow-x: auto;

    svg {
        width: 100%;
        min-width: 68rem;
        height: auto;
    }

    .util-bio-wave-path {
        stroke-dasharray: 1400;
        stroke-dashoffset: 1400;
        animation: utilWaveDraw 1.5s cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1) forwards;
    }
}

@keyframes utilWaveDraw {
    to {
        stroke-dashoffset: 0;
    }
}

.util-bio-legend {
    @extend %flexWrapGap;
    justify-content: center;
    gap: 1.8rem;
    font-size: 1.3rem;

    &__item {
        @extend %flexItemCenter;
        gap: 0.6rem;
    }

    &__dot {
        width: 1.2rem;
        height: 1.2rem;
        border-radius: 50%;

        &--phys {
            background-color: #2563eb;
        }
        &--emo {
            background-color: #10b981;
        }
        &--intel {
            background-color: #f97316;
        }
        &--intuit {
            background-color: #8b5cf6;
        }
    }
}

.util-search-wrap {
    flex: 1 1 32rem;
    max-width: 42rem;
}

// Khung Smart Media bên trong Singleton Modal (#util-detail-modal)
.util-modal-dialog {
    max-width: 78rem;
    max-height: calc(100vh - 3.2rem);
    overflow-y: auto;
}

.util-modal-media {
    position: relative;
    width: 100%;
    aspect-ratio: 16 / 9;
    background-color: #000000;
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    overflow: hidden;

    &[hidden] {
        display: none !important;
    }

    img,
    video {
        @extend %imgCover;
    }

    &__controls {
        position: absolute;
        right: 1.4rem;
        bottom: 1.4rem;
        z-index: 3;
        @extend %flexItemCenter;
        gap: 0.8rem;
    }

    &__slide-btn {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        height: 3.6rem;
        padding: 0 1.4rem;
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        background-color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.68);
        border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.4);
        color: #ffffff;
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        backdrop-filter: blur(6px);

        &:hover {
            background-color: var(--primary-color);
            border-color: var(--primary-color);
        }
    }
}

// ==========================================================================
// 6. BỔ SUNG STYLE KINH DỊCH (3 XU 3D + 64 QUẺ) & TAROT (QUẠT 9/7 LÁ + LẬT 3D)
// ==========================================================================
.util-sr-only {
    @extend %srOnly;
}

.util-btn__svg-icon {
    width: 1.8rem;
    height: 1.8rem;
    filter: brightness(0) invert(1);
}

.util-divination-actions {
    @extend %flexCenter;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    gap: 1.2rem;
    margin-top: 0.6rem;
}

// --- 6.1. Sân khấu 3 đồng xu SVG 2 mặt (Đổi màu động theo 5 mệnh Ngũ Hành) ---
.util-coins-stage {
    @extend %flexCenter;
    gap: 2.2rem;
    padding: 1.8rem 0 1.2rem;
    perspective: 90rem;

    @include abs.screen(sm) {
        gap: 1.2rem;
    }
}

.util-coin {
    width: 8.6rem;
    height: 8.6rem;
    perspective: 60rem;

    @include abs.screen(sm) {
        width: 6.8rem;
        height: 6.8rem;
    }

    &__inner {
        position: relative;
        width: 100%;
        height: 100%;
        transform-style: preserve-3d;
        transition: transform 0.65s cubic-bezier(0.34, 1.32, 0.64, 1);
    }

    &[data-face="yin"] &__inner {
        transform: rotateY(180deg);
    }

    &.is-tossing &__inner {
        animation: coinToss3D 0.82s cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1) both;
    }

    &:nth-child(2).is-tossing &__inner {
        animation-delay: 0.08s;
    }

    &:nth-child(3).is-tossing &__inner {
        animation-delay: 0.16s;
    }

    &__face {
        position: absolute;
        inset: 0;
        @extend %flexCenter;
        backface-visibility: hidden;
        -webkit-backface-visibility: hidden;
        border-radius: 50%;
        background-color: var(--bg-surface);
        box-shadow: 0 0.8rem 2rem var(--shadow-color);

        img {
            width: 92%;
            height: 92%;
            object-fit: contain;
            filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
        }

        &--yin {
            transform: rotateY(180deg);
        }
    }
}

// --- 6.2. Khối kết quả Kinh Dịch & Xếp 6 hào từ dưới lên ---
.util-iching-bento {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: 23rem 1fr;
    align-items: start;
    gap: 2.4rem;
    padding: 2.2rem;
    background-color: var(--bg-surface);
    border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 2.4rem;

    &.is-sliding .util-iching-cards-stack {
        animation: tarotResultSlideIn 0.45s cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1);
    }

    @include abs.screen(md) {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
        justify-items: center;
        padding: 1.6rem;
    }
}

.util-iching-stage {
    @extend %flexColumnCenterAll;
    gap: 1.4rem;
    width: 100%;
    max-width: 23rem;
    padding: 2rem 1.8rem;
    background-color: var(--body-bg);
    border: 2px solid var(--primary-color);
    border-radius: 2.2rem;
    box-shadow: 0 0.8rem 2.2rem var(--shadow-color);
    cursor: pointer;
    @extend %transInteractive;

    &:hover,
    &:focus-visible {
        transform: translateY(-3px);
        box-shadow: 0 1.2rem 2.8rem var(--shadow-color);
    }

    &__svg-box {
        @extend %flexCenter;
        width: 10.4rem;
        height: 10.4rem;
        padding: 1.2rem;
        background-color: var(--primary-soft-bg, var(--bg-surface));
        border: 1.5px dashed var(--primary-color);
        border-radius: 1.8rem;

        img {
            width: 100%;
            height: 100%;
            object-fit: contain;
            filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
        }
    }

    &__lines {
        @extend %flexColumn;
        gap: 0.7rem;
        width: 100%;
        padding-top: 1rem;
        border-top: 1px dashed var(--border-color);

        .util-hex-line {
            height: 1rem;
            opacity: 0;
            animation: hexLineReveal 0.32s cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1) forwards;
        }
    }

    &__hint {
        font-size: 1.15rem;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--primary-color);
    }
}

.util-iching-cards-stack {
    @extend %flexColumn;
    gap: 1.2rem;
    width: 100%;
    min-width: 0;
}

.util-div-detail-card {
    @extend %flexColumn;
    gap: 0.55rem;
    padding: 1.4rem 1.8rem;
    background-color: var(--body-bg);
    border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 1.6rem;
    font-size: 1.4rem;
    line-height: 1.6;

    &__head {
        @extend %flexBetween;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        gap: 0.8rem;
    }

    &--highlight {
        background-color: var(--primary-soft-bg, var(--body-bg));
        border-color: var(--primary-color);
    }

    strong {
        color: var(--text-color);
        font-weight: 800;
    }
}

.util-iching-result__trigrams {
    font-size: 1.25rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    color: var(--text-muted);
}

// --- 6.3. Lưới tra cứu trọn bộ 64 Quẻ Kinh Dịch ---
.util-iching-catalog {
    @extend %flexColumn;
    gap: 1.6rem;
    padding-top: 2rem;
    border-top: 1px dashed var(--border-color);

    &__tools {
        @extend %flexItemCenter;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        gap: 1rem;
    }
}

.util-iching-filters {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(5, minmax(0, 1fr));

    @include abs.screen(md) {
        grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr));
    }

    @include abs.screen(sm) {
        grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
    }
}

.util-iching-catalog-grid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(4, minmax(0, 1fr));
    gap: 1.2rem;

    @include abs.screen(xl) {
        grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr));
    }

    @include abs.screen(md) {
        grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
    }

    @include abs.screen(sm) {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }
}

.util-hex-card {
    position: relative;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    justify-content: space-between;
    gap: 0.9rem;
    padding: 1.5rem 1.6rem;
    background-color: var(--bg-surface);
    border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 1.8rem;
    text-align: left;
    cursor: pointer;
    @extend %transInteractive;

    &:hover,
    &:focus-visible,
    &.is-current-highlight {
        border-color: var(--primary-color);
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        transform: translateY(-2px);
        box-shadow: 0 0.8rem 2rem var(--shadow-color);
    }

    &.is-current-highlight {
        border-width: 2px;
        background-color: var(--primary-soft-bg, var(--body-bg));
    }

    &__top {
        @extend %flexBetween;
        gap: 0.8rem;
    }

    &__svg {
        @extend %inlineFlexCenter;
        @extend %flexItemFixed;
        width: 4.8rem;
        height: 4.8rem;
        padding: 0.6rem;
        border-radius: 1.2rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);

        img {
            width: 100%;
            height: 100%;
            object-fit: contain;
            filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
        }
    }

    &__badge {
        padding: 0.3rem 0.9rem;
        border-radius: 99.9rem;
        background-color: var(--body-bg);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
        font-size: 1.1rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--primary-color);
        @extend %textNoWrap;
    }

    &__title {
        font-size: 1.55rem;
        font-weight: 800;
        color: var(--text-color);
    }

    &__sub {
        font-size: 1.2rem;
        font-weight: 600;
        color: var(--text-muted);
    }

    &__desc {
        font-size: 1.25rem;
        line-height: 1.5;
        color: var(--text-muted);
        @extend %textClamp2;
    }
}

// --- 6.4. Tarot Quạt Bài 9/7 Lá & Sân Khấu Lật 3D (Fix triệt để vỡ giao diện) ---
.util-tarot-fan {
    display: flex;
    align-items: flex-end;
    justify-content: center;
    padding: 3.2rem 1.6rem 2.2rem;
    min-height: 22rem;
    width: 100%;
    overflow: visible;

    @include abs.screen(md) {
        padding: 2.4rem 0.8rem 1.6rem;
        min-height: 17.5rem;
    }

    &[data-deck-state="shuffling"] {
        pointer-events: none;

        .util-tarot-back {
            animation: tarotShufflePulse 0.68s cubic-bezier(0.34, 1.56, 0.64, 1) both;
        }
    }
}

.util-tarot-back {
    --fan-angle: 0deg;
    --fan-y: 0rem;
    --shuffle-dx: 0px;

    position: relative;
    width: 10.4rem;
    aspect-ratio: 7 / 12;
    margin-left: -3.2rem;
    border-radius: 1.1rem;
    overflow: hidden;
    background: transparent;
    border: 2px solid transparent;
    box-shadow: 0 0.8rem 2rem rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.22);
    transform-origin: bottom center;
    transform: translateY(var(--fan-y)) rotate(var(--fan-angle));
    transition:
        transform 0.35s cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1),
        box-shadow 0.3s ease,
        border-color 0.25s ease;
    cursor: pointer;

    &:first-child {
        margin-left: 0;
    }

    img {
        width: 100%;
        height: 100%;
        object-fit: cover;
        display: block;
        pointer-events: none;
    }

    &[data-fan-idx="0"] {
        --fan-angle: -20deg;
        --fan-y: 2.6rem;
        --shuffle-dx: 120px;
        z-index: 1;
    }
    &[data-fan-idx="1"] {
        --fan-angle: -15deg;
        --fan-y: 1.5rem;
        --shuffle-dx: 90px;
        z-index: 2;
    }
    &[data-fan-idx="2"] {
        --fan-angle: -10deg;
        --fan-y: 0.7rem;
        --shuffle-dx: 60px;
        z-index: 3;
    }
    &[data-fan-idx="3"] {
        --fan-angle: -5deg;
        --fan-y: 0.2rem;
        --shuffle-dx: 30px;
        z-index: 4;
    }
    &[data-fan-idx="4"] {
        --fan-angle: 0deg;
        --fan-y: 0rem;
        --shuffle-dx: 0px;
        z-index: 5;
    }
    &[data-fan-idx="5"] {
        --fan-angle: 5deg;
        --fan-y: 0.2rem;
        --shuffle-dx: -30px;
        z-index: 4;
    }
    &[data-fan-idx="6"] {
        --fan-angle: 10deg;
        --fan-y: 0.7rem;
        --shuffle-dx: -60px;
        z-index: 3;
    }
    &[data-fan-idx="7"] {
        --fan-angle: 15deg;
        --fan-y: 1.5rem;
        --shuffle-dx: -90px;
        z-index: 2;
    }
    &[data-fan-idx="8"] {
        --fan-angle: 20deg;
        --fan-y: 2.6rem;
        --shuffle-dx: -120px;
        z-index: 1;
    }

    &:hover,
    &:focus-visible {
        z-index: 20;
        border-color: #facc15;
        transform: translateY(calc(var(--fan-y) - 2.2rem)) rotate(var(--fan-angle)) scale(1.08);
        box-shadow:
            0 1.4rem 3rem rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.35),
            0 0 1.6rem rgba(250, 204, 21, 0.55);
    }

    &.is-picked {
        z-index: 18;
        border-color: var(--primary-color);
        transform: translateY(calc(var(--fan-y) - 2.6rem)) rotate(var(--fan-angle)) scale(1.1);
        box-shadow:
            0 1.6rem 3.2rem var(--shadow-color),
            0 0 1.8rem rgba(250, 204, 21, 0.65);
    }

    @include abs.screen(md) {
        width: 8.4rem;
        margin-left: -3rem;

        &--edge {
            display: none;
        }

        &[data-fan-idx="1"] {
            margin-left: 0;
        }
    }

    @include abs.screen(sm) {
        width: 7rem;
        margin-left: -2.6rem;
    }
}

.util-tarot-bento {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: 24rem 1fr;
    align-items: start;
    gap: 2.6rem;
    padding: 2.2rem;
    background-color: var(--bg-surface);
    border: 1.5px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 2.4rem;

    &.is-sliding .util-iching-cards-stack {
        animation: tarotResultSlideIn 0.45s cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1);
    }

    @include abs.screen(md) {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
        justify-items: center;
        padding: 1.6rem;
    }
}

.util-tarot-stage {
    @extend %flexColumnCenterAll;
    gap: 1.2rem;
    width: 100%;
    max-width: 24rem;
    perspective: 120rem;
    cursor: pointer;

    &__inner {
        position: relative;
        width: 100%;
        aspect-ratio: 7 / 12;
        transform-style: preserve-3d;
        transition: transform 0.72s cubic-bezier(0.34, 1.32, 0.64, 1);
        border-radius: 1.8rem;
    }

    &[data-flipped="true"] &__inner {
        transform: rotateY(180deg);
    }

    &__face {
        position: absolute;
        inset: 0;
        backface-visibility: hidden;
        -webkit-backface-visibility: hidden;
        border-radius: 1.8rem;
        overflow: hidden;
        border: 2px solid var(--primary-color);
        box-shadow: 0 1.2rem 2.8rem var(--shadow-color);

        img {
            width: 100%;
            height: 100%;
            object-fit: cover;
            display: block;
            transition: transform 0.5s ease;
        }

        &--front {
            transform: rotateY(180deg);
        }
    }

    &[data-orientation="reversed"] &__face--front img {
        transform: rotate(180deg);
    }

    &__hint {
        @extend %flexColumnCenterAll;
        gap: 0.3rem;
        text-align: center;

        span {
            padding: 0.35rem 1.2rem;
            border-radius: 99.9rem;
            background-color: var(--body-bg);
            border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
            font-size: 1.2rem;
            font-weight: 800;
            color: var(--primary-color);
        }

        small {
            font-size: 1.15rem;
            font-weight: 600;
            color: var(--text-muted);
        }
    }
}

#tr-card-orientation[data-orientation="reversed"] {
    background-color: var(--color-danger, #dc2626);
}

.util-modal-tarot-img {
    width: 15.4rem !important;
    height: auto !important;
    aspect-ratio: 7 / 12;
    border-radius: 1.4rem;
    box-shadow: 0 1rem 2.4rem var(--shadow-color);

    &.is-reversed {
        transform: rotate(180deg);
    }
}

// Icon SVG 128x128 Cung Hoàng Đạo trong thẻ Hero & Lưới
.util-horoscope-hero__badge img {
    width: 5.8rem;
    height: 5.8rem;
    object-fit: contain;
    filter: brightness(0) invert(1);
}

.util-sign-btn img {
    width: 3.6rem;
    height: 3.6rem;
    object-fit: contain;
    filter: var(--zodiac-filter);
}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
    .util-coin__inner,
    .util-hex-line,
    .util-tarot-back,
    .util-tarot-stage__inner,
    .util-iching-bento.is-sliding .util-iching-cards-stack,
    .util-tarot-bento.is-sliding .util-iching-cards-stack {
        animation: none !important;
        transition-duration: 0.01ms !important;
        opacity: 1 !important;
    }
}
```

```scss
@include abs.generate-theme("fire");
@include abs.generate-theme("metal");
```
